// version: 3.1.1
self.addEventListener("install", () => {
  self.skipWaiting();
});

self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(self.clients.claim());
});

self.addEventListener("push", function (event) {
  let data = {};
  try {
    data = event.data ? event.data.json() : {};
  } catch (e) {
    data = { title: "Menu", body: event.data ? event.data.text() : "New update" };
  }

  const options = {
    body: data.time ? `${data.body || "New update"}\n⏰ ${data.time}` : (data.body || "New update from Menu"),
    icon: "/icon-192.png",
    badge: "/icon-192.png",
    data: { url: data.url || "/inbox" },
  };

  const promises = [];
  if ('setAppBadge' in self.navigator) {
    const countPromise = caches.open('badge-count').then(async (cache) => {
      const response = await cache.match('count');
      let currentCount = response ? parseInt(await response.text(), 10) : 0;
      currentCount += 1;
      await cache.put('count', new Response(currentCount.toString()));
      return self.navigator.setAppBadge(currentCount);
    }).catch((err) => console.log('Badge error:', err));
    
    promises.push(countPromise);
  }
  
  promises.push(self.registration.showNotification(data.title || "Menu", options));
  event.waitUntil(Promise.all(promises));
});

self.addEventListener("notificationclick", function (event) {
  event.notification.close();
  
  if ('clearAppBadge' in self.navigator) {
    self.navigator.clearAppBadge().catch((err) => console.log('Clear badge error:', err));
  }

  const targetUrl = (event.notification.data && event.notification.data.url) || "/inbox";
  const absoluteUrl = new URL(targetUrl, self.location.origin).href;

  event.waitUntil(
    clients.matchAll({ type: "window", includeUncontrolled: true }).then(function (clientList) {
      for (const client of clientList) {
        if (!client.url.startsWith(self.location.origin)) continue;
        client.focus();
        return clients.openWindow(absoluteUrl);
      }
      return clients.openWindow(absoluteUrl);
    })
  );
});

--- FILE: scripts/compress-existing-images.mjs ---
/**
 * Compress all recipe images in R2.
 *
 * For each recipe with an image_url pointing at our R2 public domain:
 *   1. Download the original
 *   2. If under 500KB, skip (already small)
 *   3. Otherwise, compress with sharp:
 *      - Resize if wider than 1600px
 *      - JPEG quality 82
 *      - Convert to WebP?  (no — keep original format to avoid 404 on old links)
 *   4. Re-upload to same key
 *   5. Log old vs new size
 *
 * Safe to re-run. Skips images already under threshold.
 *
 * Usage:
 *   node --env-file=.env.local scripts/compress-existing-images.mjs
 *   node --env-file=.env.local scripts/compress-existing-images.mjs --dry-run
 */

import { createClient } from "@libsql/client";
import { S3Client, PutObjectCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import sharp from "sharp";

const DRY_RUN = process.argv.includes("--dry-run");
const SIZE_THRESHOLD = 500 * 1024; // 500KB — skip if already smaller
const MAX_WIDTH = 1600;
const QUALITY = 82;

const tursoUrl = process.env.TURSO_DATABASE_URL;
const tursoToken = process.env.TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN;
if (!tursoUrl) { console.error("❌ TURSO_DATABASE_URL not set"); process.exit(1); }

const r2 = new S3Client({
  region: "auto",
  endpoint: `https://${process.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`,
  credentials: {
    accessKeyId: process.env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID,
    secretAccessKey: process.env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY,
  },
});

const db = createClient({ url: tursoUrl, authToken: tursoToken });

const publicBase = (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_R2_PUBLIC_URL || process.env.R2_PUBLIC_URL || "").replace(/\/$/, "");
if (!publicBase) { console.error("❌ NEXT_PUBLIC_R2_PUBLIC_URL not set"); process.exit(1); }

console.log("Public base:", publicBase);
console.log("Dry run:", DRY_RUN ? "YES (no writes)" : "no");
console.log("Skip if under:", Math.round(SIZE_THRESHOLD / 1024), "KB");
console.log("Max width:", MAX_WIDTH, "px");
console.log("Quality:", QUALITY);
console.log("");

const result = await db.execute("SELECT id, name, image_url FROM recipes WHERE image_url IS NOT NULL AND image_url != ''");
const rows = result.rows;
console.log(`Found ${rows.length} recipe(s) with images\n`);

let processed = 0, skipped = 0, failed = 0;
let bytesBefore = 0, bytesAfter = 0;

for (const row of rows) {
  const url = String(row.image_url);
  if (!url.startsWith(publicBase)) {
    console.log(`#${row.id} SKIP (not our R2): ${url.slice(0, 60)}`);
    skipped++;
    continue;
  }

  const key = url.slice(publicBase.length + 1);
  const label = `#${row.id} ${(row.name || "").toString().slice(0, 30)}`;

  try {
    console.log(`${label} → ${key}`);

    // Download
    const res = await fetch(url);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Download ${res.status}`);
    const original = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
    bytesBefore += original.length;

    if (original.length < SIZE_THRESHOLD) {
      console.log(`  SKIP (already ${Math.round(original.length / 1024)}KB)`);
      bytesAfter += original.length;
      skipped++;
      continue;
    }

    // Detect content type
    const contentType = res.headers.get("content-type") || "image/jpeg";
    const isJpeg = contentType.includes("jpeg") || key.endsWith(".jpg") || key.endsWith(".jpeg");
    const isPng = contentType.includes("png") || key.endsWith(".png");
    const isWebp = contentType.includes("webp") || key.endsWith(".webp");

    // Compress
    let pipeline = sharp(original).resize({
      width: MAX_WIDTH,
      withoutEnlargement: true,
    });

    let outputBuf, outputType;
    if (isJpeg) {
      outputBuf = await pipeline.jpeg({ quality: QUALITY, mozjpeg: true }).toBuffer();
      outputType = "image/jpeg";
    } else if (isPng) {
      outputBuf = await pipeline.png({ quality: QUALITY, compressionLevel: 9 }).toBuffer();
      outputType = "image/png";
    } else if (isWebp) {
      outputBuf = await pipeline.webp({ quality: QUALITY }).toBuffer();
      outputType = "image/webp";
    } else {
      console.log(`  SKIP (unknown format: ${contentType})`);
      bytesAfter += original.length;
      skipped++;
      continue;
    }

    const savings = original.length - outputBuf.length;
    const pct = Math.round((savings / original.length) * 100);
    console.log(`  ${Math.round(original.length / 1024)}KB → ${Math.round(outputBuf.length / 1024)}KB (−${pct}%)`);

    if (DRY_RUN) {
      console.log(`  [dry-run] would upload to ${key}`);
      bytesAfter += outputBuf.length;
      processed++;
      continue;
    }

    // Re-upload (same key, so URL doesn't change)
    await r2.send(new PutObjectCommand({
      Bucket: process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME,
      Key: key,
      Body: outputBuf,
      ContentType: outputType,
    }));

    console.log(`  ✅ uploaded`);
    bytesAfter += outputBuf.length;
    processed++;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`  ❌ ERROR: ${err.message}`);
    failed++;
  }
}

console.log("\n===== SUMMARY =====");
console.log(`Processed: ${processed}`);
console.log(`Skipped:   ${skipped}`);
console.log(`Failed:    ${failed}`);
console.log(`Before:    ${(bytesBefore / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`);
console.log(`After:     ${(bytesAfter / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`);
if (bytesBefore > 0) {
  const pct = Math.round(((bytesBefore - bytesAfter) / bytesBefore) * 100);
  console.log(`Saved:     ${((bytesBefore - bytesAfter) / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB (${pct}%)`);
}
--- END FILE: scripts/compress-existing-images.mjs ---

--- FILE: scripts/import-lebanese.js ---
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const target = process.argv[2] || "http://127.0.0.1:3000";
const filePath = path.join(__dirname, "lebanese-recipes.json");

if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
  console.error(`❌ File not found: ${filePath}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8"));
const list = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw.lebanese_recipes || raw.recipes || [raw];

if (!Array.isArray(list) || list.length === 0) {
  console.error("❌ No recipes found in the file");
  process.exit(1);
}

const ADMIN_USER = process.env.ADMIN_USERNAME;
const ADMIN_PASS = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD;

async function getSessionCookie() {
  if (!ADMIN_USER || !ADMIN_PASS) {
    console.warn("⚠️  ADMIN_USERNAME/ADMIN_PASSWORD not set — request will likely 401.");
    return "";
  }
  const res = await fetch(`${target}/api/auth/login`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ username: ADMIN_USER, password: ADMIN_PASS }),
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Login failed: ${res.status}`);
  const m = (res.headers.get("set-cookie") || "").match(/admin_session=[^;]+/);
  return m ? m[0] : "";
}

console.log(`Found ${list.length} recipes\n`);

function parseServings(s) {
  if (!s) return 4;
  const str = String(s);
  if (/not specified|غير محدد/i.test(str)) return 4;
  const m = str.match(/(\d+)/);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : 4;
}

function parseMinutes(s) {
  if (!s) return 0;
  const str = String(s).toLowerCase();
  if (/not specified|غير محدد/.test(str)) return 0;
  let total = 0;
  const h = str.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:hour|hr|h\b|ساعة)/);
  if (h) total += Math.round(parseFloat(h[1]) * 60);
  const m = str.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:min|minute|دقيقة|دقائق)/);
  if (m) total += parseInt(m[1], 10);
  return total || 0;
}

function splitAmountUnit(s) {
  if (!s) return { amount: "", unit: "", name: "" };
  if (typeof s !== "string") return { amount: "", unit: "", name: String(s) };
  const m = s.match(/^([½¼¾⅓⅔⅛⅜⅝⅞]|\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?(?:\s*[-–]\s*\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)?|\d+\/\d+)\s*([a-zA-Z\u0600-\u06FF]+)?\s*(.*)$/);
  if (!m) return { amount: "", unit: "", name: s };
  return { amount: (m[1] || "").trim(), unit: (m[2] || "").trim(), name: (m[3] || "").trim() };
}

function makeIngredient(en, ar) {
  if (typeof en === "object" && en !== null) {
    return {
      name: en.name || en.name_en || "",
      nameAr: en.nameAr || en.name_ar || "",
      amount: String(en.amount || ""),
      unit: en.unit || en.unit_en || "",
      unitAr: en.unitAr || en.unit_ar || "",
      preparation: en.preparation || en.notes || "",
      preparationAr: en.preparationAr || en.preparation_ar || "",
    };
  }
  const e = splitAmountUnit(en);
  const a = splitAmountUnit(ar);
  return {
    name: e.name || a.name || "",
    nameAr: a.name || "",
    amount: e.amount || a.amount || "",
    unit: e.unit || "",
    unitAr: a.unit || "",
    preparation: "",
    preparationAr: "",
  };
}

function flattenIngredients(ing, ingAr) {
  const out = [];

  if (Array.isArray(ing)) {
    const ar = Array.isArray(ingAr) ? ingAr : [];
    const len = Math.max(ing.length, ar.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      const e = ing[i];
      const a = ar[i];
      if (e == null && a == null) continue;
      if (typeof e === "object" && e !== null) {
        out.push(makeIngredient(e, a));
      } else {
        const es = (e || "").toString().trim();
        const as = (a || "").toString().trim();
        if (es || as) out.push(makeIngredient(es, as));
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  if (ing && typeof ing === "object") {
    for (const sec of Object.keys(ing).filter((k) => !k.endsWith("_arabic"))) {
      const en = ing[sec] || [];
      const ar = ing[sec + "_arabic"] || [];
      const len = Math.max(en.length, ar.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        const e = en[i];
        const a = ar[i];
        if (e == null && a == null) continue;
        if (typeof e === "object" && e !== null) {
          out.push(makeIngredient(e, a));
        } else {
          const es = (e || "").toString().trim();
          const as = (a || "").toString().trim();
          if (es || as) out.push(makeIngredient(es, as));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return out;
}

const recipes = list.map((r) => {
  const totalTime =
    Number(r.prepTime) || Number(r.prep_time_minutes) || parseMinutes(r.preparation_time) || 0;
  const cookTime =
    Number(r.cookTime) || Number(r.cook_time_minutes) || 0;
  const servings =
    Number(r.servings) || parseServings(r.servings);

  return {
    name: r.name || r.name_en || r.title_en || r.title || r.name_arabic || r.nameAr || "",
    nameAr: r.nameAr || r.name_arabic || r.title || "",
    description: r.description || r.description_en || "",
    descriptionAr: r.descriptionAr || r.description_arabic || (r.title ? r.description : "") || "",
    cuisine: r.cuisine || "Lebanese",
    cuisineAr: r.cuisineAr || "لبناني",
    category: r.category || "Main Dish",
    categoryAr: r.categoryAr || r.category_ar || "",
    servedWith: r.servedWith || r.served_with || "",
    servedWithAr: r.servedWithAr || r.served_with_ar || "",
    howToServe: r.howToServe || r.how_to_serve || "",
    howToServeAr: r.howToServeAr || r.how_to_serve_ar || "",
    prepTime: totalTime,
    cookTime,
    servings,
    dietaryTags: r.dietaryTags || r.dietary_tags || [],
    imageUrl: r.imageUrl || r.image_url || null,
    videoUrl: r.videoUrl || r.video_url || null,
    ingredients: flattenIngredients(r.ingredients, r.ingredients_arabic || r.ingredientsAr),
    instructions: (r.instructions || r.preparation || []).map((s) => {
      if (typeof s === "string") return { text: s, textAr: "" };
      return {
        text: s.text || s.text_en || s.step || "",
        textAr: s.textAr || s.text_ar || "",
      };
    }),
  };
});

(async () => {
  try {
    const cookie = await getSessionCookie();
    console.log(`Sending ${recipes.length} recipes to ${target}/api/recipes/bulk ...`);
    const res = await fetch(`${target}/api/recipes/bulk`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        ...(cookie ? { Cookie: cookie } : {}),
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(recipes),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.status >= 400) {
      console.error("❌ Failed:", res.status, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
      process.exit(1);
    }
    console.log("✅ Succeeded:", data);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("❌ Network:", err.message);
    process.exit(1);
  }
})();

--- FILE: scripts/migrate-meal-plan-v2.mjs ---
/**
 * Migrate meal_plan to support multiple recipes per (date, slot).
 *
 * Changes:
 *   - Add `position` column (1 or 2) — recipe slot per day/slot
 *   - Drop UNIQUE(date, slot) — now UNIQUE(date, slot, position)
 *
 * SQLite can't ALTER TABLE to drop constraints, so we:
 *   1. Create new table meal_plan_new
 *   2. Copy data with position=1 for existing rows
 *   3. Drop old table
 *   4. Rename new to meal_plan
 */
import { createClient } from "@libsql/client";

const url = process.env.TURSO_DATABASE_URL;
const authToken = process.env.TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN;
if (!url) { console.error("missing TURSO_DATABASE_URL"); process.exit(1); }

const db = createClient({ url, authToken });

console.log("Starting meal_plan v2 migration...");

// 1. Check existing columns
const cols = await db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(meal_plan)");
const colNames = cols.rows.map((r) => r.name);
console.log("Current columns:", colNames.join(", "));

const hasPosition = colNames.includes("position");
const hasUnique = (await db.execute("PRAGMA index_list(meal_plan)")).rows
  .some((r) => r.unique === 1);

console.log("Has position column:", hasPosition);
console.log("Has unique index:", hasUnique);

if (hasPosition && !hasUnique) {
  console.log("✅ Already migrated. Nothing to do.");
  process.exit(0);
}

// 2. Create new table
console.log("Creating meal_plan_new...");
await db.execute(`
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meal_plan_new (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    date TEXT NOT NULL,
    slot TEXT NOT NULL,
    position INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    recipe_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES recipes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE(date, slot, position)
  )
`);

// 3. Copy existing data
console.log("Copying data...");
const insertResult = await db.execute(`
  INSERT INTO meal_plan_new (date, slot, position, recipe_id, created_at)
  SELECT date, slot, 1, recipe_id, created_at FROM meal_plan
`);
console.log("Copied rows:", insertResult.rowsAffected ?? "?");

// 4. Drop old, rename new
console.log("Swapping tables...");
await db.execute("DROP TABLE meal_plan");
await db.execute("ALTER TABLE meal_plan_new RENAME TO meal_plan");

// 5. Verify
const newCols = await db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(meal_plan)");
console.log("\nNew columns:");
for (const r of newCols.rows) {
  console.log(`  ${r.name} (${r.type})${r.notnull ? " NOT NULL" : ""}`);
}

const newIdx = await db.execute("PRAGMA index_list(meal_plan)");
console.log("\nIndexes:");
for (const r of newIdx.rows) {
  console.log(`  ${r.name} — unique: ${r.unique}`);
}

const count = await db.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as n FROM meal_plan");
console.log("\nRows in new table:", count.rows[0].n);

console.log("\n✅ Migration complete");
--- END FILE: scripts/migrate-meal-plan-v2.mjs ---

--- FILE: scripts/migrate-meal-plan.mjs ---
/**
 * Create the meal_plan table in Turso.
 *
 * Columns:
 *   id, date (YYYY-MM-DD), slot (breakfast|lunch|dinner|snack),
 *   recipe_id (FK to recipes, cascade delete), created_at
 *
 * Unique constraint: one row per (date, slot).
 *
 * Safe to re-run — uses CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS.
 */
import { createClient } from "@libsql/client";

const url = process.env.TURSO_DATABASE_URL;
const authToken = process.env.TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN;
if (!url) {
  console.error("missing TURSO_DATABASE_URL");
  process.exit(1);
}

const db = createClient({ url, authToken });

console.log("Creating meal_plan table...");

await db.execute(`
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meal_plan (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    date TEXT NOT NULL,
    slot TEXT NOT NULL,
    recipe_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES recipes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE(date, slot)
  )
`);

console.log("✅ meal_plan table created");

// Verify columns
const cols = await db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(meal_plan)");
console.log("\nColumns:");
for (const row of cols.rows) {
  console.log(`  ${row.name} (${row.type})${row.notnull ? " NOT NULL" : ""}${row.pk ? " PK" : ""}`);
}

// Verify indexes
const idx = await db.execute("PRAGMA index_list(meal_plan)");
console.log("\nIndexes:");
for (const row of idx.rows) {
  console.log(`  ${row.name} — unique: ${row.unique}`);
}
--- END FILE: scripts/migrate-meal-plan.mjs ---

--- FILE: src/app/active-image/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { cuisineFlagUrl } from "@/lib/cuisine-flag";

type Recipe = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  cuisine: string | null;
  cuisineAr: string | null;
  imageUrl: string | null;
  image1Url: string | null;
  image2Url: string | null;
  useImage2: boolean | null;
};

export default function ActiveImagePage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const router = useRouter();
  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState<Recipe[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => {
        if (!data.admin) router.push("/login");
        else loadRecipes();
      });
  }, [router]);

  function loadRecipes() {
    fetch("/api/recipes", { cache: "no-store" })
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => {
        setRecipes(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }

  async function toggleImage(recipe: Recipe) {
    const newState = !recipe.useImage2;
    const newActiveUrl = newState ? recipe.image2Url : recipe.image1Url;

    await fetch(`/api/recipes/${recipe.id}`, {
      method: "PUT",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        name: recipe.name,
        nameAr: recipe.nameAr,
        isHidden: 0,
        useImage2: newState,
        image1Url: recipe.image1Url,
        image2Url: recipe.image2Url,
        imageUrl: newActiveUrl || recipe.imageUrl,
      }),
    });

    setRecipes(prev => prev.map(r => r.id === recipe.id
      ? { ...r, useImage2: newState, imageUrl: newActiveUrl || r.imageUrl }
      : r));
  }

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return recipes.filter(r => {
      if (!q) return true;
      const name = (isAr ? (r.nameAr || r.name) : r.name).toLowerCase();
      return name.includes(q);
    });
  }, [recipes, query, isAr]);

  if (loading) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>{isAr ? "جاري التحميل..." : "Loading..."}</div>;

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 900 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{isAr ? "الصور النشطة" : "Active Image"}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">
        {isAr ? "اختر الصورة النشطة لكل وصفة (1 أو 2)" : "Choose the active image for each recipe (1 or 2)"}
      </p>

      <div className="filter-bar" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
        <div className="search-wrap">
          <span className="search-icon">🔍</span>
          <input
            type="text"
            className="search-input"
            placeholder={isAr ? "ابحث عن وصفة..." : "Search recipes..."}
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="form-card" style={{ padding: 0, overflow: "hidden" }}>
        <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 14 }}>
          <thead style={{ background: "var(--bg)", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
            <tr>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600, width: 40 }}>#</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? "الاسم" : "Name"}</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600, width: 100 }}>Image 1</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600, width: 100 }}>Image 2</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600, width: 140 }}>Active</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {filtered.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={5} style={{ padding: 24, textAlign: "center", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                  {isAr ? "لا توجد وصفات." : "No recipes."}
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              filtered.map((r, i) => {
                const flagUrl = cuisineFlagUrl(r.cuisine) || cuisineFlagUrl(r.cuisineAr);
                const activeIsImage2 = !!r.image2Url && r.imageUrl === r.image2Url;

                return (
                  <tr key={r.id} style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                    <td style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontVariantNumeric: "tabular-nums" }}>{i + 1}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 12 }}>
                      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                        {flagUrl && <img src={flagUrl} alt="" style={{ width: 16, height: 12, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 2 }} />}
                        <span style={{ fontWeight: 500 }}>{isAr ? (r.nameAr || r.name) : r.name}</span>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 8, textAlign: "center" }}>
                      {r.image1Url ? (
                        <img src={r.image1Url} alt="" style={{ width: 60, height: 60, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6, border: activeIsImage2 ? "1px solid var(--line)" : "3px solid var(--olive)", opacity: activeIsImage2 ? 0.5 : 1 }} />
                      ) : (
                        <div style={{ width: 60, height: 60, background: "var(--surface-2)", borderRadius: 6, display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 11 }}>—</div>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 8, textAlign: "center" }}>
                      {r.image2Url ? (
                        <img src={r.image2Url} alt="" style={{ width: 60, height: 60, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6, border: activeIsImage2 ? "3px solid var(--accent)" : "1px solid var(--line)", opacity: activeIsImage2 ? 1 : 0.5 }} />
                      ) : (
                        <div style={{ width: 60, height: 60, background: "var(--surface-2)", borderRadius: 6, display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 11 }}>None</div>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center" }}>
                      <label style={{ display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, cursor: r.image2Url ? "pointer" : "not-allowed", opacity: r.image2Url ? 1 : 0.4 }}>
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          checked={activeIsImage2}
                          disabled={!r.image2Url}
                          onChange={() => toggleImage(r)}
                          style={{ width: 20, height: 20, cursor: r.image2Url ? "pointer" : "not-allowed", accentColor: "var(--accent)" }}
                        />
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: activeIsImage2 ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--muted)" }}>
                          {activeIsImage2 ? "Image 2" : "Image 1"}
                        </span>
                      </label>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/active-image/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createToken, COOKIE_NAME, COOKIE_MAX_AGE } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const { username, password } = await req.json();
  const expectedUser = process.env.ADMIN_USERNAME;
  const expectedPass = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD;

  if (!expectedUser || !expectedPass) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Admin credentials not configured" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }

  if (username !== expectedUser || password !== expectedPass) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid credentials" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  res.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, createToken(), {
    httpOnly: true,
    sameSite: "lax",
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    path: "/",
    maxAge: COOKIE_MAX_AGE,
  });
  return res;
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/auth/verify-phone/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

export const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";
const VIEWER_PHONE_MAX_AGE = 60 * 60 * 24 * 365; // 1 year

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const body = await req.json();
    const phoneNumber = (body.phoneNumber || "").trim();

    if (!phoneNumber) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Phone number required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Only allow phones that are actually registered
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(pushSubscriptions)
      .where(eq(pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, phoneNumber))
      .limit(1);

    if (rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "This phone is not registered. Enable notifications first." },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    res.cookies.set(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE, phoneNumber, {
      httpOnly: true,
      sameSite: "lax",
      secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
      path: "/",
      maxAge: VIEWER_PHONE_MAX_AGE,
    });
    return res;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Verify phone error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to verify" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/auth/verify-phone/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/auth/welcome/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ name: null, phone: null });

    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(pushSubscriptions)
      .where(eq(pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, phone))
      .limit(1);

    if (rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ name: null, phone: null });

    const name = (rows[0].name || "").trim();
    return NextResponse.json({ name: name || null, phone });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Welcome lookup error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ name: null, phone: null });
  }
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/chess/rooms/[code]/move/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { chessRooms } from "@/db/schema";

const PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

type Piece = [string, string] | null;
type Board = Piece[][];

export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ code: string }> }) {
  try {
    const { code } = await params;
    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ error: "no_phone" }, { status: 401 });

    const body = await req.json();
    const { newBoard, newTurn, winner } = body as { newBoard: Board; newTurn: "w" | "b"; winner?: string | null };

    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db.select().from(chessRooms).where(eq(chessRooms.code, code.toUpperCase())).limit(1);
    if (!rows[0]) return NextResponse.json({ error: "not_found" }, { status: 404 });
    const room = rows[0];

    const myColor =
      room.whitePhone === phone ? "w" :
      room.blackPhone === phone ? "b" : null;
    if (!myColor) return NextResponse.json({ error: "not_a_player" }, { status: 403 });
    if (room.turn !== myColor) return NextResponse.json({ error: "not_your_turn" }, { status: 400 });
    if (room.status !== "active") return NextResponse.json({ error: "room_not_active" }, { status: 400 });

    const [updated] = await db.update(chessRooms).set({
      board: JSON.stringify(newBoard),
      turn: newTurn,
      winner: winner ?? null,
      status: winner ? "finished" : "active",
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    }).where(eq(chessRooms.code, code.toUpperCase())).returning();

    return NextResponse.json({ room: updated });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: String(error) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/chess/rooms/[code]/move/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/chess/rooms/[code]/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { chessRooms } from "@/db/schema";

const PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ code: string }> }) {
  try {
    const { code } = await params;
    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db.select().from(chessRooms).where(eq(chessRooms.code, code.toUpperCase())).limit(1);
    if (!rows[0]) return NextResponse.json({ error: "not_found" }, { status: 404 });
    return NextResponse.json({ room: rows[0] });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: String(error) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(_req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ code: string }> }) {
  try {
    const { code } = await params;
    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ error: "no_phone" }, { status: 401 });

    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db.select().from(chessRooms).where(eq(chessRooms.code, code.toUpperCase())).limit(1);
    if (!rows[0]) return NextResponse.json({ error: "not_found" }, { status: 404 });

    const room = rows[0];
    if (room.whitePhone === phone || room.blackPhone === phone) {
      return NextResponse.json({ room, role: room.whitePhone === phone ? "white" : "black" });
    }
    if (room.blackPhone) return NextResponse.json({ error: "room_full" }, { status: 400 });

    const [updated] = await db.update(chessRooms)
      .set({ blackPhone: phone, status: "active", updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() })
      .where(eq(chessRooms.code, code.toUpperCase()))
      .returning();

    return NextResponse.json({ room: updated, role: "black" });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: String(error) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/chess/rooms/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { eq, or, desc } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { chessRooms } from "@/db/schema";

const PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

const START_BOARD = JSON.stringify([
  [["r","b"],["n","b"],["b","b"],["q","b"],["k","b"],["b","b"],["n","b"],["r","b"]],
  Array(8).fill(["p","b"]),
  [null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null],
  [null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null],
  [null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null],
  [null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null],
  Array(8).fill(["p","w"]),
  [["r","w"],["n","w"],["b","w"],["q","w"],["k","w"],["b","w"],["n","w"],["r","w"]],
]);

function makeCode() {
  const chars = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789";
  let code = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) code += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
  return code;
}

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ rooms: [] });

    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(chessRooms)
      .where(or(eq(chessRooms.whitePhone, phone), eq(chessRooms.blackPhone, phone)))
      .orderBy(desc(chessRooms.createdAt))
      .limit(20);

    return NextResponse.json({ rooms: rows });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ rooms: [], error: String(error) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST() {
  try {
    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ error: "no_phone" }, { status: 401 });

    const db = getDb();
    let code = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      code = makeCode();
      const existing = await db.select().from(chessRooms).where(eq(chessRooms.code, code)).limit(1);
      if (existing.length === 0) break;
      code = "";
    }
    if (!code) return NextResponse.json({ error: "code_generation_failed" }, { status: 500 });

    const [inserted] = await db.insert(chessRooms).values({
      code,
      whitePhone: phone,
      board: START_BOARD,
      turn: "w",
      status: "waiting",
    }).returning();

    return NextResponse.json({ room: inserted });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: String(error) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/chess/rooms/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/game-stats/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { eq, and } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { gameStats } from "@/db/schema";

// ⚠️ IMPORTANT: Ensure this matches the cookie used in user-settings/route.ts
const PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone"; 

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const game = searchParams.get("game");
    if (!game) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Missing game" }, { status: 400 });

    const store = await cookies();
    // Try both common cookie names just in case
    const phone = (store.get(PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || store.get("phone")?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ stats: {} });

    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db.select().from(gameStats).where(and(eq(gameStats.phone, phone), eq(gameStats.game, game))).limit(1);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ stats: rows[0] ? JSON.parse(rows[0].stats) : {} });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("GET game-stats error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ stats: {}, error: "Database error" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { game, stats } = body;
    if (!game || typeof stats !== "object") return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid payload" }, { status: 400 });

    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || store.get("phone")?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ saved: false, error: "No phone cookie" });

    const db = getDb();
    const existing = await db.select().from(gameStats).where(and(eq(gameStats.phone, phone), eq(gameStats.game, game))).limit(1);

    const payload = JSON.stringify(stats);

    if (existing[0]) {
      await db.update(gameStats).set({ stats: payload, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() }).where(eq(gameStats.id, existing[0].id));
    } else {
      await db.insert(gameStats).values({ phone, game, stats: payload });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ saved: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("POST game-stats error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ saved: false, error: String(error) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/game-stats/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/inbox/admin/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { notifications, notificationReads, pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET() {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const all = await db.select().from(notifications).orderBy(desc(notifications.createdAt));
    const reads = await db.select().from(notificationReads);
    const subs = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions);

    const nameByPhone = new Map<string, string | null>();
    for (const s of subs) {
      if (!nameByPhone.has(s.phoneNumber)) nameByPhone.set(s.phoneNumber, s.name);
    }

    const readersByNotif = new Map<number, { name: string | null; phone: string }[]>();
    for (const r of reads) {
      if (!readersByNotif.has(r.notificationId)) readersByNotif.set(r.notificationId, []);
      readersByNotif.get(r.notificationId)!.push({
        name: nameByPhone.get(r.phone) || null,
        phone: r.phone,
      });
    }

    const result = all.map((n) => ({
      id: n.id,
      title: n.title,
      createdAt: n.createdAt,
      readers: readersByNotif.get(n.id) || [],
    }));

    return NextResponse.json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Admin inbox error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/inbox/admin/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/leaderboard/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { gameStats } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const game = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("game") || "catch";

    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db.select().from(gameStats).where(eq(gameStats.game, game));

    const parsedRows = rows.map((r) => ({
      phone: r.phone,
      stats: JSON.parse(r.stats || "{}"),
    })).filter((r) => Object.keys(r.stats).length > 0);

    parsedRows.sort((a, b) => {
      if (game === "memory") {
        const aTime = a.stats.bestTime === 0 ? 99999 : a.stats.bestTime;
        const bTime = b.stats.bestTime === 0 ? 99999 : b.stats.bestTime;
        return aTime - bTime;
      }
      if (game === "catch" || game === "snake") {
        return (b.stats.bestCatched || b.stats.bestScore || 0) - (a.stats.bestCatched || a.stats.bestScore || 0);
      }
      return (b.stats.wins || b.stats.guessed || b.stats.solved || 0) - (a.stats.wins || a.stats.guessed || a.stats.solved || 0);
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ leaderboard: parsedRows.slice(0, 50) });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Leaderboard error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load leaderboard" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/leaderboard/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/meal-plan/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { mealPlan, recipes } from "@/db/schema";
import { and, eq, gte, lte } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

const VALID_SLOTS = ["breakfast", "lunch", "dinner", "snack", "dessert"];
const MAX_POSITIONS = 2;

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const month = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("month");
    const dateParam = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("date");

    const allRecipes = await db.select({
      id: recipes.id,
      name: recipes.name,
      nameAr: recipes.nameAr,
      imageUrl: recipes.imageUrl,
      category: recipes.category,
      categoryAr: recipes.categoryAr,
      description: recipes.description,
      descriptionAr: recipes.descriptionAr,
      prepTime: recipes.prepTime,
      cookTime: recipes.cookTime,
      servings: recipes.servings,
    }).from(recipes);

    if (dateParam) {
      if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateParam)) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "date=YYYY-MM-DD required" }, { status: 400 });
      }
      const dayRows = await db.select().from(mealPlan).where(eq(mealPlan.date, dateParam));
      if (dayRows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ entries: [], recipes: allRecipes });
      
      const dayRecipeMap = new Map<number, typeof allRecipes[0]>();
      for (const r of allRecipes) dayRecipeMap.set(r.id, r);

      const dayResult = dayRows
        .map((row) => {
          const recipe = dayRecipeMap.get(row.recipeId);
          if (!recipe) return null;
          return { id: row.id, date: row.date, slot: row.slot, position: row.position, recipe };
        })
        .filter((x) => x !== null)
        .sort((a, b) => {
          if (a.slot !== b.slot) return a.slot.localeCompare(b.slot);
          return a.position - b.position;
        });
      return NextResponse.json({ entries: dayResult, recipes: allRecipes });
    }

    if (!month || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(month)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "month=YYYY-MM or date=YYYY-MM-DD required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const from = month + "-01";
    const [y, m] = month.split("-").map(Number);
    const lastDay = new Date(y, m, 0).getDate();
    const to = month + "-" + String(lastDay).padStart(2, "0");

    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(mealPlan)
      .where(and(gte(mealPlan.date, from), lte(mealPlan.date, to)));

    if (rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ entries: [], recipes: allRecipes });

    const recipeMap = new Map<number, typeof allRecipes[0]>();
    for (const r of allRecipes) recipeMap.set(r.id, r);

    const result = rows
      .map((row) => {
        const recipe = recipeMap.get(row.recipeId);
        if (!recipe) return null;
        return { id: row.id, date: row.date, slot: row.slot, position: row.position, recipe };
      })
      .filter((x) => x !== null)
      .sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.date !== b.date) return a.date.localeCompare(b.date);
        if (a.slot !== b.slot) return a.slot.localeCompare(b.slot);
        return a.position - b.position;
      });

    return NextResponse.json({ entries: result, recipes: allRecipes });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("GET meal-plan error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { date, slot, position, recipeId } = body || {};

    if (!date || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "date=YYYY-MM-DD required" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (!slot || !VALID_SLOTS.includes(slot)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid slot" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const pos = Number(position);
    if (!pos || pos < 1 || pos > MAX_POSITIONS) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "position must be 1 or 2" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const rid = Number(recipeId);
    if (!rid || Number.isNaN(rid)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "recipeId required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, rid)).limit(1);
    if (!recipe) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Recipe not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const existing = await db
      .select()
      .from(mealPlan)
      .where(and(eq(mealPlan.date, date), eq(mealPlan.slot, slot), eq(mealPlan.position, pos)))
      .limit(1);

    if (existing.length > 0) {
      await db.update(mealPlan).set({ recipeId: rid }).where(eq(mealPlan.id, existing[0].id));
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, id: existing[0].id, updated: true });
    } else {
      const [inserted] = await db.insert(mealPlan).values({
        date, slot, position: pos, recipeId: rid,
      }).returning();
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, id: inserted.id, updated: false });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("POST meal-plan error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const idParam = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("id");
    if (!idParam) return NextResponse.json({ error: "id required" }, { status: 400 });
    
    const id = Number(idParam);
    if (Number.isNaN(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    await db.delete(mealPlan).where(eq(mealPlan.id, id));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("DELETE meal-plan error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to delete" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { notificationReads } from "@/db/schema";

const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

export async function POST(
  _req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const notificationId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(notificationId)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, reason: "no_phone" });
    }

    const db = getDb();
    await db
      .insert(notificationReads)
      .values({ notificationId, phone })
      .onConflictDoNothing();

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("POST read error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { notifications } from "@/db/schema";
import { inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    const { ids } = await req.json();
    if (!Array.isArray(ids) || ids.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "No IDs provided" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const db = getDb();
    await db.delete(notifications).where(inArray(notifications.id, ids));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to delete" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/notifications/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { notifications, polls, pushSubscriptions, notificationReads } from "@/db/schema";
import { desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const admin = await isAdmin();

    const store = await cookies();
    const cookiePhone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    const queryPhone = (req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("phone") || "").trim();
    const phone = cookiePhone || queryPhone;

    let viewerName: string | null = null;
    if (phone) {
      const sub = await db
        .select()
        .from(pushSubscriptions)
        .where(eq(pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, phone))
        .limit(1);
      viewerName = sub[0]?.name || null;
    }

    function personalizeText(t: string | null | undefined): string {
      if (!t) return "";
      let replacement = viewerName ? viewerName.trim() : (admin ? "Admin" : "");
      if (replacement) return t.replace(/\{name\}/g, replacement);
      return t
        .replace(/\{name\}/g, "")
        .replace(/\s+([,.!?;:])/g, "$1")
        .replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ")
        .trim();
    }

    const all = await db
      .select()
      .from(notifications)
      .orderBy(desc(notifications.createdAt));

    const allPolls = await db.select().from(polls);
    const pollByNotifId = new Map<number, { id: number; question: string; isOpen: number }>();
    for (const p of allPolls) {
      pollByNotifId.set(p.notificationId, { id: p.id, question: p.question, isOpen: p.isOpen });
    }

    const allReads = await db.select().from(notificationReads);
    const readsByNotif = new Map<number, Set<string>>();
    for (const r of allReads) {
      if (!readsByNotif.has(r.notificationId)) readsByNotif.set(r.notificationId, new Set());
      readsByNotif.get(r.notificationId)!.add(r.phone);
    }

    function enrich(n: typeof notifications.$inferSelect) {
      const poll = pollByNotifId.get(n.id);
      const storedPollUrl = (n as unknown as { pollUrl?: string | null }).pollUrl;
      const readers = readsByNotif.get(n.id);
      const isRead = phone && readers ? readers.has(phone) : false;

      return {
        ...n,
        title: personalizeText(n.title),
        body: personalizeText(n.body),
        pollId: poll?.id ?? null,
        pollQuestion: poll ? personalizeText(poll.question) : null,
        pollIsOpen: poll?.isOpen ?? null,
        pollUrl: storedPollUrl ?? (poll ? `/poll/${poll.id}` : null),
        location: (n as unknown as { location?: string | null }).location || null,
        time: (n as unknown as { time?: string | null }).time || null,
        isRead,
      };
    }

    if (admin) return NextResponse.json(all.map(enrich));

    if (!phone) {
      return NextResponse.json(all.filter((n) => n.targetPhone === "all").map(enrich));
    }

    const filtered = all.filter((n) => {
      if (!n.targetPhone) return false;
      if (n.targetPhone === "all") return true;
      const targets = n.targetPhone.split(",").map((s) => s.trim());
      return targets.includes(phone);
    });

    return NextResponse.json(filtered.map(enrich));
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/notifications/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/notifications/unread-count/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { notifications } from "@/db/schema";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const admin = await isAdmin();

    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();

    const sinceParam = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("since");
    const since = sinceParam || "1970-01-01T00:00:00Z";

    const all = await db.select().from(notifications);

    const filtered = all.filter((n) => {
      if (!n.createdAt) return false;
      if (new Date(n.createdAt) <= new Date(since)) return false;
      if (admin) return true;
      if (!phone) return n.targetPhone === "all";
      if (!n.targetPhone) return false;
      if (n.targetPhone === "all") return true;
      const targets = n.targetPhone.split(",").map((s) => s.trim());
      return targets.includes(phone);
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ count: filtered.length });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ count: 0 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/notifications/unread-count/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/polls/[id]/responses/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { polls, pollResponses, notifications, pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

function isValidPhone(p: string | null | undefined): p is string {
  if (!p) return false;
  const t = p.trim();
  if (!t) return false;
  if (t.toLowerCase() === "unknown") return false;
  return true;
}

export async function GET(
  _req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const pollId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(pollId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    const db = getDb();

    const [poll] = await db.select().from(polls).where(eq(polls.id, pollId)).limit(1);
    if (!poll) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Poll not found" }, { status: 404 });

    const [notification] = await db
      .select()
      .from(notifications)
      .where(eq(notifications.id, poll.notificationId))
      .limit(1);

    const responses = await db.select().from(pollResponses).where(eq(pollResponses.pollId, pollId));

    const allSubs = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions);
    const subByPhone = new Map<string, { name: string | null; phoneNumber: string }>();
    for (const s of allSubs) {
      if (!isValidPhone(s.phoneNumber)) continue;
      if (!subByPhone.has(s.phoneNumber)) {
        subByPhone.set(s.phoneNumber, { name: s.name, phoneNumber: s.phoneNumber });
      }
    }

    const target = notification?.targetPhone || "all";
    let invitees: string[];
    if (!target || target === "all") {
      invitees = Array.from(subByPhone.keys());
    } else {
      invitees = target.split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(isValidPhone);
    }

    const answerByPhone = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const r of responses) {
      if (isValidPhone(r.phone)) answerByPhone.set(r.phone, r.answer);
    }

    const yes = responses
      .filter((r) => r.answer === "yes" && isValidPhone(r.phone))
      .map((r) => {
        const sub = subByPhone.get(r.phone);
        return {
          phone: r.phone,
          name: r.name || sub?.name || null,
          respondedAt: r.respondedAt,
        };
      });

    const no = responses
      .filter((r) => r.answer === "no" && isValidPhone(r.phone))
      .map((r) => {
        const sub = subByPhone.get(r.phone);
        return {
          phone: r.phone,
          name: r.name || sub?.name || null,
          respondedAt: r.respondedAt,
        };
      });

    const pending = invitees
      .filter((phone) => !answerByPhone.has(phone))
      .map((phone) => {
        const sub = subByPhone.get(phone);
        return {
          phone,
          name: sub?.name || null,
        };
      });

    return NextResponse.json({
      pollId: poll.id,
      question: poll.question,
      isOpen: !!poll.isOpen,
      yes,
      no,
      pending,
      counts: {
        yes: yes.length,
        no: no.length,
        pending: pending.length,
        total: invitees.length,
      },
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("GET responses error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load responses" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/polls/[id]/responses/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/polls/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import webpush from "web-push";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { polls, pollResponses, notifications, pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";
const THROTTLE_WINDOW_MS = 60000;

const pendingBatches = new Map<number, { timer: NodeJS.Timeout; yes: number; no: number }>();

function initWebPush() {
  const subject = process.env.VAPID_SUBJECT;
  const publicKey = process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY;
  const privateKey = process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
  if (!subject || !publicKey || !privateKey) throw new Error("Missing VAPID env vars");
  webpush.setVapidDetails(subject, publicKey, privateKey);
}

function parseTargetPhones(targetPhone: string | null): string[] {
  if (!targetPhone || targetPhone === "all") return [];
  return targetPhone.split(",").map((s: string) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

async function sendAdminPush(db: ReturnType<typeof getDb>, question: string, yesCount: number, noCount: number, pollId: number) {
  const adminPhone = (process.env.ADMIN_PHONE || "").trim();
  console.log("[admin-notify] ADMIN_PHONE=", JSON.stringify(adminPhone));
  if (!adminPhone) {
    console.log("[admin-notify] SKIPPED - ADMIN_PHONE not set");
    return;
  }
  try {
    initWebPush();
    const subs = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions).where(eq(pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, adminPhone));
    console.log("[admin-notify] found", subs.length, "subs for", adminPhone);
    if (subs.length === 0) return;

    const parts = [];
    if (yesCount > 0) parts.push(yesCount + " Yes");
    if (noCount > 0) parts.push(noCount + " No");
    const countStr = parts.join(", ") || "0";

    const payload = JSON.stringify({
      title: "Vote on \"" + question + "\"",
      body: countStr + " - tap to see who",
      url: "/poll/" + pollId,
    });

    await Promise.allSettled(subs.map((s: { endpoint: string; p256dh: string; auth: string }) => webpush.sendNotification({ endpoint: s.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: s.p256dh, auth: s.auth } }, payload)));
    console.log("[admin-notify] push sent to", subs.length, "device(s)");
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[admin-notify] Admin push failed:", err);
  }
}

function scheduleAdminNotify(db: ReturnType<typeof getDb>, pollId: number, question: string, answer: "yes" | "no") {
  const existing = pendingBatches.get(pollId);
  if (existing) {
    if (answer === "yes") existing.yes += 1;
    else existing.no += 1;
    console.log("[admin-notify] batched vote - yes:", existing.yes, "no:", existing.no);
    return;
  }
  const batch = {
    timer: setTimeout(async () => {
      const counts = pendingBatches.get(pollId);
      pendingBatches.delete(pollId);
      if (!counts) return;
      await sendAdminPush(db, question, counts.yes, counts.no, pollId);
    }, THROTTLE_WINDOW_MS),
    yes: answer === "yes" ? 1 : 0,
    no: answer === "no" ? 1 : 0,
  };
  pendingBatches.set(pollId, batch);
  sendAdminPush(db, question, batch.yes, batch.no, pollId).catch((err) => console.error("[admin-notify] first vote push error:", err));
}

export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const pollId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(pollId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    const db = getDb();
    const [poll] = await db.select().from(polls).where(eq(polls.id, pollId)).limit(1);
    if (!poll) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Poll not found" }, { status: 404 });

    const [notification] = await db.select().from(notifications).where(eq(notifications.id, poll.notificationId)).limit(1);
    const responses = await db.select().from(pollResponses).where(eq(pollResponses.pollId, pollId));

    const yes = responses.filter((r) => r.answer === "yes");
    const no = responses.filter((r) => r.answer === "no");

    const store = await cookies();
    
    // Look up the user's name to replace {name}
    const viewerPhone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    let viewerName: string | null = null;
    if (viewerPhone) {
      const [sub] = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions).where(eq(pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, viewerPhone)).limit(1);
      if (sub && sub.name) viewerName = sub.name.trim();
    }

    function personalize(t: string | null | undefined): string {
      if (!t) return "";
      if (!viewerName) return t.replace(/\{name\}/g, "").replace(/\s+([,.!?;:])/g, "$1").replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ").trim();
      return t.replace(/\{name\}/g, viewerName);
    }
  
    const myResponse = viewerPhone ? responses.find((r) => r.phone === viewerPhone) : null;

    const targetPhones = parseTargetPhones(notification?.targetPhone || null);
    const isTargetedToMe = !targetPhones.length || (viewerPhone && targetPhones.includes(viewerPhone));

    const rawUrl = notification?.url || null;
    const recipeUrl = rawUrl && rawUrl !== "/inbox" && !rawUrl.startsWith("/poll/") ? rawUrl : null;

    return NextResponse.json({
      id: poll.id,
      question: personalize(poll.question),
      isOpen: !!poll.isOpen,
      title: personalize(notification?.title || poll.question),
      body: personalize(notification?.body || ""),
      targetPhone: notification?.targetPhone || "all",
      recipeUrl,
      yesCount: yes.length,
      noCount: no.length,
      myAnswer: myResponse ? myResponse.answer : null,
      canVote: !!viewerPhone && isTargetedToMe && !!poll.isOpen,
      hasPhoneCookie: !!viewerPhone,
      isTargetedToMe,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("GET poll error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load poll" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const pollId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(pollId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    const db = getDb();
    const body = await req.json();
    const answer = (body.answer || "").toString().toLowerCase();
    if (answer !== "yes" && answer !== "no") return NextResponse.json({ error: "Answer must be yes or no" }, { status: 400 });

    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Verify your phone first" }, { status: 401 });

    const [poll] = await db.select().from(polls).where(eq(polls.id, pollId)).limit(1);
    if (!poll) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Poll not found" }, { status: 404 });
    if (!poll.isOpen) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Poll is closed" }, { status: 400 });

    const [notification] = await db.select().from(notifications).where(eq(notifications.id, poll.notificationId)).limit(1);
    const targetPhones = parseTargetPhones(notification?.targetPhone || null);
    if (targetPhones.length > 0 && !targetPhones.includes(phone)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "You are not invited to this poll" }, { status: 403 });

    const existing = await db.select().from(pollResponses).where(eq(pollResponses.pollId, pollId)).then((rows) => rows.find((r) => r.phone === phone));
    const prevAnswer = existing?.answer;

    if (existing) {
      await db.update(pollResponses).set({ answer, respondedAt: new Date().toISOString() }).where(eq(pollResponses.id, existing.id));
    } else {
      await db.insert(pollResponses).values({ pollId, phone, answer, name: null, respondedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    }

    console.log("[admin-notify] vote:", prevAnswer, "->", answer, "poll:", pollId);
    if (prevAnswer !== answer) {
      scheduleAdminNotify(db, pollId, poll.question, answer);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, answer });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("POST vote error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save vote" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function PUT(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const pollId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(pollId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    const db = getDb();
    const body = await req.json();
    const isOpen = body.isOpen ? 1 : 0;

    const [poll] = await db.select().from(polls).where(eq(polls.id, pollId)).limit(1);
    if (!poll) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Poll not found" }, { status: 404 });

    await db.update(polls).set({ isOpen }).where(eq(polls.id, pollId));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, isOpen: !!isOpen });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("PUT poll error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to update poll" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/polls/[id]/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/push/presets/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushPresets } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET() {
  const db = getDb();
  const presets = await db.select().from(pushPresets);
  return NextResponse.json(presets);
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const db = getDb();
  const body = await req.json();
  const [inserted] = await db.insert(pushPresets).values({
    name: body.name,
    title: body.title,
    body: body.body,
    pollQuestion: body.pollQuestion || null,
  }).returning();
  return NextResponse.json(inserted);
}

export async function PUT(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const db = getDb();
  const body = await req.json();
  await db.update(pushPresets).set({
    name: body.name,
    title: body.title,
    body: body.body,
    pollQuestion: body.pollQuestion || null,
  }).where(eq(pushPresets.id, body.id));
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}

export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const db = getDb();
  const id = Number(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("id"));
  await db.delete(pushPresets).where(eq(pushPresets.id, id));
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/push/presets/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/push/send/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import webpush from "web-push";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushSubscriptions, notifications, polls, recipes, settings } from "@/db/schema";
import { inArray, eq, and } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

function initWebPush() {
  const subject = process.env.VAPID_SUBJECT;
  const publicKey = process.env.VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY;
  const privateKey = process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
  if (!subject || !publicKey || !privateKey) throw new Error("Missing VAPID env vars");
  webpush.setVapidDetails(subject, publicKey, privateKey);
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    initWebPush();
    const db = getDb();
    const appSettings = await db.select().from(settings);
    const s = Object.fromEntries(appSettings.map((r) => [r.key, r.value]));
    const defaultTitle = s.app_name || "Menu";
    const body = await req.json();

    const title = body.title || defaultTitle;
    const text = body.body || "New recipe added";
    const url = body.url || "/inbox";
    const targetPhone = body.targetPhone || "all";
    const location = (body.location || "").trim() || null;
    const time = (body.time || "").trim() || null;
    const pollQuestion = (body.poll?.question || "").trim();
    const personalize = !!body.personalize || title.includes("{name}") || text.includes("{name}");

    let previewImage: string | null = null;
    let previewTitle: string | null = null;

    const recipeMatch = url.match(/\/recipes\/(\d+)/);
    if (recipeMatch) {
      const recipeId = Number(recipeMatch[1]);
      try {
        const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, recipeId)).limit(1);
        if (recipe) {
          previewImage = recipe.imageUrl || null;
          previewTitle = recipe.name || null;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Recipe lookup failed:", err);
      }
    }

    if (!previewImage && url.startsWith("http")) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(url);
        const html = await res.text();
        const imgMatch = html.match(/<meta\s+property="og:image"\s+content="([^"]+)"/i);
        if (imgMatch) previewImage = imgMatch[1];
        const titleMatch = html.match(/<meta\s+property="og:title"\s+content="([^"]+)"/i);
        if (titleMatch) previewTitle = titleMatch[1].replace(/&#x27;/g, "'").replace(/&quot;/g, '"');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch link preview:", err);
      }
    }

    const [inserted] = await db.insert(notifications).values({
      title,
      body: text,
      url,
      previewImage,
      previewTitle,
      targetPhone,
      pollUrl: null,
      location,
      time,
    }).returning();

    let pollId: number | null = null;
    let pollUrl: string | null = null;
    let pushUrl = url;

    if (pollQuestion) {
      const [poll] = await db.insert(polls).values({
        notificationId: inserted.id,
        question: pollQuestion,
        isOpen: 1,
      }).returning();
      pollId = poll.id;
      pollUrl = "/poll/" + poll.id;
      pushUrl = pollUrl;
      await db.update(notifications).set({ pollUrl }).where(eq(notifications.id, inserted.id));
    }

    const payloadObj: Record<string, unknown> = {
      title: pollQuestion || title,
      body: pollQuestion ? "Tap to RSVP: " + text : text,
      url: pushUrl,
    };
    if (pollId) payloadObj.pollId = pollId;
    if (location) payloadObj.location = location;
    if (time) payloadObj.time = time;

    let subs;
    if (targetPhone === "all") {
      // Send to everyone EXCEPT excluded
      subs = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions).where(eq(pushSubscriptions.excluded, 0));
    } else {
      // Specific phones or groups — IGNORE the excluded flag
      const phones = targetPhone.split(",").map((p: string) => p.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      if (phones.length === 0) {
        subs = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions).where(eq(pushSubscriptions.excluded, 0));
      } else {
        subs = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions).where(inArray(pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, phones));
      }
    }

    if (subs.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "No subscriptions found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    function personalizeText(t: string, name: string | null): string {
      if (!personalize) return t;
      const replacement = (name || "").trim();
      if (replacement) return t.replace(/\{name\}/g, replacement);
      return t
        .replace(/\{name\}/g, "")
        .replace(/\s+([,.!?;:])/g, "$1")
        .replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ")
        .trim();
    }

    const results = await Promise.allSettled(
      subs.map((s) => {
        const perPayload = JSON.stringify({
          ...payloadObj,
          title: personalizeText(payloadObj.title as string, s.name || null),
          body: personalizeText(payloadObj.body as string, s.name || null),
        });
        return webpush.sendNotification(
          { endpoint: s.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: s.p256dh, auth: s.auth } },
          perPayload
        );
      })
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      sent: results.filter((r) => r.status === "fulfilled").length,
      total: subs.length,
      pollId,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Send error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/push/send/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/push/subscribe/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushSubscriptions, visitors } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, and, ne } from "drizzle-orm";

export const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";
const VIEWER_PHONE_MAX_AGE = 60 * 60 * 24 * 365;

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const body = await req.json();

    const sub = body.subscription || body;
    const phoneNumber = (body.phoneNumber || "").trim();
    const visitorId = (body.visitorId || "").trim();

    if (!sub?.endpoint || !sub?.keys?.p256dh || !sub?.keys?.auth) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid subscription data" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (!phoneNumber) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Phone number required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const existingForPhone = await db
      .select({ endpoint: pushSubscriptions.endpoint })
      .from(pushSubscriptions)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, phoneNumber),
          ne(pushSubscriptions.endpoint, sub.endpoint)
        )
      )
      .limit(1);

    if (existingForPhone.length > 0) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "This phone number is already registered on another device." },
        { status: 409 }
      );
    }

    await db
      .insert(pushSubscriptions)
      .values({
        endpoint: sub.endpoint,
        p256dh: sub.keys.p256dh,
        auth: sub.keys.auth,
        phoneNumber,
      })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: pushSubscriptions.endpoint,
        set: {
          p256dh: sub.keys.p256dh,
          auth: sub.keys.auth,
          phoneNumber,
        },
      });

    if (visitorId) {
      await db.update(visitors).set({ phone: phoneNumber }).where(eq(visitors.id, visitorId));
    }

    const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    res.cookies.set(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE, phoneNumber, {
      httpOnly: true,
      sameSite: "lax",
      secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
      path: "/",
      maxAge: VIEWER_PHONE_MAX_AGE,
    });
    return res;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Subscribe error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save subscription" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/push/subscribe/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/push/unsubscribe/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const { endpoint } = await req.json();

    if (!endpoint) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "endpoint required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await db
      .delete(pushSubscriptions)
      .where(eq(pushSubscriptions.endpoint, endpoint));

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Unsubscribe error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { mealPlan } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, desc } from "drizzle-orm";

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const recipeId = Number(id);
    
    if (Number.isNaN(recipeId)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const db = getDb();
    
    // Fetch all meal plan entries for this recipe, newest first
    const history = await db
      .select({
        id: mealPlan.id,
        date: mealPlan.date,
        slot: mealPlan.slot,
      })
      .from(mealPlan)
      .where(eq(mealPlan.recipeId, recipeId))
      .orderBy(desc(mealPlan.date));

    return NextResponse.json(history);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("GET recipe history error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load history" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
