--- FILE: src/app/api/recipes/[id]/reviews/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipeReviews, pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, and, desc } from "drizzle-orm";

const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

export async function GET(
  _req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const recipeId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(recipeId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    const db = getDb();

    const reviewRows = await db
      .select({
        id: recipeReviews.id,
        rating: recipeReviews.rating,
        comment: recipeReviews.comment,
        phone: recipeReviews.phone,
        updatedAt: recipeReviews.updatedAt,
      })
      .from(recipeReviews)
      .where(eq(recipeReviews.recipeId, recipeId))
      .orderBy(desc(recipeReviews.updatedAt));

    const subs = await db
      .select({ phoneNumber: pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, name: pushSubscriptions.name })
      .from(pushSubscriptions);

    const nameByPhone = new Map<string, string | null>();
    for (const s of subs) {
      if (!s.phoneNumber) continue;
      if (!nameByPhone.has(s.phoneNumber)) {
        nameByPhone.set(s.phoneNumber, s.name);
      } else if (!nameByPhone.get(s.phoneNumber) && s.name) {
        nameByPhone.set(s.phoneNumber, s.name);
      }
    }

    const store = await cookies();
    const viewerPhone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();

    let mine = null;
    if (viewerPhone) {
      const myReview = reviewRows.find((r) => r.phone === viewerPhone);
      if (myReview) mine = { rating: myReview.rating, comment: myReview.comment };
    }

    const total = reviewRows.length;
    const average =
      total > 0 ? reviewRows.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.rating, 0) / total : 0;

    const publicReviews = reviewRows.map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      rating: r.rating,
      comment: r.comment,
      updatedAt: r.updatedAt,
      name: nameByPhone.get(r.phone) || null,
    }));

    return NextResponse.json({ average, count: total, mine, reviews: publicReviews });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("GET reviews error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load reviews" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const recipeId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(recipeId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Verify your phone to rate." }, { status: 401 });

    const body = await req.json();
    const rating = Number(body.rating);
    const comment = (body.comment || "").trim() || null;

    if (!rating || rating < 1 || rating > 5) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Rating must be 1-5" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const db = getDb();
    const existing = await db
      .select()
      .from(recipeReviews)
      .where(and(eq(recipeReviews.recipeId, recipeId), eq(recipeReviews.phone, phone)))
      .limit(1);

    if (existing.length > 0) {
      await db
        .update(recipeReviews)
        .set({ rating, comment, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() })
        .where(eq(recipeReviews.id, existing[0].id));
    } else {
      await db.insert(recipeReviews).values({ recipeId, phone, rating, comment });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("POST review error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save review" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  try {
    const reviewId = Number(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("reviewId"));
    const db = getDb();
    await db.delete(recipeReviews).where(eq(recipeReviews.id, reviewId));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("DELETE review error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/recipes/[id]/reviews/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/recipes/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

type Payload = {
  name?: string;
  nameAr?: string | null;
  description?: string | null;
  descriptionAr?: string | null;
  cuisine?: string | null;
  cuisineAr?: string | null;
  category?: string | null;
  categoryAr?: string | null;
  servedWith?: string | null;
  servedWithAr?: string | null;
  howToServe?: string | null;
  howToServeAr?: string | null;
  prepTime?: number;
  cookTime?: number;
  servings?: number;
  dietaryTags?: string[];
  imageUrl?: string | null;
  image1Url?: string | null;
  image2Url?: string | null;
  useImage2?: boolean | null;
  videoUrl?: string | null;
  isHidden?: number;
  ingredients?: {
    name: string;
    nameAr?: string | null;
    amount?: string | null;
    unit?: string | null;
    unitAr?: string | null;
    preparation?: string | null;
    preparationAr?: string | null;
  }[];
  instructions?: { text: string; textAr?: string | null }[];
};

export async function PUT(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const recipeId = Number(id);
    if (Number.isNaN(recipeId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid id" }, { status: 400 });

    const body: Payload = await req.json();
    if (!body.name?.trim() && !body.nameAr?.trim()) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Recipe name is required" }, { status: 400 });

    // Fetch existing recipe to use as fallback for any missing fields
    const existingRows = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, recipeId)).limit(1);
    if (!existingRows[0]) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Recipe not found" }, { status: 404 });
    const existing = existingRows[0];

    // Swap active image if useImage2 is explicitly provided
    if (body.useImage2 === true && body.image2Url) { body.imageUrl = body.image2Url; }
    if (body.useImage2 === false && body.image1Url) { body.imageUrl = body.image1Url; }

    // Update only the fields that were provided in the request. Keep existing values otherwise.
    await db.update(recipes).set({
      name: body.name !== undefined ? (body.name?.trim() || body.nameAr?.trim() || "") : existing.name,
      nameAr: body.nameAr !== undefined ? (body.nameAr?.trim() || null) : existing.nameAr,
      description: body.description !== undefined ? (body.description?.trim() || null) : existing.description,
      descriptionAr: body.descriptionAr !== undefined ? (body.descriptionAr?.trim() || null) : existing.descriptionAr,
      cuisine: body.cuisine !== undefined ? (body.cuisine?.trim() || null) : existing.cuisine,
      cuisineAr: body.cuisineAr !== undefined ? (body.cuisineAr?.trim() || null) : existing.cuisineAr,
      category: body.category !== undefined ? (body.category?.trim() || null) : existing.category,
      categoryAr: body.categoryAr !== undefined ? (body.categoryAr?.trim() || null) : existing.categoryAr,
      servedWith: body.servedWith !== undefined ? (body.servedWith?.trim() || null) : existing.servedWith,
      servedWithAr: body.servedWithAr !== undefined ? (body.servedWithAr?.trim() || null) : existing.servedWithAr,
      howToServe: body.howToServe !== undefined ? (body.howToServe?.trim() || null) : existing.howToServe,
      howToServeAr: body.howToServeAr !== undefined ? (body.howToServeAr?.trim() || null) : existing.howToServeAr,
      prepTime: body.prepTime !== undefined ? (Number(body.prepTime) || 0) : existing.prepTime,
      cookTime: body.cookTime !== undefined ? (Number(body.cookTime) || 0) : existing.cookTime,
      servings: body.servings !== undefined ? (Number(body.servings) || 1) : existing.servings,
      dietaryTags: body.dietaryTags !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(body.dietaryTags ?? []) : existing.dietaryTags,
      imageUrl: body.imageUrl !== undefined ? body.imageUrl : existing.imageUrl,
      image1Url: body.image1Url !== undefined ? body.image1Url : existing.image1Url,
      image2Url: body.image2Url !== undefined ? body.image2Url : existing.image2Url,
      useImage2: body.useImage2 !== undefined ? body.useImage2 : existing.useImage2,
      videoUrl: body.videoUrl !== undefined ? (body.videoUrl ?? null) : existing.videoUrl,
      isHidden: body.isHidden !== undefined ? body.isHidden : existing.isHidden,
    }).where(eq(recipes.id, recipeId));

    // Only update ingredients if they were provided
    if (body.ingredients !== undefined) {
      await db.delete(ingredients).where(eq(ingredients.recipeId, recipeId));
      if (body.ingredients?.length) {
        await db.insert(ingredients).values(
          body.ingredients
            .filter((i) => i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim())
            .map((i) => ({
              recipeId,
              name: i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim() || "",
              nameAr: i.nameAr?.trim() || null,
              amount: i.amount?.trim() || null,
              unit: i.unit?.trim() || null,
              unitAr: i.unitAr?.trim() || null,
              preparation: i.preparation?.trim() || null,
              preparationAr: i.preparationAr?.trim() || null,
            }))
        );
      }
    }

    // Only update instructions if they were provided
    if (body.instructions !== undefined) {
      await db.delete(instructions).where(eq(instructions.recipeId, recipeId));
      if (body.instructions?.length) {
        await db.insert(instructions).values(
          body.instructions
            .filter((s) => s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim())
            .map((s, idx) => ({
              recipeId,
              stepNumber: idx + 1,
              text: s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim() || "",
              textAr: s.textAr?.trim() || null,
            }))
        );
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Update recipe error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to update: " + String(err) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/recipes/[id]/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/recipes/bulk/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

type BulkRecipe = {
  id?: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr?: string;
  description?: string;
  descriptionAr?: string;
  cuisine?: string;
  cuisineAr?: string;
  category?: string;
  categoryAr?: string;
  servedWith?: string;
  servedWithAr?: string;
  howToServe?: string;
  howToServeAr?: string;
  prepTime?: number;
  cookTime?: number;
  servings?: number;
  dietaryTags?: string[] | string;
  imageUrl?: string | null;
  videoUrl?: string | null;
  image1Url?: string | null;
  image2Url?: string | null;
  useImage2?: boolean | number | null;
  ingredients?: {
    name: string;
    nameAr?: string;
    amount?: string;
    unit?: string;
    unitAr?: string;
    preparation?: string;
    preparationAr?: string;
  }[];
  instructions?: { text: string; textAr?: string }[];
};

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const list: BulkRecipe[] = Array.isArray(body) ? body : body.recipes;

    if (!Array.isArray(list) || list.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Expected non-empty array" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const insertedIds: number[] = [];
    const updatedIds: number[] = [];

    for (const r of list) {
      if (!r.name?.trim() && !r.nameAr?.trim()) continue;

      let tags: string[] = [];
      if (Array.isArray(r.dietaryTags)) {
        tags = r.dietaryTags;
      } else if (typeof r.dietaryTags === "string") {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(r.dietaryTags);
          tags = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
        } catch {
          tags = r.dietaryTags.split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        }
      }
      tags = Array.from(new Set(tags));

      const values = {
        name: r.name?.trim() || r.nameAr?.trim() || "",
        nameAr: r.nameAr?.trim() || null,
        description: r.description?.trim() || null,
        descriptionAr: r.descriptionAr?.trim() || null,
        cuisine: r.cuisine?.trim() || null,
        cuisineAr: r.cuisineAr?.trim() || null,
        category: r.category?.trim() || null,
        categoryAr: r.categoryAr?.trim() || null,
        servedWith: r.servedWith?.trim() || null,
        servedWithAr: r.servedWithAr?.trim() || null,
        howToServe: r.howToServe?.trim() || null,
        howToServeAr: r.howToServeAr?.trim() || null,
        prepTime: Number(r.prepTime) || 0,
        cookTime: Number(r.cookTime) || 0,
        servings: Number(r.servings) || 4,
        dietaryTags: JSON.stringify(tags),
        
        
        
        
        
      };

      let recipeId: number;

      if (r.id) {
        const existing = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, r.id)).limit(1);
        if (existing.length > 0) {
          await db.update(recipes).set(values).where(eq(recipes.id, r.id));
          recipeId = r.id;
          updatedIds.push(r.id);
          await db.delete(ingredients).where(eq(ingredients.recipeId, r.id));
          await db.delete(instructions).where(eq(instructions.recipeId, r.id));
        } else {
          const [inserted] = await db.insert(recipes).values({ ...values, id: r.id }).returning();
          recipeId = inserted.id;
          insertedIds.push(recipeId);
        }
      } else {
        const [inserted] = await db.insert(recipes).values(values).returning();
        recipeId = inserted.id;
        insertedIds.push(recipeId);
      }

      if (r.ingredients?.length) {
        await db.insert(ingredients).values(
          r.ingredients
            .filter((i) => i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim())
            .map((i) => ({
              recipeId,
              name: i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim() || "",
              nameAr: i.nameAr?.trim() || null,
              amount: i.amount?.trim() || null,
              unit: i.unit?.trim() || null,
              unitAr: i.unitAr?.trim() || null,
              preparation: i.preparation?.trim() || null,
              preparationAr: i.preparationAr?.trim() || null,
            }))
        );
      }

      if (r.instructions?.length) {
        await db.insert(instructions).values(
          r.instructions
            .filter((s) => s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim())
            .map((s, idx) => ({
              recipeId,
              stepNumber: idx + 1,
              text: s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim() || "",
              textAr: s.textAr?.trim() || null,
            }))
        );
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      inserted: insertedIds.length,
      updated: updatedIds.length,
      insertedIds,
      updatedIds,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Bulk import error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Bulk import failed: " + String(err) },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/recipes/bulk/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/recipes/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import { desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const admin = await isAdmin();
    
    // If a "light" query is requested, only fetch necessary fields (for calendar, pickers, etc.)
    const isLight = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("light") === "true";

    if (isLight) {
      const list = admin
        ? await db.select({
            id: recipes.id,
            name: recipes.name,
            nameAr: recipes.nameAr,
            imageUrl: recipes.imageUrl,
            image1Url: recipes.image1Url,
            image2Url: recipes.image2Url,
            useImage2: recipes.useImage2,
            category: recipes.category,
            categoryAr: recipes.categoryAr,
          }).from(recipes).orderBy(desc(recipes.createdAt))
        : await db.select({
            id: recipes.id,
            name: recipes.name,
            nameAr: recipes.nameAr,
            imageUrl: recipes.imageUrl,
            image1Url: recipes.image1Url,
            image2Url: recipes.image2Url,
            useImage2: recipes.useImage2,
            category: recipes.category,
            categoryAr: recipes.categoryAr,
          }).from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.isHidden, 0)).orderBy(desc(recipes.createdAt));

      return NextResponse.json(list);
    }

    // Full query (used by the HomeClient if not using server props, but usually we do)
    const all = admin
      ? await db.select().from(recipes).orderBy(desc(recipes.createdAt))
      : await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.isHidden, 0)).orderBy(desc(recipes.createdAt));

    const allIngredients = await db.select().from(ingredients);
    
    const recipesWithIngredients = all.map((r) => ({
      ...r,
      ingredients: allIngredients.filter((i) => i.recipeId === r.id),
    }));
    
    return NextResponse.json(recipesWithIngredients);
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

type Payload = {
  name: string; nameAr: string;
  description: string; descriptionAr: string;
  cuisine: string; cuisineAr: string;
  category: string; categoryAr: string;
  servedWith: string; servedWithAr: string;
  howToServe: string; howToServeAr: string;
  prepTime: number; cookTime: number; servings: number;
  dietaryTags: string[]; imageUrl: string | null; videoUrl: string | null;
  ingredients: { name: string; nameAr: string; amount: string; unit: string; unitAr: string; preparation: string; preparationAr: string }[];
  instructions: { text: string; textAr: string }[];
};

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const body: Payload = await req.json();
    if (!body.name?.trim() && !body.nameAr?.trim()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Recipe name is required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const [recipe] = await db.insert(recipes).values({
      name: body.name?.trim() || body.nameAr?.trim() || "",
      nameAr: body.nameAr?.trim() || null,
      description: body.description?.trim() || null,
      descriptionAr: body.descriptionAr?.trim() || null,
      cuisine: body.cuisine?.trim() || null,
      cuisineAr: body.cuisineAr?.trim() || null,
      category: body.category?.trim() || null,
      categoryAr: body.categoryAr?.trim() || null,
      servedWith: body.servedWith?.trim() || null,
      servedWithAr: body.servedWithAr?.trim() || null,
      howToServe: body.howToServe?.trim() || null,
      howToServeAr: body.howToServeAr?.trim() || null,
      prepTime: Number(body.prepTime) || 0,
      cookTime: Number(body.cookTime) || 0,
      servings: Number(body.servings) || 1,
      dietaryTags: JSON.stringify(body.dietaryTags ?? []),
      imageUrl: body.imageUrl ?? null,
      videoUrl: body.videoUrl ?? null,
    }).returning();

    if (body.ingredients?.length) {
      await db.insert(ingredients).values(
        body.ingredients.filter((i) => i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim()).map((i) => ({
          recipeId: recipe.id,
          name: i.name?.trim() || i.nameAr?.trim() || "",
          nameAr: i.nameAr?.trim() || null,
          amount: i.amount?.trim() || null,
          unit: i.unit?.trim() || null,
          unitAr: i.unitAr?.trim() || null,
          preparation: i.preparation?.trim() || null,
          preparationAr: i.preparationAr?.trim() || null,
        }))
      );
    }

    if (body.instructions?.length) {
      await db.insert(instructions).values(
        body.instructions.filter((s) => s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim()).map((s, idx) => ({
          recipeId: recipe.id, stepNumber: idx + 1,
          text: s.text?.trim() || s.textAr?.trim() || "",
          textAr: s.textAr?.trim() || null,
        }))
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ id: recipe.id });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Save recipe error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save recipe" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/recipients/groups/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipientGroups, recipientGroupMembers } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET() {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const db = getDb();
  const groups = await db.select().from(recipientGroups);
  const members = await db.select().from(recipientGroupMembers);
  
  const result = groups.map(g => ({
    ...g,
    phones: members.filter(m => m.groupId === g.id).map(m => m.phone)
  }));
  return NextResponse.json(result);
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const db = getDb();
  const { name, phones } = await req.json();
  if (!name) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Name required" }, { status: 400 });

  const [group] = await db.insert(recipientGroups).values({ name }).returning();
  
  if (phones && phones.length > 0) {
    await db.insert(recipientGroupMembers).values(
      phones.map((phone: string) => ({ groupId: group.id, phone }))
    );
  }
  return NextResponse.json(group);
}

export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const db = getDb();
  const id = Number(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("id"));
  await db.delete(recipientGroups).where(eq(recipientGroups.id, id));
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/recipients/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

export async function GET() {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const db = getDb();
  const subs = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions);
  return NextResponse.json(subs);
}

export async function PUT(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const db = getDb();
  const body = await req.json();
  
  if (!body.id) return NextResponse.json({ error: "ID required" }, { status: 400 });
  
  await db.update(pushSubscriptions)
    .set({ name: body.name, phoneNumber: body.phoneNumber, excluded: body.excluded ? 1 : 0 })
    .where(eq(pushSubscriptions.id, body.id));
    
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}

export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const db = getDb();
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const id = searchParams.get("id");
  
  if (!id) return NextResponse.json({ error: "ID required" }, { status: 400 });
  
  await db.delete(pushSubscriptions).where(eq(pushSubscriptions.id, Number(id)));
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/replies/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { polls, pollResponses, notifications, pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { desc } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

function isValidPhone(p: string | null | undefined): p is string {
  if (!p) return false;
  const t = p.trim();
  if (!t) return false;
  if (t.toLowerCase() === "unknown") return false;
  return true;
}

export async function GET() {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    const db = getDb();

    const allPolls = await db.select().from(polls).orderBy(desc(polls.id));
    const allResponses = await db.select().from(pollResponses);
    const allNotifications = await db.select().from(notifications);
    const allSubs = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions);

    const notifById = new Map<number, typeof notifications.$inferSelect>();
    for (const n of allNotifications) notifById.set(n.id, n);

    const subByPhone = new Map<string, { name: string | null }>();
    for (const s of allSubs) {
      if (!isValidPhone(s.phoneNumber)) continue;
      if (!subByPhone.has(s.phoneNumber)) subByPhone.set(s.phoneNumber, { name: s.name });
    }

    const replies = [];

    for (const poll of allPolls) {
      const notif = notifById.get(poll.notificationId);
      if (!notif) continue;

      const responses = allResponses.filter((r) => r.pollId === poll.id && isValidPhone(r.phone));

      const target = notif.targetPhone || "all";
      let invitees: string[];
      if (!target || target === "all") {
        invitees = Array.from(subByPhone.keys());
      } else {
        invitees = target.split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(isValidPhone);
      }

      const answerByPhone = new Map<string, string>();
      for (const r of responses) answerByPhone.set(r.phone, r.answer);

      const yesVoters = responses
        .filter((r) => r.answer === "yes")
        .sort((a, b) => (a.respondedAt || "").localeCompare(b.respondedAt || ""))
        .map((r) => ({
          phone: r.phone,
          name: r.name || subByPhone.get(r.phone)?.name || null,
          respondedAt: r.respondedAt,
        }));

      const noVoters = responses
        .filter((r) => r.answer === "no")
        .sort((a, b) => (a.respondedAt || "").localeCompare(b.respondedAt || ""))
        .map((r) => ({
          phone: r.phone,
          name: r.name || subByPhone.get(r.phone)?.name || null,
          respondedAt: r.respondedAt,
        }));

      const pendingVoters = invitees
        .filter((phone) => !answerByPhone.has(phone))
        .map((phone) => ({
          phone,
          name: subByPhone.get(phone)?.name || null,
        }));

      // Combined list of all voters (yes + no) sorted by time ascending
      const allVoters = [
        ...yesVoters.map((v) => ({ ...v, answer: "yes" as const })),
        ...noVoters.map((v) => ({ ...v, answer: "no" as const })),
      ].sort((a, b) => (a.respondedAt || "").localeCompare(b.respondedAt || ""));

      const lastVoteAt = responses.length
        ? responses.map((r) => r.respondedAt || "").sort().reverse()[0]
        : null;

      replies.push({
        pollId: poll.id,
        question: poll.question,
        isOpen: !!poll.isOpen,
        createdAt: poll.createdAt,
        lastVoteAt,
        targetPhone: target,
        inviteeCount: invitees.length,
        counts: {
          yes: yesVoters.length,
          no: noVoters.length,
          pending: pendingVoters.length,
          total: invitees.length,
        },
        voters: allVoters,
        pending: pendingVoters,
      });
    }

    // Sort: polls with recent votes first, unanswered last
    replies.sort((a, b) => {
      const aTime = a.lastVoteAt || "";
      const bTime = b.lastVoteAt || "";
      if (aTime && !bTime) return -1;
      if (!aTime && bTime) return 1;
      if (aTime && bTime) return bTime.localeCompare(aTime);
      return b.pollId - a.pollId;
    });

    return NextResponse.json(replies);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("GET /api/replies error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load replies" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/replies/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/reviews/day/[date]/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { dayReviews, pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, and, desc } from "drizzle-orm";

const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

export async function GET(
  _req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ date: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { date } = await params;
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid date format" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const db = getDb();

    const reviewRows = await db
      .select({
        id: dayReviews.id,
        rating: dayReviews.rating,
        comment: dayReviews.comment,
        phone: dayReviews.phone,
        updatedAt: dayReviews.updatedAt,
      })
      .from(dayReviews)
      .where(eq(dayReviews.date, date))
      .orderBy(desc(dayReviews.updatedAt));

    const subs = await db
      .select({ phoneNumber: pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, name: pushSubscriptions.name })
      .from(pushSubscriptions);

    const nameByPhone = new Map<string, string | null>();
    for (const s of subs) {
      if (!s.phoneNumber) continue;
      if (!nameByPhone.has(s.phoneNumber)) {
        nameByPhone.set(s.phoneNumber, s.name);
      } else if (!nameByPhone.get(s.phoneNumber) && s.name) {
        nameByPhone.set(s.phoneNumber, s.name);
      }
    }

    const store = await cookies();
    const viewerPhone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();

    let mine = null;
    if (viewerPhone) {
      const myReview = reviewRows.find((r) => r.phone === viewerPhone);
      if (myReview) mine = { rating: myReview.rating, comment: myReview.comment };
    }

    const total = reviewRows.length;
    const average =
      total > 0 ? reviewRows.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.rating, 0) / total : 0;

    const publicReviews = reviewRows.map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      rating: r.rating,
      comment: r.comment,
      updatedAt: r.updatedAt,
      name: nameByPhone.get(r.phone) || null,
    }));

    return NextResponse.json({ average, count: total, mine, reviews: publicReviews });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("GET day reviews error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load reviews" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ date: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { date } = await params;
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid date format" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Verify your phone to rate." }, { status: 401 });

    const body = await req.json();
    const rating = Number(body.rating);
    const comment = (body.comment || "").trim() || null;

    if (!rating || rating < 1 || rating > 5) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Rating must be 1-5" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const db = getDb();
    const existing = await db
      .select()
      .from(dayReviews)
      .where(and(eq(dayReviews.date, date), eq(dayReviews.phone, phone)))
      .limit(1);

    if (existing.length > 0) {
      await db
        .update(dayReviews)
        .set({ rating, comment, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() })
        .where(eq(dayReviews.id, existing[0].id));
    } else {
      await db.insert(dayReviews).values({ date, phone, rating, comment });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("POST day review error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save review" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ date: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const reviewId = Number(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("reviewId"));
    const db = getDb();
    await db.delete(dayReviews).where(eq(dayReviews.id, reviewId));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("DELETE day review error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/reviews/day/[date]/route.ts ---

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { settings } from "@/db/schema";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db.select().from(settings);
    const data = Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.key, r.value]));
    return NextResponse.json(data);
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to load" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const body = await req.json(); // Expecting { today_color: "#ff0000", ... }

    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(body)) {
      await db.insert(settings)
        .values({ key, value: String(value) })
        .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: settings.key, set: { value: String(value) } });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to save" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/share/whatsapp/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, asc } from "drizzle-orm";

const BASE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://food-menu-app-xet2.onrender.com";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const id = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("id");
  const lang = (req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("lang") || "en") as "en" | "ar";

  if (!id) return NextResponse.json({ error: "id required" }, { status: 400 });

  const numericId = Number(id);
  if (Number.isNaN(numericId)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid id" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const db = getDb();
  const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, numericId)).limit(1);
  if (!recipe) return NextResponse.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });

  const ings = await db
    .select()
    .from(ingredients)
    .where(eq(ingredients.recipeId, numericId))
    .orderBy(asc(ingredients.id));

  const steps = await db
    .select()
    .from(instructions)
    .where(eq(instructions.recipeId, numericId))
    .orderBy(asc(instructions.stepNumber));

  const totalTime = recipe.prepTime + recipe.cookTime;
  const link = `${BASE_URL}/recipes/${recipe.id}`;

  const isAr = lang === "ar";

  const name = isAr ? (recipe.nameAr || recipe.name) : recipe.name;
  const desc = isAr ? (recipe.descriptionAr || recipe.description || "") : (recipe.description || "");
  const cuisine = isAr ? (recipe.cuisineAr || recipe.cuisine || "") : (recipe.cuisine || "");
  const servedWith = isAr ? (recipe.servedWithAr || recipe.servedWith || "") : (recipe.servedWith || "");
  const howToServe = isAr ? (recipe.howToServeAr || recipe.howToServe || "") : (recipe.howToServe || "");

  const topIngs = ings
    .slice(0, 4)
    .map((i) => (isAr ? (i.nameAr || i.name) : i.name))
    .filter(Boolean);

  const firstStep = steps[0]
    ? (isAr ? (steps[0].textAr || steps[0].text) : steps[0].text)
    : "";

  const message = isAr
    ? buildArabic({ name, desc, cuisine, totalTime, servings: recipe.servings, topIngs, firstStep, servedWith, howToServe, link })
    : buildEnglish({ name, desc, cuisine, totalTime, servings: recipe.servings, topIngs, firstStep, servedWith, howToServe, link });

  const encoded = encodeURIComponent(message);
  const url = `https://wa.me/?text=${encoded}`;

  return NextResponse.json({ message, url });
}

function buildEnglish(d: {
  name: string; desc: string; cuisine: string; totalTime: number;
  servings: number; topIngs: string[]; firstStep: string;
  servedWith: string; howToServe: string; link: string;
}) {
  const ingLine = d.topIngs.length ? d.topIngs.join(" • ") : "";
  return `╭─────────────────────╮
   ✦  NEW RECIPE  ✦
╰─────────────────────╯

🍽️ *${d.name}*
_${[d.cuisine, `${d.totalTime} min`, `serves ${d.servings}`].filter(Boolean).join(" • ")}_

─────────────────────

${d.desc ? `_${d.desc}_\n\n` : ""}🛒 *You'll need*
${ingLine}

${d.firstStep ? `👨‍🍳 *How it starts*\n${d.firstStep}\n\n` : ""}─────────────────────
${d.servedWith ? `\n🍽️ *Serve with*\n${d.servedWith}\n` : ""}${d.howToServe ? `\n📌 *Tip*\n${d.howToServe}\n` : ""}
─────────────────────

👉 Full recipe with photos & steps:
${d.link}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
🕌 _Menu — your cookbook_`;
}

function buildArabic(d: {
  name: string; desc: string; cuisine: string; totalTime: number;
  servings: number; topIngs: string[]; firstStep: string;
  servedWith: string; howToServe: string; link: string;
}) {
  const ingLine = d.topIngs.length ? d.topIngs.join(" • ") : "";
  return `╭─────────────────────╮
   ✦  وصفة جديدة  ✦
╰─────────────────────╯

🍽️ *${d.name}*
_${[d.cuisine, `${d.totalTime} دقيقة`, `يكفي ${d.servings}`].filter(Boolean).join(" • ")}_

─────────────────────

${d.desc ? `_${d.desc}_\n\n` : ""}🛒 *المكونات الأساسية*
${ingLine}

${d.firstStep ? `👨‍🍳 *الطريقة*\n${d.firstStep}\n\n` : ""}─────────────────────
${d.servedWith ? `\n🍽️ *يقدم مع*\n${d.servedWith}\n` : ""}${d.howToServe ? `\n📌 *طريقة التقديم*\n${d.howToServe}\n` : ""}
─────────────────────

👉 الوصفة كاملة بالصور:
${d.link}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
🕌 _منيو — كتاب وصفاتك_`;
}

--- FILE: src/app/api/upload-latest/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { S3Client, PutObjectCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import { randomUUID } from "crypto";

const s3 = new S3Client({
  region: "auto",
  endpoint: `https://${process.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`,
  credentials: {
    accessKeyId: process.env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID!,
    secretAccessKey: process.env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY!,
  },
});

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const formData = await req.formData();
    const file = formData.get("file") as File;
    if (!file) return NextResponse.json({ error: "No file" }, { status: 400 });

    const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
    const ext = file.name.split(".").pop();
    const filename = `latest/${randomUUID()}.${ext}`;

    await s3.send(
      new PutObjectCommand({
        Bucket: process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME!,
        Key: filename,
        Body: buffer,
        ContentType: file.type,
      })
    );

    const url = `${process.env.R2_PUBLIC_URL}/${filename}`;
    return NextResponse.json({ url });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Upload error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Upload failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/upload-latest/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/upload/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { PutObjectCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import { getSignedUrl } from "@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner";
import { r2, R2_BUCKET } from "@/lib/r2";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import { randomUUID } from "crypto";

const ALLOWED_TYPES = [
  "image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp", "image/gif",
  "video/mp4", "video/webm", "video/quicktime",
];

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    const { filename, contentType } = await req.json();

    if (!filename || !contentType) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "filename and contentType are required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (!ALLOWED_TYPES.includes(contentType)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unsupported file type" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const ext = filename.split(".").pop() ?? "bin";
    const isVideo = contentType.startsWith("video/");
    const folder = isVideo ? "videos" : "recipes";
    const key = `${folder}/${randomUUID()}.${ext}`;

    const command = new PutObjectCommand({
      Bucket: R2_BUCKET,
      Key: key,
      ContentType: contentType,
    });

    const uploadUrl = await getSignedUrl(r2, command, {
      expiresIn: isVideo ? 900 : 60,
      unhoistableHeaders: new Set(["x-amz-checksum-crc32"]),
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ uploadUrl, key, isVideo });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Upload presign error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed to create upload URL" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/upload/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/user-activity/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { visitors } from "@/db/schema";

const PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

export async function PATCH(req: Request) {
  const body = await req.json();
  const page = body.page || "/";
  
  const store = await cookies();
  const phone = (store.get(PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
  if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ updated: false });

  const db = getDb();
  const existing = await db.select().from(visitors).where(eq(visitors.phone, phone)).limit(1);

  if (existing[0]) {
    await db.update(visitors)
      .set({ lastSeen: sql`CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`, lastPage: page, visits: existing[0].visits! + 1 })
      .where(eq(visitors.phone, phone));
  } else {
    await db.insert(visitors).values({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), phone, lastPage: page, visits: 1 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ updated: true });
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/user-activity/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/user-settings/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { userSettings } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, and } from "drizzle-orm";

const VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE = "viewer_phone";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({});

    const db = getDb();
    const rows = await db.select().from(userSettings).where(eq(userSettings.phone, phone));
    const result: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const r of rows) result[r.key] = r.value;
    return NextResponse.json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("GET user settings error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({});
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const store = await cookies();
    const phone = (store.get(VIEWER_PHONE_COOKIE)?.value || "").trim();
    if (!phone) return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, reason: "no_phone" });

    const body = await req.json();
    const { key, value } = body;
    if (!key || !value) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Missing key or value" }, { status: 400 });

    const db = getDb();
    const existing = await db
      .select()
      .from(userSettings)
      .where(and(eq(userSettings.phone, phone), eq(userSettings.key, key)))
      .limit(1);

    if (existing.length > 0) {
      await db
        .update(userSettings)
        .set({ value, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() })
        .where(eq(userSettings.id, existing[0].id));
    } else {
      await db.insert(userSettings).values({ phone, key, value });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("POST user settings error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/user-settings/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/api/visitors/route.ts ---
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { visitors, pushSubscriptions } from "@/db/schema";
import { desc, eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    // If the user is an admin, do not track them
    if (await isAdmin()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, skipped: true });
    }

    const db = getDb();
    const { id, phone } = await req.json();
    if (!id) return NextResponse.json({ error: "ID required" }, { status: 400 });

    await db.insert(visitors).values({ id, phone: phone || null, visits: 1 }).onConflictDoUpdate({
      target: visitors.id,
      set: { 
        lastSeen: new Date().toISOString(), 
        ...(phone ? { phone } : {}),
        visits: sql`${visitors.visits} + 1`
      },
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("POST visitor error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function PUT(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const { id, phone } = await req.json();
    if (!id || !phone) return NextResponse.json({ error: "ID and phone required" }, { status: 400 });

    const sub = await db.select().from(pushSubscriptions).where(eq(pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber, phone)).limit(1);
    if (sub.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Phone not registered for push notifications" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    await db.update(visitors).set({ phone }).where(eq(visitors.id, id));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("PUT visitor error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const idParam = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("id");
    if (!idParam) return NextResponse.json({ error: "ID required" }, { status: 400 });
    
    const id = String(idParam);
    
    await db.delete(visitors).where(eq(visitors.id, id));
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("DELETE visitor error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const db = getDb();
    const all = await db
      .select({
        id: visitors.id,
        phone: visitors.phone,
        visits: visitors.visits,
        lastSeen: visitors.lastSeen,
        lastPage: visitors.lastPage,
        name: pushSubscriptions.name,
      })
      .from(visitors)
      .leftJoin(pushSubscriptions, eq(visitors.phone, pushSubscriptions.phoneNumber))
      .orderBy(desc(visitors.lastSeen));
      
    return NextResponse.json(all);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("GET visitor error:", err);
    return NextResponse.json([], { status: 500 });
  }
}
--- END FILE: src/app/api/visitors/route.ts ---

--- FILE: src/app/appearance/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { useTheme } from "@/components/ThemeProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";

export default function AppearancePage() {
  const { lang, setLang } = useLang();
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const [fontSize, setFontSize] = useState("16px");

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("font_size") || "16px";
    setFontSize(saved);

    fetch("/api/user-settings")
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data.font_size) {
          setFontSize(data.font_size);
          document.documentElement.style.fontSize = data.font_size;
          localStorage.setItem("font_size", data.font_size);
        }
        if (data.theme) {
          setTheme(data.theme as "dark" | "light");
        }
        if (data.lang) {
          setLang(data.lang as "en" | "ar");
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  function handleFontSize(size: string) {
    setFontSize(size);
    localStorage.setItem("font_size", size);
    document.documentElement.style.fontSize = size;
    fetch("/api/user-settings", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ key: "font_size", value: size }),
    }).catch(() => {});
  }

  function handleTheme(t: "dark" | "light") {
    setTheme(t);
    fetch("/api/user-settings", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ key: "theme", value: t }),
    }).catch(() => {});
  }

  function handleLang(l: "en" | "ar") {
    document.cookie = `lang=${l}; path=/; max-age=31536000`;
    setLang(l);
    fetch("/api/user-settings", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ key: "lang", value: l }),
    }).catch(() => {});
  }

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 600 }}>
      
      {/* Back Button on its own line at the top left */}
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <button onClick={() => window.history.back()} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
          {isAr ? ar.appearance.back : "← Back"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Title Centered */}
      <div className="page-head" style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
        <h1>{isAr ? ar.appearance.title : "Appearance"}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub" style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
        {isAr ? ar.appearance.subtitle : "Customize how the app looks and feels."}
      </p>

      {/* LANGUAGE */}
      <div className="form-section">
        <h2>{isAr ? ar.appearance.language : "Language"}</h2>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10 }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`btn ${lang === "en" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`}
            onClick={() => handleLang("en")}
            style={{ flex: 1, padding: "16px" }}
          >
            {ar.appearance.english}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`btn ${lang === "ar" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`}
            onClick={() => handleLang("ar")}
            style={{ flex: 1, padding: "16px" }}
          >
            {ar.appearance.arabic}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* THEME */}
      <div className="form-section">
        <h2>{isAr ? ar.appearance.theme : "Theme"}</h2>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10 }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`btn ${theme === "dark" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`}
            onClick={() => handleTheme("dark")}
            style={{ flex: 1, padding: "16px" }}
          >
            {isAr ? ar.appearance.dark : "Dark"} ☾
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`btn ${theme === "light" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`}
            onClick={() => handleTheme("light")}
            style={{ flex: 1, padding: "16px" }}
          >
            {isAr ? ar.appearance.light : "Light"} ☀
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* FONT SIZE */}
      <div className="form-section">
        <h2>{isAr ? ar.appearance.textSize : "Text Size"}</h2>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`btn ${fontSize === "14px" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`}
            onClick={() => handleFontSize("14px")}
            style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 120, padding: "16px" }}
          >
            {isAr ? ar.appearance.small : "Small"} A-
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`btn ${fontSize === "16px" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`}
            onClick={() => handleFontSize("16px")}
            style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 120, padding: "16px" }}
          >
            {isAr ? ar.appearance.normal : "Default"} A
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`btn ${fontSize === "18px" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`}
            onClick={() => handleFontSize("18px")}
            style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 120, padding: "16px" }}
          >
            {isAr ? ar.appearance.large : "Large"} A+
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`btn ${fontSize === "20px" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`}
            onClick={() => handleFontSize("20px")}
            style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 120, padding: "16px" }}
          >
            {isAr ? ar.appearance.extraLarge : "Extra Large"} A++
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/calendar/[date]/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import DayReviews from "@/components/DayReviews";

type Slot = "breakfast" | "lunch" | "dinner" | "snack" | "dessert";

type Recipe = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  description: string | null;
  descriptionAr: string | null;
  imageUrl: string | null;
  category: string | null;
  categoryAr: string | null;
  prepTime: number;
  cookTime: number;
  servings: number;
};

type Entry = {
  id: number;
  date: string;
  slot: Slot;
  position: number;
  recipe: Recipe;
};

const SLOTS: { key: Slot; label: string; labelAr: string; color: string }[] = [
  { key: "breakfast", label: "Breakfast", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.breakfast, color: "var(--olive)" },
  { key: "lunch", label: "Lunch", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.lunch, color: "#d97757" },
  { key: "dinner", label: "Dinner", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.dinner, color: "var(--accent)" },
  { key: "snack", label: "Snack", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.snack, color: "#7ba05b" },
  { key: "dessert", label: "Dessert", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.dessert, color: "#c94e35" },
];

const MONTH_NAMES_EN = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];
const DAY_NAMES_EN = ["Sunday", "Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday"];

function getDayStatus(dateStr: string): "past" | "today" | "future" {
  const today = new Date();
  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const target = new Date(dateStr + "T00:00:00");
  if (target < today) return "past";
  if (target.getTime() === today.getTime()) return "today";
  return "future";
}

function formatLongDate(dateStr: string, lang: "en" | "ar"): string {
  const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split("-").map(Number);
  const date = new Date(y, m - 1, d);
  
  if (lang === "ar") {
    const dayName = ar.calendar.days[date.getDay()];
    const monthName = ar.calendar.months[m - 1];
    return `${dayName}، ${d} ${monthName} ${y}`;
  } else {
    const dayName = DAY_NAMES_EN[date.getDay()];
    const monthName = MONTH_NAMES_EN[m - 1];
    return `${dayName}, ${monthName} ${d}, ${y}`;
  }
}

export default function DayPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const params = useParams();
  const dateStr = params?.date as string;

  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<Entry[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me").then(r => r.json()).then(d => setIsAdmin(d.admin)).catch(() => {});
    if (!dateStr || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateStr)) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    
    // The API now returns { entries: [...] } instead of just an array
    fetch(`/api/meal-plan?date=${dateStr}`)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setEntries(Array.isArray(data.entries) ? data.entries : []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [dateStr]);

  function getShiftedDate(days: number) {
    const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split("-").map(Number);
    const date = new Date(y, m - 1, d);
    date.setDate(date.getDate() + days);
    const ny = date.getFullYear();
    const nm = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const nd = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${ny}-${nm}-${nd}`;
  }

  if (!dateStr || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateStr)) {
    return (
      <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40 }}>
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{isAr ? ar.calendar.invalidDate : "Invalid date"}</h2>
          <Link href="/calendar" className="btn btn-primary">
            {isAr ? ar.calendar.backToCalendar : "← Back to calendar"}
          </Link>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const status = getDayStatus(dateStr);
  const longDate = formatLongDate(dateStr, lang);

  const statusText = isAr
    ? (status === "past"
        ? `${ar.calendar.servedOn} ${longDate}`
        : status === "today"
          ? `${ar.calendar.toBeServedToday} ${longDate}`
          : `${ar.calendar.toBeServedOn} ${longDate}`)
    : (status === "past"
        ? `Served on ${longDate}`
        : status === "today"
          ? `To be served today, ${longDate}`
          : `To be served on ${longDate}`);

  const statusColor =
    status === "past" ? "var(--muted)" :
    status === "today" ? "var(--accent)" :
    "var(--olive)";

  const bySlot = new Map<Slot, Entry[]>();
  for (const e of entries) {
    if (!bySlot.has(e.slot)) bySlot.set(e.slot, []);
    bySlot.get(e.slot)!.push(e);
  }

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 800 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <Link href="/calendar" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
          {isAr ? ar.calendar.backToCalendar : "← Calendar"}
        </Link>
        
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          <Link href={`/calendar/${getShiftedDate(-1)}`} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", padding: "6px 12px" }}>
            {isAr ? ar.calendar.prevDay : "← Prev Day"}
          </Link>
          <Link href={`/calendar/${getShiftedDate(1)}`} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", padding: "6px 12px" }}>
            {isAr ? ar.calendar.nextDay : "Next Day →"}
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{longDate}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub" style={{ color: statusColor, fontWeight: 600 }}>
        {statusText}
      </p>

      {loading ? (
        <p className="helper">{isAr ? ar.calendar.loading : "Loading..."}</p>
      ) : entries.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{isAr ? ar.calendar.noMeals : "No meals planned"}</h2>
          <p>{isAr ? ar.calendar.noRecipesForDay : "No recipes assigned to this day."}</p>
          <Link href="/calendar" className="btn btn-primary">
            {isAr ? ar.calendar.backToCalendar : "← Back to calendar"}
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 24, marginTop: 24 }}>
          {SLOTS.map((slot) => {
            const slotEntries = bySlot.get(slot.key) || [];
            if (slotEntries.length === 0) return null;

            return (
              <section key={slot.key}>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, marginBottom: 12, paddingBottom: 8, borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                  <span style={{ display: "inline-block", width: 10, height: 10, borderRadius: "50%", background: slot.color }} />
                  <h2 style={{ margin: 0, fontFamily: "var(--font-body)", fontSize: "0.8125rem", fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "0.06em", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
                    {isAr ? slot.labelAr : slot.label}
                  </h2>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: "0.6875rem", color: "var(--muted)", marginInlineStart: "auto" }}>
                    {slotEntries.length} {isAr ? ar.calendar.recipes : slotEntries.length === 1 ? "recipe" : "recipes"}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
                  {slotEntries.map((entry) => {
                    const name = isAr ? (entry.recipe.nameAr || entry.recipe.name) : entry.recipe.name;
                    const desc = isAr ? (entry.recipe.descriptionAr || entry.recipe.description) : entry.recipe.description;
                    const category = isAr ? (entry.recipe.categoryAr || entry.recipe.category) : entry.recipe.category;
                    return (
                      <Link key={entry.id} href={`/recipes/${entry.recipe.id}`} style={{ display: "flex", gap: 14, padding: 12, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "var(--radius)", textDecoration: "none", color: "inherit", transition: "border-color 0.15s" }} onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "var(--line-strong)"; }} onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "var(--line)"; }}>
                        {entry.recipe.imageUrl ? (
                          <img src={entry.recipe.imageUrl} alt={name} loading="lazy" style={{ width: 100, height: 100, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 8, flexShrink: 0 }} />
                        ) : (
                          <div style={{ width: 100, height: 100, background: "var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, flexShrink: 0 }} />
                        )}
                        <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
                          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "flex-start", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 8 }}>
                            <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: "0.9375rem", fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--ink)", overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", display: "-webkit-box", WebkitLineClamp: 2, WebkitBoxOrient: "vertical" }}>{name}</h3>
                            {category && <span style={{ fontSize: 10, padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 999, background: "var(--surface-2)", color: "var(--muted)", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>{category}</span>}
                          </div>
                          {desc && <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "var(--ink-soft)", lineHeight: 1.5, overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", display: "-webkit-box", WebkitLineClamp: 2, WebkitBoxOrient: "vertical" }}>{desc}</p>}
                          <div style={{ fontSize: "0.6875rem", color: "var(--muted)", display: "flex", gap: 12, marginTop: "auto", fontVariantNumeric: "tabular-nums" }}>
                            <span>⏱ {entry.recipe.prepTime + entry.recipe.cookTime} {isAr ? ar.detail.min : "min"}</span>
                            <span>🍽 {entry.recipe.servings}</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </Link>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </section>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
      {dateStr && (
        <DayReviews date={dateStr} isAdmin={isAdmin} />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/calendar/[date]/page.tsx ---
