--- FILE: src/app/calendar/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { useModal } from "@/components/ModalProvider";

type Slot = "breakfast" | "lunch" | "dinner" | "snack" | "dessert";

type RecipeMini = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  imageUrl: string | null;
  category: string | null;
  categoryAr: string | null;
};

type PlanEntry = {
  id: number;
  date: string;
  slot: Slot;
  position: number;
  recipe: RecipeMini;
};

type RecipeFull = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  imageUrl: string | null;
  category: string | null;
  categoryAr: string | null;
};

const SLOTS: { key: Slot; label: string; labelAr: string; color: string }[] = [
  { key: "breakfast", label: "Breakfast", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.breakfast, color: "var(--olive)" },
  { key: "lunch", label: "Lunch", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.lunch, color: "#d97757" },
  { key: "dinner", label: "Dinner", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.dinner, color: "var(--accent)" },
  { key: "snack", label: "Snack", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.snack, color: "#7ba05b" },
  { key: "dessert", label: "Dessert", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.dessert, color: "#c94e35" },
];

const MAX_POSITIONS = 2;

function formatDate(y: number, m: number, d: number): string {
  return `${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(d).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function todayStr(): string {
  const t = new Date();
  return formatDate(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth(), t.getDate());
}

const MONTH_NAMES_EN = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];
const MONTH_NAMES_AR = ar.calendar.months;
const DAY_NAMES_EN = ["Sun", "Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat"];
const DAY_NAMES_AR = ar.calendar.days;

export default function CalendarPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const router = useRouter();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const { show } = useModal();

  const [year, setYear] = useState(() => new Date().getFullYear());
  const [month, setMonth] = useState(() => new Date().getMonth());
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<PlanEntry[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);

  const [slotMenuDate, setSlotMenuDate] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pickerDate, setPickerDate] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pickerSlot, setPickerSlot] = useState<Slot | null>(null);
  const [pickerPosition, setPickerPosition] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState<RecipeFull[]>([]);
  const [recipeSearch, setRecipeSearch] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((d) => setIsAdmin(!!d.admin))
      .catch(() => setIsAdmin(false));
  }, []);

  const monthParam = `${year}-${String(month + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  const loadMonth = useCallback(() => {
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/meal-plan?month=${monthParam}`, { cache: "no-store" })
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setEntries(Array.isArray(data.entries) ? data.entries : []);
        if (Array.isArray(data.recipes)) setRecipes(data.recipes);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [monthParam]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadMonth();
  }, [loadMonth]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!slotMenuDate) return;
    function closeSlotMenu(e: MouseEvent) {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (target.closest("button[data-slot-toggle]")) return;
      if (target.closest("[data-slot-menu]")) return;
      setSlotMenuDate(null);
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(() => document.addEventListener("click", closeSlotMenu), 0);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      document.removeEventListener("click", closeSlotMenu);
    };
  }, [slotMenuDate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pickerDate && recipes.length === 0) {
      fetch("/api/recipes?light=true", { cache: "no-store" })
        .then((r) => r.json())
        .then((d) => setRecipes(Array.isArray(d) ? d : []))
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [pickerDate, recipes.length]);

  const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();
  const firstDayOfWeek = new Date(year, month, 1).getDay();
  const today = todayStr();

  const byDate = new Map<string, Map<Slot, Map<number, PlanEntry>>>();
  for (const e of entries) {
    if (!byDate.has(e.date)) byDate.set(e.date, new Map());
    const slotMap = byDate.get(e.date)!;
    if (!slotMap.has(e.slot)) slotMap.set(e.slot, new Map());
    slotMap.get(e.slot)!.set(e.position, e);
  }

  function prevMonth() {
    if (month === 0) { setYear(year - 1); setMonth(11); }
    else setMonth(month - 1);
  }
  function nextMonth() {
    if (month === 11) { setYear(year + 1); setMonth(0); }
    else setMonth(month + 1);
  }
  function goToday() {
    const t = new Date();
    const dateStr = formatDate(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth(), t.getDate());
    router.push(`/calendar/${dateStr}`);
  }

  function openPicker(date: string, slot: Slot, position: number) {
    if (!isAdmin) return;
    setPickerDate(date);
    setPickerSlot(slot);
    setPickerPosition(position);
    setRecipeSearch("");
  }

  function closePicker() {
    setPickerDate(null);
    setPickerSlot(null);
    setPickerPosition(null);
  }

  async function assignRecipe(recipeId: number) {
    if (!pickerDate || !pickerSlot || pickerPosition === null) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/meal-plan", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          date: pickerDate,
          slot: pickerSlot,
          position: pickerPosition,
          recipeId,
        }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Save failed");
      closePicker();
      loadMonth();
    } catch (err) {
      show({ message: isAr ? ar.calendar.saveFailed : "Failed to save", tone: "danger" });
    }
  }

  async function removeEntry(id: number) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/meal-plan?id=${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Delete failed");
      closePicker();
      loadMonth();
    } catch (err) {
      show({ message: isAr ? ar.calendar.removeFailed : "Failed to remove", tone: "danger" });
    }
  }

  const filteredRecipes = recipeSearch.trim()
    ? recipes.filter((r) => {
        const q = recipeSearch.toLowerCase();
        return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (r.nameAr && r.nameAr.includes(recipeSearch));
      })
    : recipes;

  const pickerCurrentEntry =
    pickerDate && pickerSlot && pickerPosition !== null
      ? byDate.get(pickerDate)?.get(pickerSlot)?.get(pickerPosition) || null
      : null;

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{isAr ? ar.calendar.title : "Meal Calendar"}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">
        {isAr ? ar.calendar.sub : "Up to 2 recipes per slot per day"}
      </p>

      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={prevMonth} style={{ fontSize: "0.875rem", padding: "6px 12px" }}>←</button>
        <div style={{ fontSize: "1.25rem", fontWeight: 600, fontFamily: "var(--font-head)", flex: 1, textAlign: "center" }}>
          {isAr ? MONTH_NAMES_AR[month] : MONTH_NAMES_EN[month]} {year}
        </div>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={nextMonth} style={{ fontSize: "0.875rem", padding: "6px 12px" }}>→</button>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={goToday} style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", padding: "6px 12px" }}>
          {isAr ? ar.calendar.today : "Today"}
        </button>
        <Link href="/week" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", padding: "6px 12px" }}>📄 {isAr ? ar.nav.week : "Weekly"}</Link>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(7, 1fr)", gap: 4, marginBottom: 4 }}>
        {(isAr ? DAY_NAMES_AR : DAY_NAMES_EN).map((d) => (
          <div key={d} style={{ textAlign: "center", fontSize: "0.625rem", fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "0.05em", color: "var(--muted)", padding: "6px 0" }}>
            {d}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(7, minmax(0, 1fr))", gap: 4 }}>
        {Array.from({ length: firstDayOfWeek }).map((_, i) => (
          <div key={`empty-${i}`} />
        ))}

        {Array.from({ length: daysInMonth }).map((_, i) => {
          const day = i + 1;
          const date = formatDate(year, month, day);
          const dayEntries = byDate.get(date);
          const isToday = date === today;

          const slotFilled = new Map<Slot, PlanEntry[]>();
          if (dayEntries) {
            for (const [slot, posMap] of dayEntries.entries()) {
              slotFilled.set(slot, Array.from(posMap.values()).sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position));
            }
          }
          const hasAny = slotFilled.size > 0;

          return (
            <div
              key={date}
              onClick={(e) => {
                const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
                if (target.closest("button") || target.closest("a")) return;
                window.location.href = `/calendar/${date}`;
              }}
              style={{
                minHeight: 80,
                minWidth: 0,
                padding: 5,
                background: isToday ? "var(--accent-soft)" : "var(--surface)",
                border: isToday ? "1px solid var(--accent)" : "1px solid var(--line)",
                borderRadius: 8,
                display: "flex",
                flexDirection: "column",
                gap: 4,
                fontSize: "0.625rem",
                cursor: "pointer",
                position: "relative",
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: "0.6875rem", color: isToday ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--ink)" }}>
                  {day}
                </span>
                {isAdmin && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      e.preventDefault();
                      setSlotMenuDate(slotMenuDate === date ? null : date);
                    }}
                    onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    data-slot-toggle
                    title={isAr ? ar.calendar.add : "Add"}
                    style={{
                      background: "var(--accent)",
                      border: "1px solid var(--accent)",
                      color: "#fff",
                      cursor: "pointer",
                      fontSize: "0.8125rem",
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      width: 18,
                      height: 18,
                      borderRadius: 4,
                      display: "flex",
                      alignItems: "center",
                      justifyContent: "center",
                      padding: 0,
                      lineHeight: 1,
                      flexShrink: 0,
                    }}
                  >
                    +
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              {hasAny ? (
                SLOTS.map((slot) => {
                  const items = slotFilled.get(slot.key);
                  if (!items || items.length === 0) return null;
                  return (
                    <div key={slot.key} style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 1 }}>
                      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 3 }}>
                        <span style={{ display: "inline-block", width: 4, height: 4, borderRadius: "50%", background: slot.color, flexShrink: 0 }} />
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 7, fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "0.04em", color: "var(--muted)", flex: 1 }}>
                          {isAr ? slot.labelAr : slot.label}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      {items.map((entry) => (
                        <div
                          key={entry.id}
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            if (isAdmin) openPicker(date, slot.key, entry.position);
                            else window.location.href = `/calendar/${date}`;
                          }}
                          title={`${isAr ? slot.labelAr : slot.label} ${entry.position}: ${isAr ? (entry.recipe.nameAr || entry.recipe.name) : entry.recipe.name}`}
                          style={{
                            display: "block",
                            maxWidth: "100%",
                            padding: "2px 4px",
                            borderRadius: 3,
                            background: "var(--bg)",
                            color: "var(--ink)",
                            fontSize: 9,
                            overflow: "hidden",
                            textOverflow: "ellipsis",
                            whiteSpace: "nowrap",
                            borderLeft: `2px solid ${slot.color}`,
                            cursor: "pointer",
                          }}
                        >
                          {isAr ? (entry.recipe.nameAr || entry.recipe.name) : entry.recipe.name}
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  );
                })
              ) : (
                isAdmin && (
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 8, color: "var(--muted)", fontStyle: "italic", marginTop: "auto" }}>
                    {isAr ? ar.calendar.noMeals : "No meals"}
                  </div>
                )
              )}

              {isAdmin && slotMenuDate === date && (
                <div
                  data-slot-menu
                  onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                  onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                  style={{
                    position: "absolute",
                    top: "calc(100% + 4px)",
                    left: 0,
                    zIndex: 50,
                    background: "var(--surface-solid)",
                    border: "1px solid var(--line-strong)",
                    borderRadius: 8,
                    boxShadow: "var(--shadow-lg)",
                    padding: 4,
                    display: "flex",
                    flexDirection: "column",
                    gap: 2,
                    minWidth: 130,
                  }}
                >
                  {SLOTS.map((slot) => {
                    const positionsUsed = slotFilled.get(slot.key)?.length || 0;
                    const firstEmpty = positionsUsed < 2 ? positionsUsed + 1 : null;
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={slot.key}
                        type="button"
                        disabled={!firstEmpty}
                        onClick={() => {
                          if (!firstEmpty) return;
                          setSlotMenuDate(null);
                          openPicker(date, slot.key, firstEmpty);
                        }}
                        style={{
                          background: "transparent",
                          border: "none",
                          textAlign: "start",
                          padding: "5px 8px",
                          fontSize: "0.6875rem",
                          color: firstEmpty ? "var(--ink)" : "var(--muted)",
                          cursor: firstEmpty ? "pointer" : "not-allowed",
                          borderRadius: 4,
                          display: "flex",
                          alignItems: "center",
                          gap: 6,
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => { if (firstEmpty) e.currentTarget.style.background = "var(--surface-2)"; }}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"; }}
                      >
                        <span style={{ display: "inline-block", width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: "50%", background: slot.color }} />
                        {isAr ? slot.labelAr : slot.label}
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 9, color: "var(--muted)", marginInlineStart: "auto" }}>
                          {positionsUsed}/2
                        </span>
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {loading && (
        <p className="helper" style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>{isAr ? ar.calendar.loading : "Loading..."}</p>
      )}

      {pickerDate && pickerSlot && pickerPosition !== null && (
        <div
          onClick={closePicker}
          style={{
            position: "fixed",
            inset: 0,
            background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)",
            backdropFilter: "blur(8px)",
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            padding: 20,
            zIndex: 100,
          }}
        >
          <div
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            style={{
              background: "var(--surface-solid)",
              border: "1px solid var(--line-strong)",
              borderRadius: 12,
              padding: 20,
              maxWidth: 500,
              width: "100%",
              maxHeight: "80vh",
              display: "flex",
              flexDirection: "column",
              gap: 14,
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
              <div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: "0.6875rem", fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                  {pickerDate}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: "1rem", fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--ink)" }}>
                  {SLOTS.find((s) => s.key === pickerSlot)?.[isAr ? "labelAr" : "label"]} · {isAr ? ar.calendar.slotRecipe : "Recipe"} {pickerPosition}
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={closePicker}
                style={{ background: "transparent", border: "none", fontSize: "1.25rem", cursor: "pointer", color: "var(--muted)" }}
              >
                ×
              </button>
            </div>

            {pickerCurrentEntry && (
              <div style={{ padding: 10, background: "var(--bg)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8 }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: "0.6875rem", color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                  {isAr ? ar.calendar.currentRecipe : "Current"}
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
                  {pickerCurrentEntry.recipe.imageUrl ? (
                    <img src={pickerCurrentEntry.recipe.imageUrl} alt={pickerCurrentEntry.recipe.name} style={{ width: 40, height: 40, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6 }} />
                  ) : (
                    <div style={{ width: 40, height: 40, background: "var(--line)", borderRadius: 6 }} />
                  )}
                  <div style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: "0.8125rem", fontWeight: 500 }}>
                    {isAr ? (pickerCurrentEntry.recipe.nameAr || pickerCurrentEntry.recipe.name) : pickerCurrentEntry.recipe.name}
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn btn-ghost"
                    onClick={() => removeEntry(pickerCurrentEntry.id)}
                    style={{ fontSize: "0.6875rem", padding: "4px 10px" }}
                  >
                    {isAr ? ar.calendar.deleteBtn : "Remove"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder={isAr ? ar.calendar.searchRecipePlaceholder : "Search recipes..."}
              value={recipeSearch}
              onChange={(e) => setRecipeSearch(e.target.value)}
              style={{ width: "100%", padding: "8px 12px", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, background: "var(--input-bg)", color: "var(--ink)", fontSize: "0.8125rem" }}
              autoFocus
            />

            <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: "auto", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
              {filteredRecipes.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="helper">{isAr ? ar.calendar.noRecipes : "No recipes"}</p>
              ) : (
                filteredRecipes.map((r) => (
                  <button
                    key={r.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => assignRecipe(r.id)}
                    style={{
                      display: "flex",
                      alignItems: "center",
                      gap: 10,
                      padding: 8,
                      background: "transparent",
                      border: "1px solid var(--line)",
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      cursor: "pointer",
                      color: "var(--ink)",
                      textAlign: "start",
                      fontSize: "0.8125rem",
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.background = "var(--surface-2)"; }}
                    onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"; }}
                  >
                    {r.imageUrl ? (
                      <img src={r.imageUrl} alt={r.name} style={{ width: 36, height: 36, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6, flexShrink: 0 }} />
                    ) : (
                      <div style={{ width: 36, height: 36, background: "var(--line)", borderRadius: 6, flexShrink: 0 }} />
                    )}
                    <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 500 }}>{isAr ? (r.nameAr || r.name) : r.name}</div>
                      {r.category && (
                        <div style={{ fontSize: "0.6875rem", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                          {isAr ? (r.categoryAr || r.category) : r.category}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/games/catch/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import GameHeader from "@/components/GameHeader";
import { useGameStats } from "@/hooks/useGameStats";

type Item = {
  id: number;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  speed: number;
  emoji: string;
  isBad: boolean;
};

const GOOD_ITEMS = ["🥔", "🧅", "🍅", "🥕", "🥦", "🌽", "🍄"];
const BAD_ITEM = "🔴";
const BASKET_WIDTH = 100;
const BASKET_HEIGHT = 50;
const ITEM_SIZE = 40;

export default function CatchGamePage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const { stats, saveStats } = useGameStats("catch", { lastCatched: 0, bestCatched: 0, played: 0 });

  const gameAreaRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const basketRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const requestRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const lastTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const spawnTimerRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const itemsRef = useRef<Item[]>([]);
  const basketXRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const isPlayingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const livesRef = useRef<number>(3);
  const idCounterRef = useRef<number>(0);

  const [score, setScore] = useState(0);
  const [lives, setLives] = useState(3);
  const [gameOver, setGameOver] = useState(false);
  const [showRules, setShowRules] = useState(false);
  const [renderItems, setRenderItems] = useState<Item[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => { livesRef.current = lives; }, [lives]);

  // Save score when the game ends
  useEffect(() => {
    if (gameOver) {
      saveStats((prev) => ({
        lastCatched: score,
        bestCatched: Math.max(prev.bestCatched || 0, score),
        played: (prev.played || 0) + 1
      }));
    }
  }, [gameOver]); // Only trigger on game over

  const stopGame = () => {
    isPlayingRef.current = false;
    if (requestRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(requestRef.current);
    requestRef.current = 0;
    itemsRef.current = [];
    setRenderItems([]);
  };

  const loseLife = () => {
    const next = livesRef.current - 1;
    livesRef.current = next;
    setLives(next);
    if (next <= 0) {
      stopGame();
      setGameOver(true);
    }
  };

  const spawnItem = (width: number) => {
    const isBad = Math.random() < 0.2;
    const emoji = isBad ? BAD_ITEM : GOOD_ITEMS[Math.floor(Math.random() * GOOD_ITEMS.length)];
    const x = Math.random() * (width - ITEM_SIZE);
    const speed = 0.12 + Math.random() * 0.08;
    itemsRef.current.push({ id: idCounterRef.current++, x, y: -ITEM_SIZE, speed, emoji, isBad });
  };

  const loop = (time: number) => {
    if (!isPlayingRef.current) return;
    const area = gameAreaRef.current;
    if (!area) return;

    if (!lastTimeRef.current) lastTimeRef.current = time;
    const dt = time - lastTimeRef.current;
    lastTimeRef.current = time;

    const width = area.clientWidth;
    const height = area.clientHeight;

    spawnTimerRef.current += dt;
    if (spawnTimerRef.current > 750) {
      spawnTimerRef.current = 0;
      spawnItem(width);
    }

    const basketLeft = basketXRef.current - BASKET_WIDTH / 2;
    const basketRight = basketXRef.current + BASKET_WIDTH / 2;
    const basketTop = height - 20 - BASKET_HEIGHT;

    const survivors: Item[] = [];
    for (const it of itemsRef.current) {
      it.y += it.speed * dt;
      const itLeft = it.x;
      const itRight = it.x + ITEM_SIZE;
      const itBottom = it.y + ITEM_SIZE;

      if (itBottom >= basketTop && it.y <= basketTop + BASKET_HEIGHT && itRight >= basketLeft && itLeft <= basketRight) {
        if (it.isBad) loseLife();
        else setScore((s) => s + 1);
        continue;
      }

      if (it.y > height) {
        if (!it.isBad) loseLife();
        continue;
      }
      survivors.push(it);
    }
    itemsRef.current = survivors;
    setRenderItems([...survivors]);

    if (isPlayingRef.current) requestRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  };

  const startGame = () => {
    stopGame();
    setScore(0);
    setLives(3);
    livesRef.current = 3;
    setGameOver(false);
    lastTimeRef.current = 0;
    spawnTimerRef.current = 0;
    idCounterRef.current = 0;
    itemsRef.current = [];

    const area = gameAreaRef.current;
    if (area) {
      const cx = area.clientWidth / 2;
      basketXRef.current = cx;
      if (basketRef.current) basketRef.current.style.left = `${cx}px`;
    }
    isPlayingRef.current = true;
    requestRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => startGame(), 50);
    return () => { clearTimeout(t); stopGame(); };
  }, []);

  const handleMove = (clientX: number) => {
    const area = gameAreaRef.current;
    const basket = basketRef.current;
    if (!area || !basket) return;
    const rect = area.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = clientX - rect.left;
    const half = BASKET_WIDTH / 2;
    const clamped = Math.max(half, Math.min(rect.width - half, x));
    basketXRef.current = clamped;
    basket.style.left = `${clamped}px`;
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ position: "relative", height: "100dvh", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", background: "var(--bg)", overflow: "hidden" }}>
      <Link href="/games" style={{ position: "absolute", top: 12, insetInlineEnd: 12, zIndex: 50, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: "none", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
        ✕ {isAr ? "خروج" : "Exit"}
      </Link>

      <GameHeader
        title="Catch the Ingredients"
        titleAr="اجمع المكونات"
        onNewGame={startGame}
        onShowRules={() => setShowRules(true)}
        score={score}
        scoreLabel="SCORE"
        extra={lives}
        extraLabel="LIVES"
        best={stats.bestCatched}
        bestLabel="BEST"
      />

      <div ref={gameAreaRef} onMouseMove={(e) => handleMove(e.clientX)} onTouchMove={(e) => handleMove(e.touches[0].clientX)} onTouchStart={(e) => handleMove(e.touches[0].clientX)} style={{ position: "relative", flex: 1, width: "100%", overflow: "hidden", touchAction: "none" }}>
        {renderItems.map((it) => (
          <div key={it.id} style={{ position: "absolute", left: it.x, top: it.y, width: ITEM_SIZE, height: ITEM_SIZE, fontSize: 32, lineHeight: `${ITEM_SIZE}px`, textAlign: "center", pointerEvents: "none", userSelect: "none" }}>
            {it.emoji}
          </div>
        ))}
        <div ref={basketRef} style={{ position: "absolute", bottom: 20, width: BASKET_WIDTH, height: BASKET_HEIGHT, background: "#8b5a2b", borderRadius: "0 0 40px 40px", border: "4px solid #5c3a1a", borderTop: "none", transform: "translateX(-50%)", zIndex: 5, boxShadow: "inset 0 -10px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.5), 0 10px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)" }} />
      </div>

      {showRules && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 100 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", padding: 32, borderRadius: 20, maxWidth: 400, width: "100%" }}>
            <h2 style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>{isAr ? ar.catch.rulesTitle : "Game Rules"}</h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: "0.9rem", lineHeight: 1.5, color: "var(--ink-soft)" }} dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: isAr ? ar.catch.rulesText : "<strong>Goal:</strong> Move the basket to catch falling ingredients.<br><br><strong>Lives:</strong> You have 3 hearts. If an ingredient falls or you catch a red item, you lose a heart.<br><br><strong>Score:</strong> Each ingredient you catch gives you a point!" }} />
            <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => setShowRules(false)} style={{ width: "100%", marginTop: 16 }}>{isAr ? ar.catch.gotIt : "Got it"}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {gameOver && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 100 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", padding: 32, borderRadius: 20, maxWidth: 400, width: "100%", textAlign: "center" }}>
            <h2 style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-head)", fontSize: "2rem", margin: "0 0 8px 0" }}>{isAr ? ar.catch.gameOver : "Game Over"}</h2>
            <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 24 }}>{isAr ? ar.catch.youCaught : "You caught"}</p>
            <div style={{ fontSize: "3rem", fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--accent)", marginBottom: 24 }}>{score}</div>
            <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 24 }}>{isAr ? ar.catch.ingredientsCount : "ingredients!"}</p>
            <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={startGame} style={{ width: "100%" }}>{isAr ? ar.catch.playAgain : "Play Again"}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/games/catch/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/games/chess/[code]/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

type PieceType = "p" | "n" | "b" | "r" | "q" | "k";
type Piece = [PieceType, "w" | "b"];
type Square = Piece | null;
type Board = Square[][];
type Pos = { r: number; c: number };

type Room = {
  id: number;
  code: string;
  whitePhone: string;
  blackPhone: string | null;
  board: string;
  turn: "w" | "b";
  status: "waiting" | "active" | "finished";
  winner: string | null;
};

const PIECES: Record<"w" | "b", Record<PieceType, string>> = {
  w: { k: "♔", q: "♕", r: "♖", b: "♗", n: "♘", p: "♙" },
  b: { k: "♚", q: "♛", r: "♜", b: "♝", n: "♞", p: "♟" },
};

const START: Board = [
  [["r","b"],["n","b"],["b","b"],["q","b"],["k","b"],["b","b"],["n","b"],["r","b"]],
  Array(8).fill(["p","b"] as Piece),
  [null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null],
  [null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null],
  [null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null],
  [null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null],
  Array(8).fill(["p","w"] as Piece),
  [["r","w"],["n","w"],["b","w"],["q","w"],["k","w"],["b","w"],["n","w"],["r","w"]],
];

function cloneBoard(b: Board): Board { return b.map((row) => row.map((sq) => (sq ? [...sq] as Piece : null))); }
function inside(r: number, c: number): boolean { return r >= 0 && r < 8 && c >= 0 && c < 8; }

function findKing(board: Board, color: "w" | "b"): Pos | null {
  for (let r = 0; r < 8; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
    const p = board[r][c];
    if (p && p[0] === "k" && p[1] === color) return { r, c };
  }
  return null;
}

function isInCheck(board: Board, color: "w" | "b"): boolean {
  const king = findKing(board, color);
  if (!king) return false;
  const opp: "w" | "b" = color === "w" ? "b" : "w";
  for (let r = 0; r < 8; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
    const p = board[r][c];
    if (!p || p[1] !== opp) continue;
    if (canReach(board, { r, c }, king)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function canReach(board: Board, from: Pos, to: Pos): boolean {
  const p = board[from.r][from.c];
  if (!p) return false;
  const type = p[0];
  const color = p[1];
  const dr = to.r - from.r, dc = to.c - from.c;
  const absR = Math.abs(dr), absC = Math.abs(dc);
  const clearPath = () => {
    const stepR = Math.sign(dr), stepC = Math.sign(dc);
    let r = from.r + stepR, c = from.c + stepC;
    while (r !== to.r || c !== to.c) { if (board[r][c]) return false; r += stepR; c += stepC; }
    return true;
  };
  switch (type) {
    case "p": {
      const dir = color === "w" ? -1 : 1;
      const startRow = color === "w" ? 6 : 1;
      if (dc === 0 && dr === dir && !board[to.r][to.c]) return true;
      if (dc === 0 && dr === dir * 2 && from.r === startRow && !board[to.r][to.c] && !board[from.r + dir][from.c]) return true;
      if (absC === 1 && dr === dir && board[to.r][to.c] && board[to.r][to.c]![1] !== color) return true;
      return false;
    }
    case "n": return (absR === 2 && absC === 1) || (absR === 1 && absC === 2);
    case "b": return absR === absC && absR > 0 && clearPath();
    case "r": return ((dr === 0 && dc !== 0) || (dc === 0 && dr !== 0)) && clearPath();
    case "q": return ((absR === absC && absR > 0) || (dr === 0 && dc !== 0) || (dc === 0 && dr !== 0)) && clearPath();
    case "k": return absR <= 1 && absC <= 1 && (absR + absC > 0);
  }
}

function isLegalMove(board: Board, from: Pos, to: Pos): boolean {
  const p = board[from.r][from.c];
  if (!p) return false;
  const target = board[to.r][to.c];
  if (target && target[1] === p[1]) return false;
  if (!canReach(board, from, to)) return false;
  const test = cloneBoard(board);
  test[to.r][to.c] = test[from.r][from.c];
  test[from.r][from.c] = null;
  return !isInCheck(test, p[1]);
}

function hasAnyLegalMove(board: Board, color: "w" | "b"): boolean {
  for (let r = 0; r < 8; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
    const p = board[r][c];
    if (!p || p[1] !== color) continue;
    for (let tr = 0; tr < 8; tr++) for (let tc = 0; tc < 8; tc++) {
      if (isLegalMove(board, { r, c }, { r: tr, c: tc })) return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}


// Compute captured pieces by comparing current board to initial board
function getCapturedPieces(board: Board): { w: PieceType[]; b: PieceType[] } {
  const initial: Record<"w" | "b", Record<PieceType, number>> = {
    w: { p: 8, n: 2, b: 2, r: 2, q: 1, k: 1 },
    b: { p: 8, n: 2, b: 2, r: 2, q: 1, k: 1 },
  };
  const current: Record<"w" | "b", Record<PieceType, number>> = {
    w: { p: 0, n: 0, b: 0, r: 0, q: 0, k: 0 },
    b: { p: 0, n: 0, b: 0, r: 0, q: 0, k: 0 },
  };
  for (const row of board) {
    for (const sq of row) {
      if (sq) current[sq[1]][sq[0]]++;
    }
  }
  const capturedW: PieceType[] = []; // Black pieces captured by White
  const capturedB: PieceType[] = []; // White pieces captured by Black
  for (const type of ["p","n","b","r","q"] as PieceType[]) {
    const lostBlack = initial.b[type] - current.b[type];
    const lostWhite = initial.w[type] - current.w[type];
    for (let i = 0; i < lostBlack; i++) capturedW.push(type);
    for (let i = 0; i < lostWhite; i++) capturedB.push(type);
  }
  return { w: capturedW, b: capturedB };
}

export default function ChessRoomPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const code = String(params.code || "").toUpperCase();
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";

  const [room, setRoom] = useState<Room | null>(null);
  const [board, setBoard] = useState<Board>(cloneBoard(START));
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Pos | null>(null);
  const [gameOver, setGameOver] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [myColor, setMyColor] = useState<"w" | "b" | "spectator">("spectator");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const lastTurnRef = useRef<string>("");

  // Fetch my role + join if possible + initial state
  useEffect(() => {
    async function init() {
      try {
        // Try to join (will succeed if we're not already a player)
        const joinRes = await fetch(`/api/chess/rooms/${code}`, { method: "POST" });
        const joinData = await joinRes.json();

        if (joinData.error === "room_full") {
          // Could be a spectator — try plain GET
          const getRes = await fetch(`/api/chess/rooms/${code}`);
          const getData = await getRes.json();
          if (getData.room) {
            setRoom(getData.room);
            setBoard(JSON.parse(getData.room.board));
            lastTurnRef.current = getData.room.turn;
          } else {
            setError("Room not found");
          }
          setMyColor("spectator");
        } else if (joinData.room) {
          setRoom(joinData.room);
          setBoard(JSON.parse(joinData.room.board));
          lastTurnRef.current = joinData.room.turn;
          // Use the role returned by the server (it reads the httpOnly cookie)
          if (joinData.role === "white") setMyColor("w");
          else if (joinData.role === "black") setMyColor("b");
          else setMyColor("spectator");
        }
      } catch (e) {
        setError(String(e));
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }
    if (code) init();
  }, [code]);

  // Poll for updates
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!code) return;
    const interval = setInterval(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/chess/rooms/${code}`, { cache: "no-store" });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.room) {
          setRoom(data.room);
          if (data.room.turn !== lastTurnRef.current) {
            setBoard(JSON.parse(data.room.board));
            lastTurnRef.current = data.room.turn;
            setSelected(null);
          }
          if (data.room.winner) {
            setGameOver(data.room.winner === "w" ? (isAr ? "كش مات — فاز الأبيض" : "Checkmate — White wins") : (isAr ? "كش مات — فاز الأسود" : "Checkmate — Black wins"));
          }
        }
      } catch {}
    }, 1500);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [code, isAr]);

  // Retry role detection if we're still a spectator but should be a player
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!code || myColor !== "spectator" || !room) return;
    const retry = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/chess/rooms/${code}`, { method: "POST" });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.role === "white") setMyColor("w");
        else if (data.role === "black") setMyColor("b");
      } catch {}
    };
    const t = setTimeout(retry, 500);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [code, myColor, room]);

  async function pushMove(newBoard: Board, newTurn: "w" | "b", winner: string | null) {
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/chess/rooms/${code}/move`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ newBoard, newTurn, winner }),
      });
      setBoard(newBoard);
      lastTurnRef.current = newTurn;
      setSelected(null);
      if (winner) {
        setGameOver(winner === "w" ? (isAr ? "كش مات — فاز الأبيض" : "Checkmate — White wins") : (isAr ? "كش مات — فاز الأسود" : "Checkmate — Black wins"));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError(String(e));
    }
  }

  function handleSquareClick(r: number, c: number) {
    if (!room || room.status !== "active" || gameOver) return;
    if (myColor === "spectator") return;
    const currentTurnColor = room.turn;
    if (myColor !== currentTurnColor) return; // not your turn

    const p = board[r][c];

    if (!selected) {
      if (p && p[1] === myColor) setSelected({ r, c });
      return;
    }
    if (selected.r === r && selected.c === c) { setSelected(null); return; }
    if (p && p[1] === myColor) { setSelected({ r, c }); return; }

    if (isLegalMove(board, selected, { r, c })) {
      const next = cloneBoard(board);
      const piece = next[selected.r][selected.c]!;
      let finalPiece: Piece = piece;
      if (piece[0] === "p" && (r === 0 || r === 7)) finalPiece = ["q", piece[1]];
      next[r][c] = finalPiece;
      next[selected.r][selected.c] = null;

      const nextTurn: "w" | "b" = currentTurnColor === "w" ? "b" : "w";
      const check = isInCheck(next, nextTurn);
      const canMove = hasAnyLegalMove(next, nextTurn);
      let winner: string | null = null;
      if (!canMove) {
        if (check) winner = myColor; // I just won
        // stalemate → no winner, no checkmate; we'll just stop
      }
      pushMove(next, nextTurn, winner);
    } else {
      setSelected(null);
    }
  }

  if (loading) return <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: "center" }}>{isAr ? "جارٍ التحميل..." : "Loading..."}</div>;
  if (error) return <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: "center", color: "var(--accent)" }}>{error}</div>;
  if (!room) return <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: "center" }}>{isAr ? "لم يتم العثور على الغرفة" : "Room not found"}</div>;

  const view: "w" | "b" = myColor === "b" ? "b" : "w";
  const rows = view === "w" ? [...Array(8).keys()] : [...Array(8).keys()].reverse();
  const cols = view === "w" ? [...Array(8).keys()] : [...Array(8).keys()].reverse();

  const highlighted = new Set<string>();
  if (selected) {
    for (let r = 0; r < 8; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
      if (isLegalMove(board, selected, { r, c })) highlighted.add(`${r},${c}`);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ minHeight: "100dvh", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", padding: 8, background: "var(--bg)", overflowX: "hidden" }}>
      <Link href="/games/chess/lobby" style={{ position: "absolute", top: 12, insetInlineEnd: 12, zIndex: 50, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: "none", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
        ✕ {isAr ? "خروج" : "Exit"}
      </Link>

      <div style={{ maxWidth: 600, margin: "0 auto", width: "100%", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
        <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", borderRadius: 12, padding: 16, textAlign: "center" }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 4 }}>
            {isAr ? "رمز الغرفة" : "Room code"}
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: 6, fontFamily: "monospace", color: "var(--accent)" }}>
            {room.code}
          </div>
        </div>

        {room.status === "waiting" && (
          <div style={{ background: "var(--accent-soft)", color: "var(--accent)", padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700 }}>
            ⏳ {isAr ? "في انتظار انضمام اللاعب الثاني..." : "Waiting for the second player to join..."}
          </div>
        )}

        {room.status === "active" && (
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 16, color: "var(--ink)" }}>
            {myColor === "spectator"
              ? (isAr ? "أنت مشاهد" : "You are a spectator")
              : room.turn === myColor
                ? (isAr ? "🎮 دورك!" : "🎮 Your turn!")
                : (isAr ? "⏳ دور الخصم..." : "⏳ Opponent's turn...")}
          </div>
        )}

        <div
          style={{
            display: "grid",
            gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(8, 1fr)",
            gridTemplateRows: "repeat(8, 1fr)",
            aspectRatio: "1 / 1",
            width: "100%",
            maxWidth: "100%",
            margin: "0 auto",
            border: "4px solid #5c3a1a",
            boxSizing: "border-box",
            borderRadius: 8,
            overflow: "hidden",
            boxShadow: "0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.4)",
          }}
        >
          {rows.map((r) =>
            cols.map((c) => {
              const isLight = (r + c) % 2 === 0;
              const piece = board[r][c];
              const isSelected = selected && selected.r === r && selected.c === c;
              const isHighlighted = highlighted.has(`${r},${c}`);
              return (
                <div
                  key={`${r}-${c}`}
                  onClick={() => handleSquareClick(r, c)}
                  style={{
                    background: isLight ? "#f0d9b5" : "#b58863",
                    display: "flex",
                    alignItems: "center",
                    justifyContent: "center",
                    fontSize: "clamp(36px, 13vw, 60px)",
                    lineHeight: 1,
                    cursor: "pointer",
                    minWidth: 0,
                    minHeight: 0,
                    overflow: "hidden",
                    position: "relative",
                    userSelect: "none",
                    outline: isSelected ? "3px solid #ff6b4a" : "none",
                    outlineOffset: "-3px",
                  }}
                >
                  {isHighlighted && (
                    <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: "30%", borderRadius: "50%", background: "rgba(63, 185, 80, 0.5)" }} />
                  )}
                  {piece && (
                    <span style={{ color: piece[1] === "w" ? "#ffffff" : "#000000", textShadow: piece[1] === "w" ? "0 0 2px #000, 1px 1px 0 #000" : "0 0 2px #fff, 1px 1px 0 #fff", position: "relative", zIndex: 1 }}>
                      {PIECES[piece[1]][piece[0]]}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>

        {(() => {
          const captured = getCapturedPieces(board);
          return (
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6, background: "var(--surface)", padding: 12, borderRadius: 10 }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--muted)", minWidth: 50 }}>
                  {isAr ? "الأبيض" : "White"}
                </span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 22, letterSpacing: 2, color: "var(--ink)" }}>
                  {captured.w.length === 0 ? "—" : captured.w.map((t, i) => (
                    <span key={i} style={{ color: "#000", textShadow: "0 0 1px #fff" }}>{PIECES.b[t]}</span>
                  ))}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--muted)", minWidth: 50 }}>
                  {isAr ? "الأسود" : "Black"}
                </span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 22, letterSpacing: 2, color: "var(--ink)" }}>
                  {captured.b.length === 0 ? "—" : captured.b.map((t, i) => (
                    <span key={i} style={{ color: "#fff", textShadow: "0 0 1px #000" }}>{PIECES.w[t]}</span>
                  ))}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })()}

        {gameOver && (
          <div style={{ background: "var(--olive-soft)", border: "1px solid var(--olive)", padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 18, color: "var(--olive)" }}>
            {gameOver}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

type Room = {
  id: number;
  code: string;
  whitePhone: string;
  blackPhone: string | null;
  status: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

export default function ChessLobbyPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const router = useRouter();

  const [rooms, setRooms] = useState<Room[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [joinCode, setJoinCode] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/chess/rooms")
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((d) => {
        setRooms(d.rooms || []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  async function createRoom() {
    setCreating(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/chess/rooms", { method: "POST" });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.room) {
        router.push(`/games/chess/${data.room.code}`);
      } else {
        setError(data.error || "Failed to create room");
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError(String(e));
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  }

  async function joinRoom() {
    const code = joinCode.trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!code) return;
    setError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/chess/rooms/${code}`, { method: "POST" });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.room) {
        router.push(`/games/chess/${code}`);
      } else {
        setError(data.error === "room_full" ? (isAr ? "الغرفة ممتلئة" : "Room is full") : (isAr ? "لم يتم العثور على الغرفة" : "Room not found"));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError(String(e));
    }
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ minHeight: "100vh", background: "var(--bg)", padding: "40px 16px" }}>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 600, margin: "0 auto" }}>
        <Link href="/games" style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--muted)", textDecoration: "none", marginBottom: 24, display: "inline-block" }}>
          ← {isAr ? "الألعاب" : "Games"}
        </Link>

        <h1 style={{ fontSize: "2rem", fontWeight: 800, margin: "0 0 8px", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
          ♔ {isAr ? "شطرنج أونلاين" : "Online Chess"}
        </h1>
        <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 32 }}>
          {isAr ? "العب مع صديق على جهاز آخر" : "Play with a friend on another device"}
        </p>

        {/* CREATE ROOM */}
        <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 16, padding: 24, marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", fontWeight: 700, margin: "0 0 12px", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
            {isAr ? "إنشاء غرفة جديدة" : "Create a new room"}
          </h2>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 16 }}>
            {isAr ? "ستحصل على رمز لمشاركته مع صديقك" : "You'll get a code to share with your friend"}
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={createRoom}
            disabled={creating}
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              background: "var(--accent)",
              color: "white",
              border: "none",
              padding: "14px",
              borderRadius: 10,
              fontSize: 16,
              fontWeight: 700,
              cursor: creating ? "wait" : "pointer",
              opacity: creating ? 0.6 : 1,
            }}
          >
            {creating ? (isAr ? "جارٍ الإنشاء..." : "Creating...") : (isAr ? "➕ إنشاء غرفة" : "➕ Create room")}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* JOIN ROOM */}
        <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 16, padding: 24, marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", fontWeight: 700, margin: "0 0 12px", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
            {isAr ? "الانضمام إلى غرفة" : "Join a room"}
          </h2>
          <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 16 }}>
            {isAr ? "أدخل رمز الغرفة الذي شاركه صديقك" : "Enter the room code your friend shared"}
          </p>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={joinCode}
              onChange={(e) => setJoinCode(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && joinRoom()}
              placeholder={isAr ? "مثال: ABC123" : "e.g. ABC123"}
              maxLength={6}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                padding: "12px 16px",
                border: "1px solid var(--line)",
                borderRadius: 10,
                fontSize: 18,
                fontWeight: 700,
                letterSpacing: 4,
                textAlign: "center",
                background: "var(--bg)",
                color: "var(--ink)",
                textTransform: "uppercase",
                fontFamily: "monospace",
              }}
            />
            <button
              onClick={joinRoom}
              disabled={!joinCode.trim()}
              style={{
                background: "var(--olive)",
                color: "white",
                border: "none",
                padding: "12px 24px",
                borderRadius: 10,
                fontSize: 15,
                fontWeight: 700,
                cursor: joinCode.trim() ? "pointer" : "not-allowed",
                opacity: joinCode.trim() ? 1 : 0.4,
              }}
            >
              {isAr ? "دخول" : "Join"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div style={{ padding: 12, background: "var(--accent-soft)", color: "var(--accent)", borderRadius: 10, marginBottom: 16, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600 }}>
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* MY ROOMS */}
        <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 16, padding: 24 }}>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", fontWeight: 700, margin: "0 0 12px", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
            {isAr ? "غرفي" : "My rooms"}
          </h2>
          {loading ? (
            <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", margin: 0 }}>{isAr ? "جارٍ التحميل..." : "Loading..."}</p>
          ) : rooms.length === 0 ? (
            <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", margin: 0, fontSize: 14 }}>{isAr ? "لا توجد غرف بعد" : "No rooms yet"}</p>
          ) : (
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 8 }}>
              {rooms.map((room) => (
                <Link
                  key={room.id}
                  href={`/games/chess/${room.code}`}
                  style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", padding: 12, background: "var(--bg)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, textDecoration: "none", color: "inherit" }}
                >
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2 }}>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 2, fontFamily: "monospace", color: "var(--ink)" }}>{room.code}</span>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                      {room.status === "waiting" ? (isAr ? "⏳ في الانتظار" : "⏳ Waiting") : room.status === "active" ? (isAr ? "🎮 نشطة" : "🎮 Active") : (isAr ? "✅ انتهت" : "✅ Finished")}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 20 }}>→</span>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/games/guess/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useGameStats } from "@/hooks/useGameStats";

type Ingredient = { name: string };
type Recipe = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  imageUrl: string | null;
  ingredients: Ingredient[];
};

const DECOY_POOL = [
  "sugar", "flour", "baking powder", "vanilla extract", "honey", "soy sauce", "mayonnaise",
  "mustard", "ketchup", "paprika", "cumin", "coriander", "cinnamon", "nutmeg", "chili powder",
  "beef broth", "chicken broth", "red wine", "balsamic vinegar", "worcestershire", "tabasco",
  "cornstarch", "breadcrumbs", "almonds", "walnuts", "pecans", "raisins", "coconut", "spinach",
  "mushrooms", "zucchini", "eggplant", "cauliflower", "peas", "corn", "potato", "sweet potato",
  "bacon", "ham", "sausage", "tofu", "lentils", "chickpeas", "black beans", "quinoa",
  "feta", "cheddar", "brie", "blue cheese", "cream cheese", "sour cream", "pesto",
  "hot sauce", "maple syrup", "molasses", "cocoa powder", "dark chocolate", "white chocolate",
  "peanut butter", "jam", "capers", "pickles", "artichoke", "asparagus", "beets",
  "radish", "celery", "cabbage", "kale", "arugula", "bean sprouts", "water", "salt", "pepper", "oil"
];

function getEmoji(name: string) {
  const n = name.toLowerCase();
  if (n.includes("pepper") || n.includes("chili")) return "🌶️";
  if (n.includes("tomato")) return "🍅";
  if (n.includes("onion")) return "🧅";
  if (n.includes("garlic")) return "🧄";
  if (n.includes("meat") || n.includes("beef") || n.includes("lamb") || n.includes("steak")) return "🥩";
  if (n.includes("chicken")) return "🍗";
  if (n.includes("fish") || n.includes("shrimp") || n.includes("seafood")) return "🐟";
  if (n.includes("cheese") || n.includes("parmesan") || n.includes("mozzarella")) return "🧀";
  if (n.includes("egg")) return "🥚";
  if (n.includes("milk") || n.includes("cream") || n.includes("yogurt")) return "🥛";
  if (n.includes("flour") || n.includes("bread") || n.includes("dough")) return "🌾";
  if (n.includes("rice")) return "🍚";
  if (n.includes("oil") || n.includes("butter")) return "🫒";
  if (n.includes("water")) return "💧";
  if (n.includes("lemon") || n.includes("lime")) return "🍋";
  if (n.includes("potato")) return "🥔";
  if (n.includes("carrot")) return "🥕";
  if (n.includes("herb") || n.includes("parsley") || n.includes("cilantro") || n.includes("mint")) return "🌿";
  if (n.includes("spice") || n.includes("cumin") || n.includes("cinnamon") || n.includes("powder")) return "🥣";
  if (n.includes("sugar") || n.includes("honey") || n.includes("syrup")) return "🍯";
  if (n.includes("nut") || n.includes("almond") || n.includes("pine")) return "🥜";
  return "🥄";
}

function shuffle(arr: string[]) {
  const newArr = [...arr];
  for (let i = newArr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [newArr[i], newArr[j]] = [newArr[j], newArr[i]];
  }
  return newArr;
}

export default function GamePage() {
  const { stats, saveStats } = useGameStats("guess", { guessed: 0, played: 0 });

  const [recipe, setRecipe] = useState<Recipe | null>(null);
  const [correctSet, setCorrectSet] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [cards, setCards] = useState<{ name: string; eliminated: boolean; correct: boolean; wrong: boolean }[]>([]);
  const [finished, setFinished] = useState(false);
  const [won, setWon] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [msgType, setMsgType] = useState<"win" | "fail" | "info">("info");
  const [lives, setLives] = useState(5);
  const [hints, setHints] = useState(5);
  const [isRevealed, setIsRevealed] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showRules, setShowRules] = useState(false);

  async function startGame() {
    setLoading(true);
    setFinished(false);
    setWon(false);
    setMessage("");
    setMsgType("info");
    setLives(5);
    setHints(5);
    setIsRevealed(false);

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/recipes");
      const data = await res.json();
      const validRecipes = data.filter((r: any) => r.ingredients && r.ingredients.length >= 3);

      if (validRecipes.length === 0) {
        setMessage("No recipes with ingredients found.");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const randomRecipe = validRecipes[Math.floor(Math.random() * validRecipes.length)];
      setRecipe(randomRecipe);

      const correct = randomRecipe.ingredients.map((i: any) => i.name.toLowerCase().trim());
      setCorrectSet(new Set(correct));

      const decoys = DECOY_POOL.filter(d => !correct.includes(d));
      const targetTotal = Math.min(18, Math.max(12, correct.length + 6));
      const needDecoys = Math.max(5, targetTotal - correct.length);
      const selectedDecoys = shuffle(decoys).slice(0, needDecoys);

      const allIngredients = Array.from(new Set([...correct, ...selectedDecoys]));
      const shuffled = shuffle(allIngredients);

      setCards(shuffled.map(name => ({
        name,
        eliminated: false,
        correct: false,
        wrong: false
      })));
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage("Failed to load recipes.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    startGame();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (finished) {
      saveStats((prev) => ({
        guessed: (prev.guessed || 0) + (won ? 1 : 0),
        played: (prev.played || 0) + 1
      }));
    }
  }, [finished]);

  function handleCardClick(index: number) {
    if (finished) return;
    const card = cards[index];
    if (card.correct || card.wrong || card.eliminated) return;

    if (correctSet.has(card.name)) {
      const nextCards = cards.map((c, i) => i === index ? { ...c, correct: true } : c);
      setCards(nextCards);
      const remainingCorrect = nextCards.filter(c => correctSet.has(c.name) && !c.correct).length;

      if (remainingCorrect === 0 && !isRevealed) {
        setFinished(true); setWon(true); setMessage("You guessed all the ingredients!"); setMsgType("win");
        setCards(nextCards.map(c => !correctSet.has(c.name) ? { ...c, eliminated: true } : c));
      } else {
        setMessage("Correct. Keep going!"); setMsgType("win");
      }
    } else {
      const newLives = lives - 1;
      setLives(newLives);
      const nextCards = cards.map((c, i) => i === index ? { ...c, wrong: true } : c);

      if (newLives <= 0) {
        setFinished(true); setMessage("You lost all 5 lives! Game over."); setMsgType("fail");
        setCards(nextCards.map(c => correctSet.has(c.name) ? { ...c, correct: true } : c));
      } else {
        setCards(nextCards); setMessage("That was a real ingredient! You lost 1 life."); setMsgType("fail");
      }
    }
  }

  function useHint() {
    if (finished || hints <= 0) return;
    const hiddenCorrectIndices = cards.map((c, i) => ({ c, i })).filter(({ c }) => correctSet.has(c.name) && !c.correct && !c.eliminated).map(({ i }) => i);

    if (hiddenCorrectIndices.length === 0) {
      setMessage("No more hidden correct ingredients to reveal!"); setMsgType("info"); return;
    }

    const randomIndex = hiddenCorrectIndices[Math.floor(Math.random() * hiddenCorrectIndices.length)];
    setHints(h => h - 1);
    const nextCards = cards.map((c, i) => i === randomIndex ? { ...c, correct: true, wrong: false } : c);
    setCards(nextCards);

    const remainingCorrect = nextCards.filter(c => correctSet.has(c.name) && !c.correct).length;
    if (remainingCorrect === 0 && !isRevealed) {
      setFinished(true); setWon(true); setMessage("You guessed all the ingredients!"); setMsgType("win");
      setCards(nextCards.map(c => !correctSet.has(c.name) ? { ...c, eliminated: true } : c));
    } else {
      setMessage(""); setMsgType("info");
    }
  }

  function imDone() {
    if (finished) return;
    setIsRevealed(true); setFinished(true);
    const nextCards = cards.map(card => {
      if (correctSet.has(card.name)) return { ...card, correct: true, eliminated: false, wrong: false };
      return { ...card, eliminated: true, wrong: false };
    });
    setCards(nextCards); setWon(false); setMessage("Game ended. The correct ingredients are revealed."); setMsgType("info");
  }

  const remainingCorrect = cards.filter(c => correctSet.has(c.name) && !c.correct && !c.wrong).length;

  return (
    <div style={{ minHeight: "100vh", background: "#f6f4ef", fontFamily: "system-ui, sans-serif", paddingBottom: 40 }}>
      
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 800, margin: "0 auto", padding: "40px 16px" }}>
        
        {/* HEADER SECTION WITH EXIT, TITLE, AND ALL 3 BUTTONS */}
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 24, position: "relative" }}>
          
          {/* Exit button moved to top right, padding to avoid overlap */}
          <Link href="/games" style={{ position: "absolute", top: -10, right: 0, background: "white", color: "#1a1a1a", padding: "6px 12px", borderRadius: 8, textDecoration: "none", fontWeight: 700, border: "1px solid #e0e0e0", fontSize: 13 }}>
            ✕ Exit
          </Link>

          <h1 style={{ fontSize: "2.5rem", fontWeight: 800, margin: "0 0 16px 0", color: "#1a1a1a", textAlign: "center" }}>
            Guess the <span style={{ color: "#d4af37" }}>Ingredients</span>
          </h1>
          
          {/* Single line buttons with nowrap */}
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "center", gap: 8, flexWrap: "nowrap" }}>
            <button onClick={startGame} style={{ background: "#1a1a1a", color: "white", padding: "8px 16px", borderRadius: 30, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, border: "2px solid #d4af37", cursor: "pointer", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
              ▶ New Game
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => setShowRules(true)} style={{ background: "white", color: "#1a1a1a", padding: "8px 16px", borderRadius: 30, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, border: "2px solid #e0e0e0", cursor: "pointer", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
              Rules
            </button>
            <Link href="/games/leaderboard" style={{ background: "white", color: "#1a1a1a", padding: "8px 16px", borderRadius: 30, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, border: "2px solid #e0e0e0", textDecoration: "none", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
              🏆 Leaderboard
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* STATS BAR */}
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", background: "white", borderRadius: 16, padding: "12px 24px", marginBottom: 24, boxShadow: "0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)", border: "1px solid #e0e0e0" }}>
          
          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 28, color: "#d4af37" }}>★</span>
            <div>
              <div style={{ color: "#888", fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 1 }}>SCORE</div>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: "#1a1a1a", lineHeight: 1 }}>{remainingCorrect}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ width: 1, background: "#e0e0e0" }} />

          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 28, color: "#ff4b4b" }}>♥</span>
            <div>
              <div style={{ color: "#888", fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 1 }}>LIVES</div>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: "#ff4b4b", lineHeight: 1 }}>{lives}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ width: 1, background: "#e0e0e0" }} />

          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 28, color: "#2e7d32" }}>✓</span>
            <div>
              <div style={{ color: "#888", fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 1 }}>WINS</div>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: "#2e7d32", lineHeight: 1 }}>{stats.guessed || 0} / {stats.played || 0}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* RECIPE CARD (Text moved below image) */}
        <div style={{ background: "white", borderRadius: 24, overflow: "hidden", boxShadow: "0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)", marginBottom: 20 }}>
          
          <div style={{ height: 220, position: "relative" }}>
            {recipe?.imageUrl ? (
              <img src={recipe.imageUrl} alt={recipe.name} style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%", objectFit: "cover" }} />
            ) : (
              <div style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", fontSize: "56px", background: "#f0f0f0" }}>🍽️</div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: "16px 20px" }}>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: "1.6rem", fontWeight: 800, margin: "0 0 12px 0", color: "#1a1a1a" }}>
              {loading ? "Loading..." : recipe?.name || "Unknown Recipe"}
            </h2>
            
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
              <span style={{ background: "#f5f5f5", border: "1px solid #e0e0e0", padding: "4px 12px", borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "#666" }}>
                {recipe?.ingredients?.length || 0} ingredients
              </span>
              <button onClick={finished ? startGame : imDone} style={{ background: "#e8f5e9", color: "#2e7d32", border: "1px solid #c8e6c9", padding: "4px 12px", borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, cursor: "pointer" }}>
                {finished ? "Play Again" : "Reveal"}
              </button>
              <button onClick={useHint} disabled={finished || hints === 0} style={{ background: "#f5f5f5", color: "#666", border: "1px solid #e0e0e0", padding: "4px 12px", borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, cursor: (finished || hints === 0) ? "not-allowed" : "pointer", opacity: (finished || hints === 0) ? 0.5 : 1 }}>
                💡 Hint ({hints})
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* INGREDIENTS GRID */}
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(auto-fill, minmax(100px, 1fr))", gap: 10 }}>
          {cards.map((card, i) => {
            let bg = "white";
            let color = "#1a1a1a";
            let border = "1px solid #e0e0e0";

            if (card.wrong) { bg = "#ffebee"; border = "1px solid #ffcdd2"; color = "#c62828"; }
            if (card.eliminated) { bg = "#f5f5f5"; color = "#bdbdbd"; border = "1px dashed #e0e0e0"; }
            if (card.correct) { bg = "#e8f5e9"; border = "1px solid #c8e6c9"; color = "#2e7d32"; }

            return (
              <div key={i} onClick={() => handleCardClick(i)} style={{ background: bg, color, border, borderRadius: 12, padding: "12px 6px", textAlign: "center", fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, cursor: (card.eliminated || card.correct || card.wrong) ? "default" : "pointer", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", gap: 6, minHeight: 90, opacity: card.eliminated ? 0.6 : 1, userSelect: "none", transition: "all 0.2s" }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 24, filter: card.eliminated ? "grayscale(1)" : "none" }}>{getEmoji(card.name)}</span>
                <span style={{ wordBreak: "break-word", lineHeight: 1.1 }}>{card.name}</span>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* WIN MODAL */}
      {won && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", zIndex: 100, padding: 20 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "white", borderRadius: 24, padding: "40px", textAlign: "center", maxWidth: 400, width: "100%", boxShadow: "0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)" }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: "64px", marginBottom: "16px" }}>🎉</div>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: "#1a1a1a", margin: "0 0 12px" }}>Perfect!</h2>
            <p style={{ color: "#666", fontSize: 16, marginBottom: 32, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>You successfully guessed all the ingredients for <strong style={{ color: "#1a1a1a" }}>{recipe?.name}</strong>.</p>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12 }}>
              <button onClick={() => setWon(false)} style={{ flex: 1, background: "#f5f5f5", color: "#1a1a1a", border: "none", padding: "14px", borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>Cancel</button>
              <button onClick={startGame} style={{ flex: 1, background: "#2e7d32", color: "white", border: "none", padding: "14px", borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>Play Again</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* RULES MODAL */}
      {showRules && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 100 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "white", padding: 32, borderRadius: 24, maxWidth: 400, width: "100%", boxShadow: "0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)" }}>
            <h2 style={{ marginTop: 0, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 800 }}>Game Rules</h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 16, lineHeight: 1.6, color: "#666", marginBottom: 24 }}>
              Click the ingredients you think belong to the recipe. Wrong picks cost a life. You have 5 lives and 5 hints.
            </p>
            <button onClick={() => setShowRules(false)} style={{ width: "100%", background: "#1a1a1a", color: "white", border: "none", padding: "14px", borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>Got it</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}
