--- FILE: src/app/games/leaderboard/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";

const GAMES = [
  { id: "catch", name: "Catch", label: "BEST SCORE" },
  { id: "memory", name: "Memory", label: "BEST TIME" },
  { id: "wordle", name: "Wordle", label: "WINS" },
  { id: "guess", name: "Guess", label: "WINS" },
  { id: "puzzle", name: "Puzzle", label: "SOLVED" },
  { id: "snake", name: "Snake", label: "BEST SCORE" },
];

export default function LeaderboardPage() {
  const [activeGame, setActiveGame] = useState(GAMES[0]);
  const [data, setData] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/leaderboard?game=${activeGame.id}`)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((d) => {
        setData(d.leaderboard || []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [activeGame]);

  return (
    <div className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 800 }}>
      <Link href="/games" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
        ← Games
      </Link>

      <h1 style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-head)", fontSize: "2.5rem", marginBottom: 8 }}>
        🏆 Leaderboard
      </h1>
      <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 32 }}>
        Top players across all games
      </p>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, overflowX: "auto", paddingBottom: 16, marginBottom: 16 }}>
        {GAMES.map((g) => (
          <button
            key={g.id}
            onClick={() => setActiveGame(g)}
            className={`btn ${activeGame.id === g.id ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`}
            style={{ whiteSpace: "nowrap", padding: "10px 16px" }}
          >
            {g.name}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Table */}
      <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 16, overflow: "hidden" }}>
        {loading ? (
          <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: "center", color: "var(--muted)" }}>Loading...</div>
        ) : data.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: "center", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
            No scores yet. Be the first!
          </div>
        ) : (
          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", textAlign: "left" }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: "var(--bg)", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                <th style={{ padding: "16px", fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--muted)", width: 60 }}>#</th>
                <th style={{ padding: "16px", fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--muted)" }}>Player</th>
                <th style={{ padding: "16px", fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--muted)", textAlign: "right" }}>
                  {activeGame.label}
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {data.map((row, i) => {
                const val = row.stats[activeGame.id === 'catch' ? 'bestCatched' : activeGame.id === 'memory' ? 'bestTime' : activeGame.id === 'wordle' ? 'wins' : activeGame.id === 'guess' ? 'guessed' : activeGame.id === 'puzzle' ? 'solved' : 'bestScore'];
                const displayVal = activeGame.id === "memory" ? `${val}s` : val;
                
                return (
                  <tr key={i} style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                    <td style={{ padding: "16px", fontWeight: 700, color: i === 0 ? "#ffd700" : i === 1 ? "#c0c0c0" : i === 2 ? "#cd7f32" : "var(--muted)" }}>
                      {i + 1}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: "16px", fontWeight: 500, color: "var(--ink)" }}>
                      {row.phone.slice(0, 4)}***{row.phone.slice(-3)}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: "16px", fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--olive)", textAlign: "right" }}>
                      {displayVal || "-"}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/games/leaderboard/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/games/memory/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import GameHeader from "@/components/GameHeader";
import { useGameStats } from "@/hooks/useGameStats";

type Card = { id: string; matchId: number; text: string; type: "recipe" | "ingredient" | "bonus"; };
type Recipe = { id: number; name: string; nameAr: string | null; ingredients: { name: string; nameAr: string | null }[]; };

export default function MemoryGamePage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const { stats, saveStats } = useGameStats("memory", { wins: 0, bestTime: 0, played: 0 });

  const [cards, setCards] = useState<Card[]>([]);
  const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [matched, setMatched] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [time, setTime] = useState(0);
  const [isLocked, setIsLocked] = useState(false);
  const [isWon, setIsWon] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showRules, setShowRules] = useState(false);

  async function startGame() {
    setLoading(true);
    setIsWon(false);
    setFlipped([]);
    setMatched([]);
    setTime(0);
    setIsLocked(false);
    setCards([]);
    setMatched([]);
    setFlipped([]); // Clear cards immediately

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/recipes");
      const data = await res.json();
      const validRecipes = (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []).filter((r: Recipe) => r.ingredients && r.ingredients.length > 0);

      if (validRecipes.length < 7) { setLoading(false); return; }
      const shuffledRecipes = validRecipes.sort(() => 0.5 - Math.random()).slice(0, 7);

      let newCards: Card[] = [];
      shuffledRecipes.forEach((r: Recipe, index: number) => {
        const recipeName = isAr ? (r.nameAr || r.name) : r.name;
        const ingName = isAr ? (r.ingredients[0].nameAr || r.ingredients[0].name) : r.ingredients[0].name;
        newCards.push({ id: `r-${index}`, matchId: index, text: recipeName, type: "recipe" });
        newCards.push({ id: `i-${index}`, matchId: index, text: ingName, type: "ingredient" });
      });
      newCards.push({ id: "bonus", matchId: 999, text: isAr ? ar.memory.bonus : "⭐ BONUS", type: "bonus" });
      newCards = newCards.sort(() => 0.5 - Math.random());
      
      setCards(newCards);
      setLoading(false);
    } catch (err) { setLoading(false); }
  }

  useEffect(() => { startGame(); }, [lang]);

  // Timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (loading || isWon || showRules || matched.length === 0) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => setTime((t) => t + 1), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [loading, isWon, showRules, matched.length]);

  // Save Stats
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isWon) {
      saveStats((prev) => ({
        wins: (prev.wins || 0) + 1,
        bestTime: prev.bestTime === 0 ? Math.max(1, time) : Math.min(prev.bestTime, Math.max(1, time)),
        played: (prev.played || 0) + 1
      }));
    }
  }, [isWon]);

  function handleCardClick(index: number) {
    if (isLocked || isWon) return;
    if (flipped.includes(index) || matched.includes(index)) return;

    const card = cards[index];
    const newFlipped = [...flipped, index];
    setFlipped(newFlipped);

    if (card.type === "bonus") {
      setIsLocked(true);
      const allIndices = cards.map((_, i) => i);
      setMatched(allIndices);
      setFlipped([]);
      setTimeout(() => { 
        setIsWon(true); 
        setIsLocked(false); 
      }, 600); // Short delay so the player sees the board light up before the win screen
      return;
    }

    if (newFlipped.length === 2) {
      const [first, second] = newFlipped;
      const card1 = cards[first];
      const card2 = cards[second];

      if (card1.matchId === card2.matchId && card1.type !== card2.type) {
        setMatched((prev) => {
          const next = [...prev, first, second];
          if (next.length === 14) {
            setTimeout(() => setIsWon(true), 500);
          }
          return next;
        });
        setFlipped([]);
      } else {
        setIsLocked(true);
        setTimeout(() => { setFlipped([]); setIsLocked(false); }, 800);
      }
    }
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>{isAr ? ar.calendar.loading : "Loading..."}</div>;
  if (cards.length === 0) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>Not enough recipes to play.</div>;

  return (
    <div style={{ height: "100dvh", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", padding: 12, background: "var(--bg)", overflow: "hidden", position: "relative" }}>
      <Link href="/games" style={{ position: "absolute", top: 12, insetInlineEnd: 12, zIndex: 50, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: "none", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
        ✕ {isAr ? "خروج" : "Exit"}
      </Link>

      <div style={{ maxWidth: 600, margin: "0 auto", width: "100%", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12, height: "100%", minHeight: 0 }}>
        <GameHeader
          title="Masterchef Memory"
          titleAr="ذاكرة الشيف"
          onNewGame={startGame}
          onShowRules={() => setShowRules(true)}
          score={stats.wins || 0}
          scoreLabel="WINS"
          extra={`${time}s`}
          extraLabel="TIME"
          best={`${stats.bestTime || 0}s`}
          bestLabel="BEST"
        />

        <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(3, 1fr)", gridTemplateRows: "repeat(5, 1fr)", gap: 8, minHeight: 0 }}>
          {cards.map((card, index) => {
            const isFlipped = flipped.includes(index) || matched.includes(index);
            const isMatched = matched.includes(index);
            const isBonus = card.type === "bonus";

            return (
              <div
                key={card.id}
                onClick={() => handleCardClick(index)}
                style={{
                  background: isMatched ? (isBonus ? "rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.15)" : "rgba(123, 160, 91, 0.15)") : (isFlipped ? "var(--surface-2)" : "var(--surface)"),
                  border: `1px solid ${isMatched ? (isBonus ? "#ffd700" : "var(--olive)") : (isFlipped ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--line)")}`,
                  borderRadius: 8, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", textAlign: "center",
                  fontSize: "0.8rem", fontWeight: 600, padding: 4, cursor: isMatched ? "default" : "pointer",
                  color: isFlipped ? (isMatched ? (isBonus ? "#ffd700" : "var(--olive)") : "var(--accent)") : "transparent",
                  transition: "all 0.2s", userSelect: "none", wordBreak: "break-word", opacity: isMatched ? 0.6 : 1,
                }}
              >
                {isFlipped ? card.text : <span style={{ fontSize: "1.5rem", opacity: 0.3 }}>🍽️</span>}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {showRules && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 100 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", padding: 32, borderRadius: 20, maxWidth: 400, width: "100%" }}>
            <h2 style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>{isAr ? ar.memory.rulesTitle : "Game Rules"}</h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: "0.9rem", lineHeight: 1.5, color: "var(--ink-soft)" }} dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: isAr ? ar.memory.rulesText : "<strong>Goal:</strong> Match every Recipe with its main Ingredient.<br><br><strong>How to play:</strong> Click on two cards. If they belong together, they turn green. If not, they flip back.<br><br><strong>⭐ Bonus Card:</strong> Find the golden Star to reveal the whole board for 5 seconds!" }} />
            <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => setShowRules(false)} style={{ width: "100%", marginTop: 16 }}>{isAr ? ar.memory.gotIt : "Got it"}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {isWon && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 100 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", padding: 32, borderRadius: 20, maxWidth: 400, width: "100%", textAlign: "center" }}>
            <h2 style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-head)", fontSize: "2rem", margin: "0 0 8px 0" }}>{isAr ? ar.memory.winTitle : "🎉 You Win!"}</h2>
            <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 24 }}>{isAr ? ar.memory.winMsg : "You matched all the dishes."}</p>
            <div style={{ fontSize: "2rem", fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--accent)", marginBottom: 24 }}>{time}s</div>
            <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={startGame} style={{ width: "100%" }}>{isAr ? ar.memory.playAgain : "Play Again"}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/games/puzzle/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import GameHeader from "@/components/GameHeader";
import { useGameStats } from "@/hooks/useGameStats";

type Recipe = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  imageUrl: string | null;
};

function shuffle(array: number[]) {
  const newArr = [...array];
  for (let i = newArr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [newArr[i], newArr[j]] = [newArr[j], newArr[i]];
  }
  return newArr;
}

export default function PuzzlePage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const { stats, saveStats } = useGameStats("puzzle", { solved: 0, played: 0 });

  const [recipe, setRecipe] = useState<Recipe | null>(null);
  const [pieces, setPieces] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [draggedIndex, setDraggedIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const [isSolved, setIsSolved] = useState(false);
  const [won, setWon] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [showRules, setShowRules] = useState(false);
  const boardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  async function startGame() {
    setLoading(true);
    setIsSolved(false);
    setWon(false);
    setSelectedIndex(null);
    setMessage("");

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/recipes");
      const data = await res.json();

      const validRecipes = data.filter((r: any) => r.imageUrl);

      if (validRecipes.length === 0) {
        setMessage(isAr ? ar.puzzle.msgNoImages : "No recipe images found.");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const randomRecipe = validRecipes[Math.floor(Math.random() * validRecipes.length)];
      setRecipe(randomRecipe);

      let newPieces = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8];
      do {
        newPieces = shuffle(newPieces);
      } while (newPieces.every((val, index) => val === index));

      setPieces(newPieces);
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage(isAr ? ar.puzzle.msgFailLoad : "Failed to load.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    startGame();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSolved) {
      saveStats((prev) => ({
        solved: (prev.solved || 0) + (won ? 1 : 0),
        played: (prev.played || 0) + 1
      }));
    }
  }, [isSolved]);

  function playSound(type: "swap" | "win") {
    try {
      const audio = new Audio();
      if (type === "swap") {
        audio.src = "https://actions.google.com/sounds/v1/cartoon/pop.ogg";
      } else if (type === "win") {
        audio.src = "https://actions.google.com/sounds/v1/cartoon/magic_chime.ogg";
      }
      audio.volume = 0.5;
      audio.play().catch(() => {});
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  function handlePieceClick(index: number) {
    if (isSolved) return;

    if (selectedIndex === null) {
      setSelectedIndex(index);
    } else if (selectedIndex === index) {
      setSelectedIndex(null);
    } else {
      const newPieces = [...pieces];
      const temp = newPieces[selectedIndex];
      newPieces[selectedIndex] = newPieces[index];
      newPieces[index] = temp;
      setPieces(newPieces);
      setSelectedIndex(null);
      playSound("swap");

      if (newPieces.every((val, i) => val === i)) {
        setIsSolved(true);
        setWon(true);
        playSound("win");
        setMessage(isAr ? ar.puzzle.msgPerfect : "Perfect! You solved the puzzle.");
      }
    }
  }

  function revealSolution() {
    if (isSolved) return;
    setIsSolved(true);
    setWon(false);
    setPieces([0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8]);
    setMessage(isAr ? ar.puzzle.msgRevealed : "Solution revealed.");
  }

  function handleDragStart(e: React.PointerEvent, index: number) {
    if (isSolved) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    setDraggedIndex(index);
    setIsDragging(true);
    (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  }

  function handleDragMove(e: React.PointerEvent) {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    e.preventDefault();
  }

  function handleDragEnd(e: React.PointerEvent) {
    if (!isDragging || draggedIndex === null) return;
    setIsDragging(false);
    (e.target as HTMLElement).releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);

    if (!boardRef.current) return;
    const boardRect = boardRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();

    if (
      e.clientX >= boardRect.left && e.clientX <= boardRect.right &&
      e.clientY >= boardRect.top && e.clientY <= boardRect.bottom
    ) {
      const col = Math.floor((e.clientX - boardRect.left) / (boardRect.width / 3));
      const row = Math.floor((e.clientY - boardRect.top) / (boardRect.height / 3));
      const dropIndex = row * 3 + col;

      if (dropIndex >= 0 && dropIndex < 9 && dropIndex !== draggedIndex) {
        const newPieces = [...pieces];
        const temp = newPieces[draggedIndex];
        newPieces[draggedIndex] = newPieces[dropIndex];
        newPieces[dropIndex] = temp;
        setPieces(newPieces);

        if (newPieces.every((val, i) => val === i)) {
          setIsSolved(true);
          setWon(true);
          setMessage(isAr ? ar.puzzle.msgPerfect : "Perfect! You solved the puzzle.");
        }
      }
    }
    setDraggedIndex(null);
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ height: "100dvh", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", padding: 12, background: "var(--bg)", overflow: "hidden", position: "relative", direction: isAr ? "rtl" : "ltr" }}>
      <Link href="/games" style={{ position: "absolute", top: 12, insetInlineEnd: 12, zIndex: 50, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: "none", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
        ✕ {isAr ? "خروج" : "Exit"}
      </Link>

      <div style={{ maxWidth: 600, margin: "0 auto", width: "100%", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12, height: "100%", minHeight: 0 }}>
        <GameHeader
          title="Recipe Puzzle"
          titleAr="لغز الوصفة"
          onNewGame={startGame}
          onShowRules={() => setShowRules(true)}
          best={`${stats.solved || 0} / ${stats.played || 0}`}
        />

        <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: "auto", minHeight: 0, paddingBottom: 60 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "16px", padding: "16px", position: "relative", boxShadow: "0 10px 30px -10px rgba(0,0,0,0.8)" }}>
            <button
              onClick={isSolved ? startGame : revealSolution}
              style={{ position: "absolute", top: "16px", insetInlineEnd: "16px", background: isSolved ? "var(--olive)" : "var(--surface-2)", color: isSolved ? "#ffffff" : "var(--accent)", border: `1px solid ${isSolved ? "var(--olive)" : "var(--accent)"}`, padding: "6px 12px", borderRadius: "8px", fontSize: "12px", fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer", zIndex: 10 }}
            >
              {isSolved ? (isAr ? ar.puzzle.newGameBtn : "New Game") : (isAr ? ar.puzzle.revealBtn : "Reveal")}
            </button>

            {loading ? (
              <div style={{ height: 300, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}>Loading...</div>
            ) : (
              <div ref={boardRef} style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(3, 1fr)", gap: "4px", width: "100%", aspectRatio: "1 / 1", background: "var(--bg)", borderRadius: "12px", padding: "4px", marginTop: "2rem", direction: "ltr", touchAction: "none", userSelect: "none" }}>
                {pieces.map((pieceId, index) => {
                  const x = (pieceId % 3) * 50;
                  const y = Math.floor(pieceId / 3) * 50;
                  const isSelected = selectedIndex === index;

                  return (
                    <div
                      key={index}
                      onPointerDown={(e) => handleDragStart(e, index)}
                      onPointerMove={handleDragMove}
                      onPointerUp={handleDragEnd}
                      onClick={() => { if (!isDragging) handlePieceClick(index); }}
                      style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${recipe?.imageUrl})`, backgroundSize: "300% 300%", backgroundPosition: `${x}% ${y}%`, borderRadius: "8px", cursor: isSolved ? "default" : "grab", boxShadow: draggedIndex === index ? "inset 0 0 0 3px var(--accent)" : isSelected ? "inset 0 0 0 3px var(--accent)" : "inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.05)", opacity: draggedIndex === index ? 0.3 : isSelected ? 0.8 : 1, transition: isDragging ? "none" : "all 0.15s", touchAction: "none" }}
                    />
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}

            {isSolved && recipe && (
              <div style={{ marginTop: "1.5rem", textAlign: "center" }}>
                <Link href={`/recipes/${recipe.id}`} style={{ color: "var(--accent)", fontSize: "16px", fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: "none" }}>
                  {isAr ? recipe.nameAr || recipe.name : recipe.name}
                </Link>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {message && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", bottom: 16, left: 16, right: 16, textAlign: "center", fontSize: "13px", fontWeight: 500, color: isSolved ? "var(--olive)" : "var(--muted)", pointerEvents: "none" }}>
          {isSolved ? "✅" : "ℹ️"} {message}
        </div>
      )}

      {won && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.8)", backdropFilter: "blur(8px)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", zIndex: 100, padding: "20px" }}>
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "20px", padding: "40px", textAlign: "center", maxWidth: "400px", width: "100%" }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: "64px", marginBottom: "16px" }}>🎉</div>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: "24px", fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--ink)", margin: "0 0 8px" }}>{isAr ? ar.puzzle.modalPerfect : "Perfect!"}</h2>
            <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: "15px", marginBottom: "24px", lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
              {isAr ? ar.puzzle.modalText : "You successfully solved the puzzle for "}
              <strong style={{ color: "var(--ink)" }}>{recipe ? (isAr ? recipe.nameAr || recipe.name : recipe.name) : ""}</strong>.
            </p>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "10px" }}>
              <button onClick={() => setWon(false)} style={{ flex: 1, background: "var(--surface-2)", color: "var(--ink)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", padding: "12px", borderRadius: "10px", fontSize: "15px", fontWeight: 600, cursor: "pointer" }}>{isAr ? ar.puzzle.modalClose : "Close"}</button>
              <button onClick={startGame} style={{ flex: 1, background: "var(--accent)", color: "#ffffff", border: "none", padding: "12px", borderRadius: "10px", fontSize: "15px", fontWeight: 600, cursor: "pointer" }}>{isAr ? ar.puzzle.newGameBtn : "New Game"}</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {showRules && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 100 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", padding: 32, borderRadius: 20, maxWidth: 400, width: "100%" }}>
            <h2 style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>{isAr ? "قواعد اللعبة" : "Game Rules"}</h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: "0.9rem", lineHeight: 1.5, color: "var(--ink-soft)" }}>
              {isAr ? "اسحب القطع أو اضغط عليها لتبديل أماكنها. رتب الصورة كما كانت لتكسب." : "Drag or tap pieces to swap them. Arrange the image back to its original order to win."}
            </p>
            <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => setShowRules(false)} style={{ width: "100%", marginTop: 16 }}>{isAr ? "فهمت" : "Got it"}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/games/puzzle/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/games/snake/page.tsx ---
"use client";
import Link from "next/link";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import GameHeader from "@/components/GameHeader";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";

const GRID_SIZE = 20;
const FOOD_EMOJIS = ["🥔", "🧅", "🍅", "🥕", "🥦", "🌽", "🍄", "🫑"];

type Point = { x: number; y: number };

export default function SnakeGamePage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);

  const [score, setScore] = useState(0);
  const [best, setBest] = useState(0);
  const [gameOver, setGameOver] = useState(false);
  const [showRules, setShowRules] = useState(false);

  const snakeRef = useRef<Point[]>([]);
  const dirRef = useRef<Point>({ x: 0, y: -1 });
  const nextDirRef = useRef<Point>({ x: 0, y: -1 });
  const foodRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; emoji: string }>({ x: 0, y: 0, emoji: "🍅" });
  const loopRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
  const touchStartRef = useRef<Point>({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const isRunningRef = useRef(false);
  const scoreRef = useRef(0);
  const bestRef = useRef(0);

  const draw = useCallback(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return;

    const tileSize = canvas.width / GRID_SIZE;

    ctx.fillStyle = "#16161a";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.03)";
    for (let i = 0; i <= GRID_SIZE; i++) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(i * tileSize, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(i * tileSize, canvas.height);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, i * tileSize);
      ctx.lineTo(canvas.width, i * tileSize);
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    ctx.font = `${tileSize - 4}px Arial`;
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText(
      foodRef.current.emoji,
      foodRef.current.x * tileSize + tileSize / 2,
      foodRef.current.y * tileSize + tileSize / 2
    );

    snakeRef.current.forEach((seg, i) => {
      if (i === 0) {
        ctx.font = `${tileSize}px Arial`;
        ctx.fillText("🍴", seg.x * tileSize + tileSize / 2, seg.y * tileSize + tileSize / 2);
      } else {
        ctx.fillStyle = "#7ba05b";
        const x = seg.x * tileSize + 2;
        const y = seg.y * tileSize + 2;
        const w = tileSize - 4;
        const h = tileSize - 4;
        const r = Math.min(4, w / 2, h / 2);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
        ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
        ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
        ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
        ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const spawnFood = useCallback(() => {
    let newFood: { x: number; y: number; emoji: string } = { x: 0, y: 0, emoji: "🍅" };
    for (let attempts = 0; attempts < 200; attempts++) {
      newFood = {
        x: Math.floor(Math.random() * GRID_SIZE),
        y: Math.floor(Math.random() * GRID_SIZE),
        emoji: FOOD_EMOJIS[Math.floor(Math.random() * FOOD_EMOJIS.length)],
      };
      const onSnake = snakeRef.current.some((s) => s.x === newFood.x && s.y === newFood.y);
      if (!onSnake) break;
    }
    foodRef.current = newFood;
  }, []);

  const endGame = useCallback(() => {
    isRunningRef.current = false;
    if (loopRef.current) {
      clearInterval(loopRef.current);
      loopRef.current = null;
    }
    setGameOver(true);
    if (scoreRef.current > bestRef.current) {
      bestRef.current = scoreRef.current;
      setBest(scoreRef.current);
      try {
        localStorage.setItem("fork_snake_best", String(scoreRef.current));
      } catch {}
    }
  }, []);

  const gameLoop = useCallback(() => {
    if (!isRunningRef.current) return;

    dirRef.current = nextDirRef.current;
    const head = {
      x: snakeRef.current[0].x + dirRef.current.x,
      y: snakeRef.current[0].y + dirRef.current.y,
    };

    if (head.x < 0 || head.x >= GRID_SIZE || head.y < 0 || head.y >= GRID_SIZE) {
      endGame();
      return;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < snakeRef.current.length - 1; i++) {
      if (head.x === snakeRef.current[i].x && head.y === snakeRef.current[i].y) {
        endGame();
        return;
      }
    }

    snakeRef.current.unshift(head);

    if (head.x === foodRef.current.x && head.y === foodRef.current.y) {
      scoreRef.current += 1;
      setScore(scoreRef.current);
      spawnFood();
    } else {
      snakeRef.current.pop();
    }

    draw();
  }, [endGame, spawnFood, draw]);

  const startGame = useCallback(() => {
    if (loopRef.current) {
      clearInterval(loopRef.current);
      loopRef.current = null;
    }
    setScore(0);
    scoreRef.current = 0;
    setGameOver(false);
    isRunningRef.current = true;

    snakeRef.current = [
      { x: 10, y: 10 },
      { x: 10, y: 11 },
      { x: 10, y: 12 },
    ];
    dirRef.current = { x: 0, y: -1 };
    nextDirRef.current = { x: 0, y: -1 };

    let fx = 5;
    let fy = 5;
    let guard = 0;
    while (snakeRef.current.some((s) => s.x === fx && s.y === fy) && guard++ < 200) {
      fx = Math.floor(Math.random() * GRID_SIZE);
      fy = Math.floor(Math.random() * GRID_SIZE);
    }
    foodRef.current = {
      x: fx,
      y: fy,
      emoji: FOOD_EMOJIS[Math.floor(Math.random() * FOOD_EMOJIS.length)],
    };

    draw();

    loopRef.current = setInterval(gameLoop, 130);
  }, [draw, gameLoop]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let storedBest = 0;
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem("fork_snake_best");
      if (stored) storedBest = Number(stored) || 0;
    } catch {}
    bestRef.current = storedBest;
    setBest(storedBest);
    startGame();

    return () => {
      isRunningRef.current = false;
      if (loopRef.current) clearInterval(loopRef.current);
    };
  }, [startGame]);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (gameOver) return;
      const { x, y } = dirRef.current;
      if (e.key === "ArrowUp" && y === 0) nextDirRef.current = { x: 0, y: -1 };
      if (e.key === "ArrowDown" && y === 0) nextDirRef.current = { x: 0, y: 1 };
      if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" && x === 0) nextDirRef.current = { x: -1, y: 0 };
      if (e.key === "ArrowRight" && x === 0) nextDirRef.current = { x: 1, y: 0 };
    }
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKey);
  }, [gameOver]);

  function onTouchStart(e: React.TouchEvent) {
    touchStartRef.current = { x: e.touches[0].clientX, y: e.touches[0].clientY };
  }

  function onTouchEnd(e: React.TouchEvent) {
    if (gameOver) return;
    const touchEndX = e.changedTouches[0].clientX;
    const touchEndY = e.changedTouches[0].clientY;
    const dx = touchEndX - touchStartRef.current.x;
    const dy = touchEndY - touchStartRef.current.y;
    const { x, y } = dirRef.current;

    if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
      if (dx > 0 && x === 0) nextDirRef.current = { x: 1, y: 0 };
      if (dx < 0 && x === 0) nextDirRef.current = { x: -1, y: 0 };
    } else {
      if (dy > 0 && y === 0) nextDirRef.current = { x: 0, y: 1 };
      if (dy < 0 && y === 0) nextDirRef.current = { x: 0, y: -1 };
    }
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ height: "100dvh", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", padding: 12, background: "var(--bg)", overflow: "hidden", position: "relative" }}>
      <Link href="/games" style={{ position: "absolute", top: 12, insetInlineEnd: 12, zIndex: 50, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: "none", color: "var(--ink)" }}>✕ {isAr ? "خروج" : "Exit"}</Link>

      <div style={{ maxWidth: 600, margin: "0 auto", width: "100%", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12, height: "100%" }}>
        <GameHeader
          title="Fork Snake"
          titleAr="ثعبان الشوكة"
          onNewGame={startGame}
          onShowRules={() => setShowRules(true)}
          score={score}
          best={best}
        />

        <div
          style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", minHeight: 0, position: "relative" }}
          onTouchStart={onTouchStart}
          onTouchEnd={onTouchEnd}
        >
          <canvas
            ref={canvasRef}
            width={400}
            height={400}
            style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "var(--radius)", maxWidth: "100%", maxHeight: "100%", aspectRatio: "1/1", touchAction: "none" }}
          />

          {gameOver && (
            <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.7)", backdropFilter: "blur(4px)", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", zIndex: 10, borderRadius: "var(--radius)" }}>
              <h2 style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-head)", fontSize: "2rem", margin: "0 0 8px 0" }}>
                {isAr ? ar.snake.gameOver : "Game Over"}
              </h2>
              <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 24 }}>
                {isAr ? ar.snake.gameOverMsg : "You hit the wall or your tail!"}
              </p>
              <div style={{ fontSize: "3rem", fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--accent)", marginBottom: 24 }}>{score}</div>
              <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={startGame} style={{ padding: "14px 28px" }}>
                {isAr ? ar.snake.playAgain : "Play Again"}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {showRules && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 100 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", padding: 32, borderRadius: 20, maxWidth: 400, width: "100%" }}>
            <h2 style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>{isAr ? ar.snake.rulesTitle : "Game Rules"}</h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: "0.9rem", lineHeight: 1.5, color: "var(--ink-soft)" }} dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: isAr ? ar.snake.rulesText : "<strong>Goal:</strong> Eat as many ingredients as possible.<br><br><strong>How to play:</strong> Swipe on mobile or use Arrow keys to move.<br><br><strong>⚠️ Warning:</strong> Do not hit the wall or your own tail, or the game is over!" }} />
            <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => setShowRules(false)} style={{ width: "100%", marginTop: 16 }}>
              {isAr ? ar.snake.gotIt : "Got it"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/games/snake/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/games/wordle/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from "react";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import GameHeader from "@/components/GameHeader";
import { useGameStats } from "@/hooks/useGameStats";

const AR_KEYBOARD_ROWS = [
  ["ض", "ص", "ث", "ق", "ف", "غ", "ع", "ه", "خ", "ح", "ج"],
  ["ش", "س", "ي", "ب", "ل", "ا", "ت", "ن", "م", "ك", "د"],
  ["ENTER", "ذ", "ط", "ظ", "ر", "ز", "و", "ة", "ى", "ء", "BACKSPACE"],
];

const KEYBOARD_ROWS = [
  ["Q", "W", "E", "R", "T", "Y", "U", "I", "O", "P"],
  ["A", "S", "D", "F", "G", "H", "J", "K", "L"],
  ["ENTER", "Z", "X", "C", "V", "B", "N", "M", "BACKSPACE"]
];

export default function WordleGamePage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const keyboardRows = isAr ? AR_KEYBOARD_ROWS : KEYBOARD_ROWS;

  const { stats, saveStats } = useGameStats("wordle", { wins: 0, played: 0 });

  const [targetWord, setTargetWord] = useState("");
  const [wordLength, setWordLength] = useState(0);
  const [currentRow, setCurrentRow] = useState(0);
  const [currentGuess, setCurrentGuess] = useState("");
  const [gameOver, setGameOver] = useState(false);
  const [isWon, setIsWon] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showRules, setShowRules] = useState(false);

  const [keyStates, setKeyStates] = useState<Record<string, "correct" | "present" | "absent">>({});
  const [gridData, setGridData] = useState<{ letter: string; state: string }[][]>([]);

  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  async function startGame() {
    setLoading(true);
    setGameOver(false);
    setIsWon(false);
    setCurrentRow(0);
    setCurrentGuess("");
    setKeyStates({});

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/recipes");
      const data = await res.json();

      let validNames = (Array.isArray(data) ? data : [])
        .map((r: any) => {
          const name = isAr ? (r.nameAr || r.name) : r.name;
          return name ? name.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z\u0600-\u06FF]/g, "") : "";
        })
        .filter((name: string) => name.length >= 4 && name.length <= 10);

      if (validNames.length === 0) {
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const randomWord = validNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * validNames.length)];
      setTargetWord(randomWord);
      setWordLength(randomWord.length);

      const emptyGrid = Array(6).fill(null).map(() =>
        Array(randomWord.length).fill(null).map(() => ({ letter: "", state: "" }))
      );
      setGridData(emptyGrid);
      setLoading(false);

      setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 100);
    } catch (err) {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    startGame();
  }, []);

  // Save stats when game over
  useEffect(() => {
    if (gameOver) {
      saveStats((prev) => ({
        wins: (prev.wins || 0) + (isWon ? 1 : 0),
        played: (prev.played || 0) + 1
      }));
    }
  }, [gameOver]);

  function handleKeyPress(key: string) {
    if (gameOver || loading) return;

    if (key === "ENTER") {
      submitGuess();
    } else if (key === "BACKSPACE") {
      setCurrentGuess((prev) => prev.slice(0, -1));
    } else if (currentGuess.length < wordLength) {
      setCurrentGuess((prev) => prev + key);
    }
  }

  function submitGuess() {
    if (currentGuess.length !== wordLength) return;

    const targetChars = targetWord.split("");
    const guessChars = currentGuess.split("");
    const letterCounts: Record<string, number> = {};

    for (const char of targetChars) {
      letterCounts[char] = (letterCounts[char] || 0) + 1;
    }

    const newGrid = [...gridData];
    const newKeyStates = { ...keyStates };
    let allCorrect = true;

    for (let i = 0; i < wordLength; i++) {
      const letter = guessChars[i];
      if (letter === targetChars[i]) {
        newGrid[currentRow][i] = { letter, state: "correct" };
        letterCounts[letter]--;
        newKeyStates[letter] = "correct";
      } else {
        allCorrect = false;
      }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < wordLength; i++) {
      const letter = guessChars[i];
      if (newGrid[currentRow][i].state !== "correct") {
        if (letterCounts[letter] > 0) {
          newGrid[currentRow][i] = { letter, state: "present" };
          letterCounts[letter]--;
          if (newKeyStates[letter] !== "correct") newKeyStates[letter] = "present";
        } else {
          newGrid[currentRow][i] = { letter, state: "absent" };
          if (newKeyStates[letter] !== "correct" && newKeyStates[letter] !== "present") {
            newKeyStates[letter] = "absent";
          }
        }
      }
    }

    setGridData(newGrid);
    setKeyStates(newKeyStates);

    if (allCorrect) {
      setGameOver(true);
      setIsWon(true);
      return;
    }

    if (currentRow === 5) {
      setGameOver(true);
      return;
    }

    setCurrentRow((r) => r + 1);
    setCurrentGuess("");
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    function onKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (showRules) return;
      if (e.key === "Enter") handleKeyPress("ENTER");
      else if (e.key === "Backspace") handleKeyPress("BACKSPACE");
      else {
        const letter = e.key.toUpperCase();
        if (/^[A-Z\u0600-\u06FF]$/.test(letter)) handleKeyPress(letter);
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [currentGuess, gameOver, loading, showRules, wordLength]);

  if (loading) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>{isAr ? "جاري التحميل..." : "Loading..."}</div>;
  if (wordLength === 0) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>{isAr ? "لا توجد وصفات" : "No recipes available."}</div>;

  const activeLetters = currentGuess.split("");

  return (
    <div style={{ height: "100dvh", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", padding: 10, background: "var(--bg)", overflow: "hidden", position: "relative" }}>
      <Link href="/games" style={{ position: "absolute", top: 12, insetInlineEnd: 12, zIndex: 50, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 14px", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, textDecoration: "none", color: "var(--ink)" }}>
        ✕ {isAr ? "خروج" : "Exit"}
      </Link>

      <input ref={inputRef} type="text" style={{ position: "absolute", opacity: 0, height: 0, width: 0 }} autoFocus />

      <div style={{ maxWidth: 600, margin: "0 auto", width: "100%", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", height: "100%", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
        
        <GameHeader
          title={isAr ? "خمن الوصفة" : "Recipe Wordle"}
          titleAr="خمن الوصفة"
          onNewGame={startGame}
          onShowRules={() => setShowRules(true)}
          best={`${stats.wins || 0} / ${stats.played || 0}`}
          bestLabel="WINS"
          bestLabelAr="فوز"
        />

        {/* GRID */}
        <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", minHeight: 0 }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6, width: "100%", maxWidth: 400, height: "100%", maxHeight: 400 }}>
            {gridData.map((row, r) => (
              <div key={r} style={{ flex: 1, display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${wordLength}, 1fr)`, gap: 4 }}>
                {row.map((cell, c) => {
                  let letter = cell.letter;
                  let state = cell.state;

                  if (r === currentRow && !gameOver) {
                    letter = activeLetters[c] || "";
                    state = "";
                  }

                  return (
                    <div key={c} style={{
                      display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center",
                      border: "2px solid",
                      borderColor: state === "correct" ? "var(--olive)" : state === "present" ? "var(--accent)" : state === "absent" ? "var(--line-strong)" : (letter ? "var(--muted)" : "var(--line)"),
                      background: state === "correct" ? "var(--olive)" : state === "present" ? "var(--accent)" : state === "absent" ? "var(--line-strong)" : "transparent",
                      color: state ? "#fff" : "var(--ink)",
                      borderRadius: 6,
                      fontSize: "clamp(1rem, 4vw, 1.5rem)",
                      fontWeight: 700,
                      textTransform: "uppercase",
                      transition: "all 0.2s",
                    }}>
                      {letter}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* KEYBOARD */}
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6, flexShrink: 0, paddingBottom: 16, marginBottom: "env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 10px)" }}>
          {keyboardRows.map((row, i) => (
            <div key={i} style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "center", gap: 4 }}>
              {row.map((key) => {
                const kState = keyStates[key];
                const isWide = key === "ENTER" || key === "BACKSPACE";
                const isDisabled = kState === "absent";

                return (
                  <button
                    key={key}
                    onClick={() => handleKeyPress(key)}
                    disabled={isDisabled}
                    style={{
                      height: 44,
                      flex: isWide ? 1.5 : 1,
                      maxWidth: isWide ? 65 : 45,
                      borderRadius: 4,
                      border: "none",
                      background: kState === "correct" ? "var(--olive)" : kState === "present" ? "var(--accent)" : kState === "absent" ? "var(--line-strong)" : "var(--surface-2)",
                      color: kState === "absent" ? "var(--muted)" : "var(--ink)",
                      fontSize: isWide ? "0.75rem" : "0.9rem",
                      fontWeight: 600,
                      textTransform: "uppercase",
                      cursor: isDisabled ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
                      opacity: isDisabled ? 0.5 : 1,
                      transition: "all 0.1s",
                    }}
                  >
                    {key === "BACKSPACE" ? "⌫" : key}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* RULES MODAL */}
      {showRules && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 100 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", padding: 32, borderRadius: 20, maxWidth: 400, width: "100%" }}>
            <h2 style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>Game Rules</h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: "0.9rem", lineHeight: 1.5, color: "var(--ink-soft)" }} dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: "<strong>Goal:</strong> Guess the Recipe Name in 6 tries.<br><br><strong>Colors:</strong><br>🟩 Green = Correct letter in the correct spot.<br>🟧 Orange = Correct letter but in the wrong spot.<br>⬛ Gray = Letter is not in the word.<br><br><em>The keyboard will disable letters that are not in the word!</em>" }} />
            <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => setShowRules(false)} style={{ width: "100%", marginTop: 16 }}>Got it</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* WIN/LOSS MODAL */}
      {gameOver && (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20, zIndex: 100 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "white", padding: 32, borderRadius: 24, maxWidth: 400, width: "100%", textAlign: "center", boxShadow: "0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)" }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: "64px", marginBottom: "16px" }}>{isWon ? "🎉" : "😢"}</div>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, color: "#1a1a1a", margin: "0 0 12px" }}>
              {isWon ? (isAr ? "!لقد فزت" : "You Win!") : (isAr ? "!انتهت اللعبة" : "Game Over")}
            </h2>
            <p style={{ color: "#666", fontSize: 16, marginBottom: 24 }}>
              {isWon 
                ? (isAr ? "لقد خمنت الوصفة بنجاح" : "You guessed the recipe!") 
                : (isAr ? `الوصفة كانت: ${targetWord}` : `The recipe was: ${targetWord}`)}
            </p>
            <button onClick={startGame} style={{ width: "100%", background: "#2e7d32", color: "white", border: "none", padding: "14px", borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>
              {isAr ? "العب مجدداً" : "Play Again"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/games/wordle/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/hidden-recipes/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { cuisineFlagUrl } from "@/lib/cuisine-flag";
import { CATEGORIES } from "@/lib/categories";

type Recipe = {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    nameAr: string | null;
    category: string | null;
    categoryAr: string | null;
    cuisine: string | null;
    cuisineAr: string | null;
    isHidden: number;
};

export default function HiddenRecipesPage() {
    const { lang } = useLang();
    const isAr = lang === "ar";
    const router = useRouter();
    const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState<Recipe[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
    const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState("");

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch("/api/auth/me")
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (!data.admin) router.push("/login");
                else loadRecipes();
            });
    }, [router]);

    function loadRecipes() {
        fetch("/api/recipes", { cache: "no-store" })
            .then(r => r.json())
            .then(data => {
                setRecipes(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
                setLoading(false);
            })
            .catch(() => setLoading(false));
    }

    async function toggleHidden(recipe: Recipe) {
        const newState = recipe.isHidden === 1 ? 0 : 1;
        await fetch(`/api/recipes/${recipe.id}`, {
            method: "PUT",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ ...recipe, isHidden: newState }),
        });
        setRecipes(prev => prev.map(r => r.id === recipe.id ? { ...r, isHidden: newState } : r));
    }

    const filtered = useMemo(() => {
        const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
        const list = recipes.filter(r => {
            const name = (isAr ? (r.nameAr || r.name) : r.name).toLowerCase();
            const category = isAr ? (r.categoryAr || r.category) : r.category;
            if (q && !name.includes(q)) return false;
            if (categoryFilter && category !== categoryFilter && r.category !== categoryFilter && r.categoryAr !== categoryFilter) return false;
            return true;
        });

        // Sort: Hidden first (1), then visible (0)
        return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
            const aHidden = a.isHidden === 1 ? 1 : 0;
            const bHidden = b.isHidden === 1 ? 1 : 0;
            // Descending order (1 comes before 0)
            return bHidden - aHidden;
        });
    }, [recipes, query, categoryFilter, isAr]);

    if (loading) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>{isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.loading : "Loading..."}</div>;

    return (
        <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 800 }}>
            <div className="page-head">
                <h1>{isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.title : "Hidden Recipes"}</h1>
            </div>
            <p className="page-sub">
                {isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.subtitle : "Select recipes to hide from the menu"}
            </p>

            {/* Filters */}
            <div className="filter-bar" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
                <div className="search-wrap">
                    <span className="search-icon">🔍</span>
                    <input
                        type="text"
                        className="search-input"
                        placeholder={isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.searchPlaceholder : "Search recipes..."}
                        value={query}
                        onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
                    />
                </div>
                <select
                    className="filter-select"
                    value={categoryFilter}
                    onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}
                >
                    <option value="">{isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.allCategories : "All categories"}</option>
                    {CATEGORIES.map(c => (
                        <option key={c.value} value={c.value}>
                            {c.icon} {isAr ? c.valueAr : c.value}
                        </option>
                    ))}
                </select>
            </div>

            <div className="form-card" style={{ padding: 0, overflow: "hidden" }}>
                <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 14 }}>
                    <thead style={{ background: "var(--bg)", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                        <tr>
                            <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>#</th>
                            <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.name : "Name"}</th>
                            <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.category : "Category"}</th>
                            <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.status : "Status"}</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        {filtered.length === 0 ? (
                            <tr>
                                <td colSpan={4} style={{ padding: 24, textAlign: "center", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                                    {isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.noRecipes : "No recipes."}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        ) : (
                            filtered.map((r, i) => {
                                const flagUrl = cuisineFlagUrl(r.cuisine) || cuisineFlagUrl(r.cuisineAr);
                                return (
                                    <tr key={r.id} style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                                        <td style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontVariantNumeric: "tabular-nums" }}>{i + 1}</td>
                                        <td style={{ padding: 12 }}>
                                            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                                                {flagUrl && <img src={flagUrl} alt="" style={{ width: 16, height: 12, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 2 }} />}
                                                <span style={{ fontWeight: 500 }}>{isAr ? (r.nameAr || r.name) : r.name}</span>
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                        <td style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                                            {isAr ? (r.categoryAr || r.category) : r.category}
                                        </td>
                                        <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center" }}>
                                            <label style={{ display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, cursor: "pointer" }}>
                                                <input
                                                    type="checkbox"
                                                    checked={r.isHidden === 1}
                                                    onChange={() => toggleHidden(r)}
                                                    style={{ width: 18, height: 18, cursor: "pointer" }}
                                                />
                                                <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: r.isHidden === 1 ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--olive)", fontWeight: 600 }}>
                                                    {r.isHidden === 1 ? (isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.hidden : "Hidden") : (isAr ? ar.hiddenRecipes.visible : "Visible")}
                                                </span>
                                            </label>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                );
                            })
                        )}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </main>
    );
}--- END FILE: src/app/hidden-recipes/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/import/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useRef, useCallback } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

type ParsedRecipe = {
  name?: string;
  nameAr?: string;
  ingredients?: unknown[];
  instructions?: unknown[];
  [key: string]: unknown;
};

type PreviewRow = {
  index: number;
  name: string;
  ingredients: number;
  steps: number;
  warning: string | null;
};

const SAMPLE = JSON.stringify(
  [
    {
      name: "Chicken Maqluba",
      nameAr: "مقلوبة دجاج",
      description: "Layered rice, chicken, and vegetables",
      cuisine: "Palestinian",
      category: "Main Dish",
      prepTime: 30,
      cookTime: 60,
      servings: 4,
      dietaryTags: ["Halal"],
      imageUrl: null,
      videoUrl: null,
      ingredients: [
        { name: "Chicken thighs", nameAr: "أفخاذ دجاج", amount: "1", unit: "kg" }
      ],
      instructions: [
        { text: "Brown the chicken.", textAr: "حمّر الدجاج." }
      ]
    }
  ],
  null,
  2
);

export default function ImportRecipesPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [json, setJson] = useState("");
  const [targetId, setTargetId] = useState("");
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<PreviewRow[] | null>(null);
  const [parseError, setParseError] = useState("");
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{ inserted: number; updated?: number; ids: number[]; updatedIds?: number[] } | null>(null);
  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);

  const reset = () => {
    setPreview(null);
    setParseError("");
    setResult(null);
  };

  function normalize(input: string): ParsedRecipe[] {
    const trimmed = input.trim();
    if (!trimmed) throw new Error("Paste JSON first");
    let data: unknown;
    try {
      data = JSON.parse(trimmed);
    } catch (e) {
      throw new Error("Invalid JSON: " + (e as Error).message);
    }
    if (data && typeof data === "object" && !Array.isArray(data)) {
      const obj = data as Record<string, unknown>;
      if (Array.isArray(obj.recipes)) return obj.recipes as ParsedRecipe[];
      return [obj as ParsedRecipe];
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(data)) throw new Error("Expected JSON array or object");
    return data as ParsedRecipe[];
  }

  function applyTargetId(list: ParsedRecipe[]): ParsedRecipe[] {
    if (!targetId.trim()) return list;
    const idNum = parseInt(targetId.trim(), 10);
    if (isNaN(idNum)) throw new Error("ID must be a number");
    return list.map((r) => ({ ...r, id: idNum }));
  }

  function handleParse() {
    reset();
    try {
      let list = normalize(json);
      list = applyTargetId(list);
      const rows: PreviewRow[] = list.map((r, i) => {
        const name = (r.name as string) || (r.nameAr as string) || "";
        const ing = Array.isArray(r.ingredients) ? r.ingredients.length : 0;
        const steps = Array.isArray(r.instructions) ? r.instructions.length : 0;
        let warning: string | null = null;
        if (!name.trim()) warning = "Missing name — will skip";
        return { index: i + 1, name: name || "(unnamed)", ingredients: ing, steps, warning };
      });
      setPreview(rows);
    } catch (e) {
      setParseError((e as Error).message);
    }
  }

  const handleDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setDragOver(false);
    const file = e.dataTransfer.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      setJson(String(reader.result || ""));
      reset();
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  }, []);

  function handleFilePick(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      setJson(String(reader.result || ""));
      reset();
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  }

  async function handleImport() {
    reset();
    let list: ParsedRecipe[];
    try {
      list = normalize(json);
      list = applyTargetId(list);
    } catch (e) {
      setParseError((e as Error).message);
      return;
    }

    setImporting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/recipes/bulk", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(list),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setParseError(data.error || "Import failed");
      } else {
        setResult({
          inserted: data.inserted || 0,
          updated: data.updated || 0,
          ids: data.insertedIds || data.ids || [],
          updatedIds: data.updatedIds || [],
        });
        setPreview(null);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setParseError((e as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setImporting(false);
    }
  }

  function loadSample() {
    setJson(SAMPLE);
    reset();
  }

  function clearAll() {
    setJson("");
    setTargetId("");
    reset();
  }

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 800 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{lang === "ar" ? "استيراد وصفات" : "Import Recipes"}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">
        {lang === "ar"
          ? "الصق مصفوفة JSON من الوصفات — سيتم عرضها للمعاينة قبل الحفظ"
          : "Paste a JSON array of recipes — preview before saving"}
      </p>

      <div className="form-card">
        {/* TARGET ID FIELD */}
        <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <label style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 6, fontWeight: 600 }}>
            {lang === "ar" ? "معرف الوصفة (اتركه فارغاً لإنشاء جديدة)" : "Recipe ID (leave blank to create new)"}
          </label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={targetId}
            onChange={(e) => { setTargetId(e.target.value); reset(); }}
            placeholder={lang === "ar" ? "مثال: 105" : "e.g. 105"}
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              padding: "10px 14px",
              fontSize: 14,
              border: "1px solid var(--line)",
              borderRadius: 8,
              background: "var(--bg)",
              color: "var(--ink)",
            }}
          />
          <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)", marginTop: 4 }}>
            {lang === "ar"
              ? "إذا أدخلت معرفاً موجوداً، سيتم تحديث الوصفة. إذا تركته فارغاً، سيتم إنشاء وصفة جديدة."
              : "If you enter an existing ID, the recipe will be updated. Leave blank to create a new recipe."}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="field">
          <label>JSON</label>
          <textarea
            value={json}
            onChange={(e) => { setJson(e.target.value); reset(); }}
            placeholder='[{"name":"Recipe","ingredients":[],"instructions":[]}]'
            dir="ltr"
            style={{ width: "100%", minHeight: 240, padding: 12, fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)", fontSize: 12.5, lineHeight: 1.5 }}
            onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); }}
            onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
            onDrop={handleDrop}
          />
          {dragOver && (
            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--accent)", marginTop: 6 }}>
              Drop file to load
            </div>
          )}
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", marginTop: 10 }}>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={loadSample} style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
              Sample
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
              Open file
            </button>
            <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept=".json,application/json" onChange={handleFilePick} style={{ display: "none" }} />
            {json && (
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={clearAll} style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
                Clear
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {parseError && (
          <div style={{ padding: "10px 14px", background: "var(--accent-soft)", color: "var(--accent)", border: "1px solid var(--accent)", borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 13, marginTop: 12 }}>
            {parseError}
          </div>
        )}

        {preview && preview.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "0.06em", color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 10 }}>
              Preview ({preview.length})
              {targetId.trim() && (
                <span style={{ marginLeft: 8, color: "var(--accent)" }}>
                  · Will update ID {targetId}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6 }}>
              {preview.map((row) => (
                <div
                  key={row.index}
                  style={{
                    display: "flex",
                    alignItems: "center",
                    gap: 10,
                    padding: "8px 12px",
                    background: row.warning ? "var(--accent-soft)" : "var(--bg)",
                    border: "1px solid var(--line)",
                    borderRadius: 6,
                    fontSize: 13,
                  }}
                >
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: row.warning ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--olive)", minWidth: 24 }}>
                    {row.warning ? "!" : "OK"}
                  </span>
                  <span style={{ flex: 1, color: "var(--ink)", fontWeight: 500 }}>
                    {row.name}
                  </span>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)", fontVariantNumeric: "tabular-nums" }}>
                    {row.ingredients} ing · {row.steps} steps
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {result && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 20, padding: 14, background: "var(--olive-soft)", border: "1px solid var(--olive)", borderRadius: 8 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--olive)", marginBottom: 6 }}>
              {result.inserted > 0 && `Inserted ${result.inserted} new recipe(s). `}
              {result.updated && result.updated > 0 && `Updated ${result.updated} existing recipe(s).`}
            </div>
            {result.ids.length > 0 && (
              <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--ink-soft)", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                New IDs: {result.ids.join(", ")}
              </div>
            )}
            {result.updatedIds && result.updatedIds.length > 0 && (
              <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--ink-soft)", marginBottom: 10 }}>
                Updated IDs: {result.updatedIds.join(", ")}
              </div>
            )}
            <Link 
              href={
                result.updatedIds && result.updatedIds.length > 0 
                  ? `/recipes/${result.updatedIds[0]}`
                  : result.ids.length > 0 
                  ? `/recipes/${result.ids[0]}`
                  : "/"
              } 
              className="btn btn-primary" 
              style={{ fontSize: 12 }}
            >
              {result.updatedIds && result.updatedIds.length > 0 ? "View Updated Recipe" : "View New Recipe"}
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}

        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 20 }}>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={handleParse} style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            Parse & Preview
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn btn-primary"
            onClick={handleImport}
            disabled={importing || !json.trim()}
            style={{ flex: 2 }}
          >
            {importing ? "Importing..." : targetId.trim() ? `Update ID ${targetId}` : "Import as New"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/import/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/inbox/[id]/InboxDetailClient.tsx ---
"use client";

import Spinner from "@/components/Spinner";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";

type Notification = {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  location: string | null;
  time: string | null;
  pollId: number | null;
  pollQuestion: string | null;
  pollUrl: string | null;
  url: string | null;
};

export default function InboxDetailClient({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";

  const [item, setItem] = useState<Notification | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  async function loadData() {
    if (!id) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/notifications", { cache: "no-store" });
      const data = await res.json();
      const found = Array.isArray(data) ? data.find((n: Notification) => n.id === Number(id)) : null;
      setItem(found || null);
    } catch {
      setItem(null);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
    // Mark as read
    fetch(`/api/notifications/${id}/read`, { method: "POST" }).catch(() => {});
  }, [id]);

  if (loading) {
    return <Spinner />;
  }

  if (!item) {
    return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>{isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.messageNotFound : "Message not found"}</div>;
  }

  const hasRecipe = item.url && item.url !== "/inbox" && !item.url.startsWith("/poll/");

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 700 }}>
      
      {/* Top Bar with Back and Refresh */}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <Link href="/inbox" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
          {isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.back : "← Back to Inbox"}
        </Link>
        <button onClick={loadData} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
          ↻ {isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.refresh : ar.inboxDetail.refreshEn}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "var(--radius-lg)", padding: 32 }}>
        <h1 style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-head)", fontSize: "2rem", margin: "0 0 8px 0", fontWeight: 400 }}>
          {item.title}
        </h1>
        <p style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "var(--muted)", margin: "0 0 24px 0" }}>
          {new Date(item.createdAt).toLocaleString()}
        </p>

        {item.location && (
          <div style={{ padding: "10px 16px", background: "var(--accent-soft)", color: "var(--accent)", borderRadius: 8, fontSize: "0.875rem", fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 8 }}>
            📍 {item.location}
          </div>
        )}
        {item.time && (
          <div style={{ padding: "10px 16px", background: "var(--surface-2)", color: "var(--ink)", borderRadius: 8, fontSize: "0.875rem", fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 24 }}>
            ⏰ {item.time}
          </div>
        )}

        <p style={{ fontSize: "0.94rem", lineHeight: 1.7, color: "var(--ink)", margin: "0 0 32px 0", whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }}>
          {item.body}
        </p>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 12 }}>
          {item.pollId && (
            <Link href={item.pollUrl || `/poll/${item.pollId}`} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ padding: "12px 20px" }}>
              📊 {isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.openPoll : "Open Poll"}
            </Link>
          )}
          {hasRecipe && (
            <Link href={item.url!} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "12px 20px" }}>
              🍽 {isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.viewRecipe : "View Recipe"}
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
