--- FILE: src/app/inbox/admin/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import Spinner from "@/components/Spinner";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";

type ReadEntry = { name: string | null; phone: string };

type AdminNotification = {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  createdAt: string;
  readers: ReadEntry[];
};

export default function InboxAdminPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const router = useRouter();
  const [items, setItems] = useState<AdminNotification[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (!data.admin) router.push("/login");
        else load();
      });
  }, [router]);

  function load() {
    fetch("/api/inbox/admin", { cache: "no-store" })
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }

  if (loading) {
    return <Spinner />;
  }

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 800 }}>
      <Link href="/inbox" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", marginBottom: 24 }}>
        {isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.back : "← Back to Inbox"}
      </Link>

      <div className="page-head" style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
        <h1>{isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.adminTitle : "Inbox Admin"}</h1>
        <button onClick={load} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.8rem" }}>
          ↻ {isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.refresh : ar.inboxDetail.refreshEn}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>
        {items.map((item) => (
          <div key={item.id} style={{ background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "var(--radius)", padding: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: "1rem" }}>{item.title}</h3>
              <span style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "var(--muted)" }}>{new Date(item.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
            </div>

            <div style={{ fontSize: "0.81rem", fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--olive)", marginBottom: 6 }}>
              {isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.readBy : "Read by"} · {item.readers.length}
            </div>
            {item.readers.length === 0 ? (
              <p style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "var(--muted)", fontStyle: "italic", margin: 0 }}>
                {isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.noReads : "No one has read this message yet."}
              </p>
            ) : (
              <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 6 }}>
                {item.readers.map((r, index) => (
                  <span key={`${r.phone}-${index}`} style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", padding: "4px 10px", background: "var(--olive-soft)", color: "var(--olive)", borderRadius: 999, fontWeight: 500 }}>
                    {r.name || (isAr ? ar.inbox.unnamed : "Unnamed")} ({r.phone})
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/inbox/admin/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/inbox/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import Spinner from "@/components/Spinner";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { useModal } from "@/components/ModalProvider";

type Notification = {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  isRead: boolean;
  location: string | null;
  time: string | null;
};

export default function InboxPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const { show, confirm } = useModal();

  const [items, setItems] = useState<Notification[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<number[]>([]);

  async function loadData() {
    setLoading(true);
    
    // 1. CLEAR APP BADGE (Set notification dot to 0)
    if ('clearAppBadge' in navigator) {
      navigator.clearAppBadge().catch((error) => console.error('Badge clear error:', error));
    }
    if ('caches' in window) {
      caches.open('badge-count').then((cache) => {
        cache.delete('count');
      });
    }
    // Save last read time
    localStorage.setItem("last_read_inbox", new Date().toISOString());

    // 2. FETCH DATA
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/notifications", { cache: "no-store" });
      const data = await res.json();
      setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch {
      setItems([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();

    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => setIsAdmin(!!data.admin))
      .catch(() => setIsAdmin(false));
  }, []);

  async function handleDelete(e: React.MouseEvent, id: number) {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    const ok = await confirm({
      message: isAr ? ar.messages.deleteMessageConfirm : "Delete message?",
      confirmText: isAr ? ar.modal.deleteBtn : "Delete",
      tone: "danger"
    });
    if (!ok) return;

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notifications/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      if (res.ok) {
        setItems((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
      } else {
        show({ message: isAr ? ar.messages.deleteFailed.ar : "Delete failed", tone: "danger" });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      show({ message: isAr ? ar.messages.deleteFailed.ar : "Delete failed", tone: "danger" });
    }
  }

  function toggleSelection(id: number) {
    setSelectedIds(prev => prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter(x => x !== id) : [...prev, id]);
  }

  function toggleAll() {
    if (selectedIds.length === items.length) setSelectedIds([]);
    else setSelectedIds(items.map(i => i.id));
  }

  async function handleBulkDelete() {
    if (selectedIds.length === 0) return;
    const ok = await confirm({
      message: isAr ? "حذف الرسائل المحددة؟" : "Delete selected messages?",
      confirmText: isAr ? ar.modal.deleteBtn : "Delete",
      tone: "danger"
    });
    if (!ok) return;

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/notifications/bulk-delete", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ids: selectedIds }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setItems((prev) => prev.filter((item) => !selectedIds.includes(item.id)));
        setSelectedIds([]);
      } else {
        show({ message: isAr ? "فشل الحذف" : "Delete failed", tone: "danger" });
      }
    } catch {
      show({ message: isAr ? "فشل الحذف" : "Delete failed", tone: "danger" });
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return <Spinner />;
  }

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 700 }}>
      <div className="page-head" style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <h1 style={{ margin: 0 }}>{isAr ? ar.inbox.title : "Inbox"}</h1>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          {isAdmin && items.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <button onClick={toggleAll} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.8rem" }}>
                {selectedIds.length === items.length ? (isAr ? "إلغاء تحديد الكل" : "Deselect All") : (isAr ? "تحديد الكل" : "Select All")}
              </button>
              {selectedIds.length > 0 && (
                <button onClick={handleBulkDelete} className="btn btn-danger" style={{ fontSize: "0.8rem" }}>
                  🗑 {isAr ? "حذف المحدد" : "Delete Selected"} ({selectedIds.length})
                </button>
              )}
            </>
          )}
          <button onClick={loadData} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.8rem" }}>
            ↻ {isAr ? ar.inboxDetail.refresh : ar.inboxDetail.refreshEn}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{isAr ? ar.inbox.empty : "No notifications yet"}</h2>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 0, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "var(--radius)", overflow: "hidden" }}>
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)",
                background: selectedIds.includes(item.id) ? "var(--accent-soft)" : (item.isRead ? "transparent" : "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02)"),
                transition: "background 0.15s",
              }}
            >
              {isAdmin && (
                <div style={{ padding: "0 0 0 16px", display: "flex", alignItems: "center" }}>
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={selectedIds.includes(item.id)}
                    onChange={() => toggleSelection(item.id)}
                    style={{ width: 18, height: 18, cursor: "pointer", accentColor: "var(--accent)" }}
                  />
                </div>
              )}
              <Link
                href={`/inbox/${item.id}`}
                style={{
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  justifyContent: "space-between",
                  padding: "16px 20px",
                  textDecoration: "none",
                  flex: 1,
                  minWidth: 0,
                }}
                onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.parentElement!.style.background = "var(--surface-2)"; }}
                onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.parentElement!.style.background = item.isRead ? "transparent" : "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02)"; }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4, flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: "0.94rem", fontWeight: item.isRead ? 400 : 700, color: item.isRead ? "var(--ink-soft)" : "var(--ink)", overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
                    {item.title}
                  </span>
                  {item.location && (
                    <span style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "var(--muted)" }}>📍 {item.location}</span>
                  )}
                  {item.time && (
                    <span style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "var(--muted)" }}>⏰ {item.time}</span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                    {new Date(item.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}
                  </span>
                  {!item.isRead && (
                    <span style={{ display: "inline-block", width: 10, height: 10, borderRadius: "50%", background: "var(--accent)" }} />
                  )}
                </div>
              </Link>

              {isAdmin && (
                <button
                  onClick={(e) => handleDelete(e, item.id)}
                  style={{
                    background: "transparent",
                    border: "none",
                    color: "var(--accent)",
                    padding: "16px 20px",
                    cursor: "pointer",
                    fontSize: "1.1rem",
                  }}
                  title={isAr ? ar.modal.deleteBtn : "Delete"}
                >
                  🗑
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/inbox/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/layout.tsx ---
import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import Script from "next/script";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import "./globals.css";
import { LanguageProvider } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ThemeProvider } from "@/components/ThemeProvider";
import NavGate from "@/components/NavGate";
import DynamicFavicon from "@/components/DynamicFavicon";
import TitleThemeSync from "@/components/TitleThemeSync";
import BackToTop from "@/components/BackToTop";
import { ModalProvider } from "@/components/ModalProvider";
import IdleReload from "@/components/IdleReload";
import PresenceTracker from "@/components/PresenceTracker";

// Static Metadata (No DB calls here to avoid blocking)
export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Hictra",
  description: "Your personal cookbook",
  openGraph: {
    title: "Hictra",
    siteName: "Hictra",
  },
};

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  width: "device-width",
  initialScale: 1,
  maximumScale: 1,
  userScalable: false,
};

const inlineBootstrap = `
(function() {
  try {
    // 1. Language
    var cookieLang = document.cookie.match(/lang=(en|ar)/);
    var l = cookieLang ? cookieLang[1] : (localStorage.getItem("lang") || "en");
    document.documentElement.lang = l;
    document.documentElement.dir = l === "ar" ? "rtl" : "ltr";

    // 2. Theme
    var t = localStorage.getItem("theme");
    if (!t) t = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: light)").matches ? "light" : "dark";
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", t);

    // 3. Font Size
    var f = localStorage.getItem("font_size");
    if (f) document.documentElement.style.fontSize = f;

    // 4. Fetch Settings (App Name & User Settings)
    fetch("/api/settings", { credentials: "same-origin" })
      .then(function(r){ return r.json(); })
      .then(function(d){
        if (d.app_name) {
          document.title = d.app_name;
          var ogTitle = document.querySelector('meta[property="og:title"]');
          if (ogTitle) ogTitle.setAttribute("content", d.app_name);
          var ogSite = document.querySelector('meta[property="og:site_name"]');
          if (ogSite) ogSite.setAttribute("content", d.app_name);
        }
      })
      .catch(function(){});

    fetch("/api/user-settings", { credentials: "same-origin" })
      .then(function(r){ return r.json(); })
      .then(function(d){
        if (d.theme) document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", d.theme);
        if (d.lang) {
          document.documentElement.lang = d.lang;
          document.documentElement.dir = d.lang === "ar" ? "rtl" : "ltr";
        }
        if (d.font_size) document.documentElement.style.fontSize = d.font_size;
      })
      .catch(function(){});
  } catch(e) {}
})();
`;

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" data-theme="dark" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <head>
        <Script id="bootstrap" strategy="beforeInteractive" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: inlineBootstrap }} />
      </head>
      <body>
        <ThemeProvider>
          <LanguageProvider>
            <Suspense fallback={null}>
              <PresenceTracker />
              <IdleReload />
            </Suspense>
            <ModalProvider>
              <DynamicFavicon />
              <TitleThemeSync />
              <Suspense fallback={null}><NavGate /></Suspense>
              {children}
            </ModalProvider>
          </LanguageProvider>
        </ThemeProvider>
        <BackToTop />
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/login/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/auth/login", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(data.error || "Login failed");
      }
      window.location.href = "/";
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Login failed");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="login-card">
      <h1>{lang === "ar" ? "تسجيل الدخول" : "Sign in"}</h1>
      <form onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="field">
          <label>{lang === "ar" ? "اسم المستخدم" : "Username"}</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={username}
            onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
            autoFocus
            required
          />
        </div>
        <div className="field">
          <label>{lang === "ar" ? "كلمة المرور" : "Password"}</label>
          <input
            type="password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </div>
        {error && (
          <p style={{ color: "var(--accent)", fontSize: 12, margin: "0 0 12px" }}>{error}</p>
        )}
        <button
          type="submit"
          className="btn btn-primary"
          disabled={busy}
          style={{ width: "100%", justifyContent: "center", padding: "10px 16px" }}
        >
          {busy ? "…" : lang === "ar" ? "دخول" : "Continue"}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/login/page.tsx ---

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { settings } from "@/db/schema";

export async function generateMetadata(): Promise<Metadata> {
  const db = getDb();
  const rows = await db.select().from(settings);
  const s = Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.key, r.value]));
  const image =
    s.no_dish_image_url ||
    s.logo_url ||
    "https://food-menu-app-xxww.onrender.com/icon-512.png";

  return {
    title: "No dish today",
    description: "Check back tomorrow — something delicious is coming.",
    openGraph: {
      title: "No dish today",
      description: "Check back tomorrow — something delicious is coming.",
      images: [{ url: image }],
      type: "website",
    },
    twitter: {
      card: "summary_large_image",
      title: "No dish today",
      description: "Check back tomorrow — something delicious is coming.",
      images: [image],
    },
  };
}

async function NoDishContent() {
  await connection();
  const db = getDb();
  const rows = await db.select().from(settings);
  const s = Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.key, r.value]));
  const imageUrl = s.no_dish_image_url || "";
  const videoUrl = s.no_dish_video_url || "";
  const logoUrl = s.logo_url || "";

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        flexDirection: "column",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",
        minHeight: "calc(100vh - 200px)",
        textAlign: "center",
        padding: "40px 24px",
        gap: 28,
      }}
    >
      {(imageUrl || logoUrl) && (
        <img
          src={imageUrl || logoUrl}
          alt="No dish today"
          style={{
            maxWidth: 360,
            width: "100%",
            height: "auto",
            borderRadius: 20,
            boxShadow: "0 24px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.18), 0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)",
          }}
        />
      )}

      {videoUrl && (
        <div
          style={{
            width: "100%",
            maxWidth: 560,
            borderRadius: 20,
            overflow: "hidden",
            border: "1px solid var(--line)",
            boxShadow: "0 24px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.18)",
            background: "#000",
            aspectRatio: "16 / 9",
          }}
        >
          <video
            src={videoUrl}
            controls
            playsInline
            preload="metadata"
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              height: "100%",
              display: "block",
              objectFit: "contain",
            }}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12, alignItems: "center" }}>
        <h1
          style={{
            fontSize: 38,
            margin: 0,
            fontFamily: "var(--font-head)",
            fontWeight: 400,
            letterSpacing: "-0.025em",
            lineHeight: 1.1,
          }}
        >
          Sorry, no dish today
        </h1>
        <p
          style={{
            fontSize: 15,
            color: "var(--ink-soft)",
            maxWidth: 480,
            lineHeight: 1.6,
            margin: 0,
          }}
        >
          Check back tomorrow — something delicious is coming.
        </p>
      </div>

      <Link
        href="/recipes"
        className="btn btn-primary"
        style={{ padding: "14px 28px", fontSize: 14 }}
      >
        ← Browse recipes
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function NoDishTodayPage() {
  return (
    <main className="container">
      <NoDishContent />
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

export default function WelcomePage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<{
    logo_url?: string;
    background_url?: string;
    welcome_title?: string;
    welcome_button?: string;
  }>({});
  const [name, setName] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/settings")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => setSettings(data))
      .catch(() => {});
    fetch("/api/auth/welcome")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => setName(data.name || null))
      .catch(() => setName(null));
  }, []);

  const defaultTitle = lang === "ar" ? "أهلاً بك في منيو هيكترا" : "Welcome to Hictra Recipes";
  const defaultButton = lang === "ar" ? "دخول" : "Enter";
  const welcomeTitle = settings.welcome_title || defaultTitle;
  const welcomeButton = settings.welcome_button || defaultButton;

  const greeting = name
    ? (lang === "ar" ? `أهلاً بك، ${name}` : `Welcome, ${name}`)
    : welcomeTitle;

  return (
    <div
      className="welcome-fullscreen"
      style={{
        backgroundImage: settings.background_url ? `url(${settings.background_url})` : undefined,
      }}
    >
      <div className="welcome-card-fullscreen">
        {settings.logo_url && (
          <img src={settings.logo_url} alt="Logo" className="welcome-logo-fullscreen" />
        )}
        <h1 className="welcome-title-fullscreen">{greeting}</h1>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="welcome-enter-btn"
          onClick={() => router.push("/recipes")}
        >
          {welcomeButton}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/poll/[id]/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

type PollData = {
  id: number;
  question: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  targetPhone: string | null;
  recipeUrl: string | null;
  yesCount: number;
  noCount: number;
  myAnswer: "yes" | "no" | null;
  canVote: boolean;
  hasPhoneCookie: boolean;
  isTargetedToMe: boolean;
};

export default function PollPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useParams();
  const pollId = params?.id as string;

  const [poll, setPoll] = useState<PollData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [voting, setVoting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [phoneInput, setPhoneInput] = useState("");
  const [verifying, setVerifying] = useState(false);
  const [verifyError, setVerifyError] = useState("");

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    if (!pollId) return;
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/polls/${pollId}`)
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data.error) {
          setError(data.error);
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }
        setPoll(data);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        setError("Failed to load");
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, [pollId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  async function verify() {
    const trimmed = phoneInput.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;
    setVerifying(true);
    setVerifyError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/auth/verify-phone", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ phoneNumber: trimmed }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setVerifyError(data.error || "Failed to verify");
        setVerifying(false);
        return;
      }
      setPhoneInput("");
      load();
    } catch (err) {
      setVerifyError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Error");
    } finally {
      setVerifying(false);
    }
  }

  async function vote(answer: "yes" | "no") {
    setVoting(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/polls/${pollId}`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ answer }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.error || "Failed to vote");
        setVoting(false);
        return;
      }
      load();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Error");
    } finally {
      setVoting(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40 }}>
        <p className="helper">{lang === "ar" ? "جاري التحميل..." : "Loading..."}</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (error && !poll) {
    return (
      <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40 }}>
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{lang === "ar" ? "خطأ" : "Error"}</h2>
          <p>{error}</p>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (!poll) return null;

  const total = poll.yesCount + poll.noCount;
  const yesPct = total > 0 ? Math.round((poll.yesCount / total) * 100) : 0;
  const hasRecipe = poll.recipeUrl && poll.recipeUrl !== "/inbox" && !poll.recipeUrl.startsWith("/poll/");

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 640 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1 style={{ fontSize: 28, margin: 0 }}>
          {lang === "ar" ? "استطلاع" : "Poll"}
        </h1>
      </div>

      <div className="form-card" style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: "0.08em", textTransform: "uppercase", color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 8 }}>
          {lang === "ar" ? "السؤال" : "Question"}
        </div>
        <h2 style={{ fontSize: 24, margin: "0 0 20px 0", fontFamily: "var(--font-head)", fontWeight: 400 }}>
          {poll.question}
        </h2>

        {poll.body && (
          <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--ink-soft)", marginBottom: 20, lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
            {poll.body}
          </p>
        )}

        <div style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 16, marginBottom: 10, fontSize: 13, color: "var(--ink-soft)" }}>
            <span>
              <strong style={{ color: "var(--olive)", fontSize: 16 }}>{poll.yesCount}</strong>{" "}
              {lang === "ar" ? "نعم" : "Yes"}
            </span>
            <span>
              <strong style={{ color: "var(--accent)", fontSize: 16 }}>{poll.noCount}</strong>{" "}
              {lang === "ar" ? "لا" : "No"}
            </span>
            <span style={{ marginInlineStart: "auto", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
              {total} {lang === "ar" ? "صوت" : total === 1 ? "vote" : "votes"}
            </span>
          </div>
          {total > 0 && (
            <div style={{ height: 8, borderRadius: 4, background: "var(--line)", overflow: "hidden", display: "flex" }}>
              <div style={{ width: `${yesPct}%`, background: "var(--olive)", transition: "width 0.3s ease" }} />
              <div style={{ width: `${100 - yesPct}%`, background: "var(--accent)", transition: "width 0.3s ease" }} />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {!poll.isOpen && (
          <div style={{ padding: "10px 14px", background: "var(--surface-2)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 13, color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 16 }}>
            {lang === "ar" ? "التصويت مغلق" : "Voting is closed"}
          </div>
        )}

        {poll.isOpen && !poll.hasPhoneCookie && (
          <div style={{ background: "var(--bg)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10, padding: 16, marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--ink)", marginBottom: 8 }}>
              {lang === "ar" ? "تحقق من رقم هاتفك للتصويت" : "Verify your phone to vote"}
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder={lang === "ar" ? "رقم هاتفك" : "Your phone number"}
                value={phoneInput}
                onChange={(e) => setPhoneInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === "Enter") verify(); }}
                style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 180, padding: "8px 12px", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, background: "var(--input-bg)", color: "var(--ink)", fontSize: 13 }}
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn btn-primary"
                onClick={verify}
                disabled={verifying || !phoneInput.trim()}
              >
                {verifying ? "…" : lang === "ar" ? "تحقق" : "Verify"}
              </button>
            </div>
            {verifyError && (
              <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--accent)", marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 0 }}>{verifyError}</p>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {poll.isOpen && poll.hasPhoneCookie && !poll.isTargetedToMe && (
          <div style={{ padding: "10px 14px", background: "var(--surface-2)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 13, color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 16 }}>
            {lang === "ar" ? "أنت غير مدعو لهذا الاستطلاع" : "You are not invited to this poll"}
          </div>
        )}

        {poll.isOpen && poll.canVote && (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, marginTop: 16 }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => vote("yes")}
              disabled={voting}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                padding: "14px 20px",
                borderRadius: 10,
                border: poll.myAnswer === "yes" ? "2px solid var(--olive)" : "1px solid var(--line)",
                background: poll.myAnswer === "yes" ? "var(--olive-soft)" : "var(--surface)",
                color: poll.myAnswer === "yes" ? "var(--olive)" : "var(--ink)",
                fontSize: 15,
                fontWeight: 600,
                cursor: voting ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
                opacity: voting ? 0.6 : 1,
                transition: "all 0.15s",
              }}
            >
              {poll.myAnswer === "yes" ? "✓ " : ""}
              {lang === "ar" ? "نعم" : "Yes"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => vote("no")}
              disabled={voting}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                padding: "14px 20px",
                borderRadius: 10,
                border: poll.myAnswer === "no" ? "2px solid var(--accent)" : "1px solid var(--line)",
                background: poll.myAnswer === "no" ? "var(--accent-soft)" : "var(--surface)",
                color: poll.myAnswer === "no" ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--ink)",
                fontSize: 15,
                fontWeight: 600,
                cursor: voting ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
                opacity: voting ? 0.6 : 1,
                transition: "all 0.15s",
              }}
            >
              {poll.myAnswer === "no" ? "✓ " : ""}
              {lang === "ar" ? "لا" : "No"}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {poll.myAnswer && poll.isOpen && (
          <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--muted)", marginTop: 12, marginBottom: 0, textAlign: "center" }}>
            {lang === "ar" ? "يمكنك تغيير صوتك بالضغط على الزر الآخر" : "You can change your vote anytime"}
          </p>
        )}

        {error && poll && (
          <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--accent)", marginTop: 12 }}>{error}</p>
        )}

        <div style={{ marginTop: 24, paddingTop: 16, borderTop: "1px solid var(--line)", display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 8 }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn btn-ghost"
            onClick={() => router.push("/inbox")}
            style={{ fontSize: 12 }}
          >
            {lang === "ar" ? "→ العودة إلى صندوق الرسائل" : "→ Back to inbox"}
          </button>
          {hasRecipe && (
            <Link
              href={poll.recipeUrl!}
              className="btn btn-ghost"
              style={{ fontSize: 12 }}
            >
              🍽 {lang === "ar" ? "عرض الوصفة" : "View recipe"} →
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/recipes/[id]/edit/page.tsx ---
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, asc } from "drizzle-orm";
import RecipeForm from "@/components/RecipeForm";
import EditRecipeShell from "@/components/EditRecipeShell";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

async function AuthGate({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) redirect("/login");
  return <>{children}</>;
}

async function EditLoader({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const numericId = Number(id);
  if (Number.isNaN(numericId)) notFound();

  const db = getDb();
  const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, numericId)).limit(1);
  if (!recipe) notFound();

  const ings = await db.select().from(ingredients).where(eq(ingredients.recipeId, numericId)).orderBy(asc(ingredients.id));
  const steps = await db.select().from(instructions).where(eq(instructions.recipeId, numericId)).orderBy(asc(instructions.stepNumber));

  return (
    <RecipeForm
      initial={{
        id: recipe.id,
        name: recipe.name,
        nameAr: recipe.nameAr,
        description: recipe.description,
        descriptionAr: recipe.descriptionAr,
        cuisine: recipe.cuisine,
        cuisineAr: recipe.cuisineAr,
        category: recipe.category,
        categoryAr: recipe.categoryAr,
        servedWith: recipe.servedWith,
        servedWithAr: recipe.servedWithAr,
        howToServe: recipe.howToServe,
        howToServeAr: recipe.howToServeAr,
        prepTime: recipe.prepTime,
        cookTime: recipe.cookTime,
        servings: recipe.servings,
        dietaryTags: recipe.dietaryTags,
        imageUrl: recipe.imageUrl,
        image1Url: recipe.image1Url,
        image2Url: recipe.image2Url,
        useImage2: recipe.useImage2,
        videoUrl: recipe.videoUrl,
        ingredients: ings.map((i) => ({
          name: i.name, nameAr: i.nameAr, amount: i.amount, unit: i.unit,
          unitAr: i.unitAr, preparation: i.preparation, preparationAr: i.preparationAr,
        })),
        instructions: steps.map((s) => ({ text: s.text, textAr: s.textAr })),
      }}
    />
  );
}

function FormSkeleton() {
  return <div className="form-card" style={{ height: 400, opacity: 0.4 }} />;
}

export default function EditRecipePage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  return (
    <main className="container">
      <EditRecipeShell>
        <Suspense fallback={<FormSkeleton />}>
          <AuthGate>
            <EditLoader params={params} />
          </AuthGate>
        </Suspense>
      </EditRecipeShell>
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/recipes/[id]/history/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";

type HistoryEntry = {
  id: number;
  date: string;
  slot: "breakfast" | "lunch" | "dinner" | "snack" | "dessert";
};

export default function RecipeHistoryPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const params = useParams();
  const recipeId = params?.id as string;

  const [history, setHistory] = useState<HistoryEntry[]>([]);
  const [recipeName, setRecipeName] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!recipeId) return;
    
    // Fetch recipe name
    fetch(`/api/recipes/${recipeId}`)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => {
        if (data.name) setRecipeName(isAr ? (data.nameAr || data.name) : data.name);
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    // Fetch history
    fetch(`/api/recipes/${recipeId}/history`)
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data.error) {
          setError(data.error);
        } else {
          setHistory(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        }
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        setError("Failed to load");
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, [recipeId, isAr]);

  return (
    <main style={{ minHeight: "100vh", background: "#0d1117", color: "#e6edf3", fontFamily: "system-ui, sans-serif", padding: "80px 1rem 120px 1rem", direction: isAr ? "rtl" : "ltr" }}>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 600, margin: "0 auto" }}>
        
        {/* HEADER */}
        <div style={{ marginBottom: "2rem" }}>
          <Link href={`/recipes/${recipeId}`} style={{ color: "#8b949e", textDecoration: "none", fontSize: "14px", fontWeight: 500, display: "inline-block", marginBottom: "1rem" }}>
            {isAr ? ar.history.backToRecipe : "← Back to Recipe"}
          </Link>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: "2rem", fontWeight: 700, margin: 0, letterSpacing: "-0.02em" }}>
            {isAr ? ar.history.title : "Recipe History"}
          </h1>
          <p style={{ color: "#8b949e", fontSize: "15px", margin: "8px 0 0 0" }}>
            {recipeName && <strong style={{ color: "#e6edf3" }}>{recipeName}</strong>}
            {" — "}
            {isAr ? ar.history.sub : "All the days this recipe was made"}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* HISTORY LIST */}
        <div style={{ background: "#161b22", border: "1px solid #30363d", borderRadius: "16px", padding: "24px", boxShadow: "0 10px 30px -10px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)" }}>
          
          {loading ? (
            <div style={{ color: "#8b949e" }}>{isAr ? ar.history.loading : "Loading..."}</div>
          ) : error ? (
            <div style={{ color: "#ff6b4a" }}>{error}</div>
          ) : history.length === 0 ? (
            <div style={{ color: "#8b949e", textAlign: "center", padding: "2rem 0" }}>
              {isAr ? ar.history.empty : "This recipe has never been made yet."}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "12px" }}>
              {history.map((entry, index) => (
                <Link 
                  key={entry.id} 
                  href={`/calendar/${entry.date}`}
                  style={{
                    display: "flex",
                    alignItems: "center",
                    justifyContent: "space-between",
                    padding: "16px",
                    background: "#0d1117",
                    border: "1px solid #30363d",
                    borderRadius: "10px",
                    textDecoration: "none",
                    color: "inherit",
                    transition: "all 0.15s",
                  }}
                  onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "#8b949e"; }}
                  onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "#30363d"; }}
                >
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "12px" }}>
                    <div style={{ 
                      width: "32px", height: "32px", borderRadius: "50%", 
                      background: "rgba(255, 107, 74, 0.1)", color: "#ff6b4a", 
                      display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", 
                      fontWeight: 700, fontSize: "14px" 
                    }}>
                      {history.length - index}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "2px" }}>
                      <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, fontSize: "15px" }}>{entry.date}</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: "13px", color: "#8b949e", textTransform: "capitalize" }}>
                        {isAr ? ar.history.slots[entry.slot] : entry.slot}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ color: "#8b949e", fontSize: "13px", fontWeight: 500 }}>
                    {isAr ? ar.history.viewDay : "View Day"} →
                  </div>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/recipes/[id]/history/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/recipes/[id]/page.tsx ---
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes, ingredients, instructions, settings, mealPlan } from "@/db/schema";
import { eq, asc, desc } from "drizzle-orm";
import RecipeDetailClient from "@/components/RecipeDetailClient";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import type { Metadata } from "next";

import { getRecipeImage } from "@/lib/get-recipe-image";
export async function generateMetadata({ 
  params, searchParams 
}: { 
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
  searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>;
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { id } = await params;
  const search = await searchParams;
  const numericId = Number(id);
  if (Number.isNaN(numericId)) return { title: "Recipe Not Found" };

  const db = getDb();
  const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, numericId)).limit(1);
  if (!recipe) return { title: "Recipe Not Found" };

  const settingsRows = await db.select().from(settings);
  const s = Object.fromEntries(settingsRows.map((r) => [r.key, r.value]));

  const isToday = "today" in search;
  const customTitle = s.today_text || "Hicham's Main Dish of the day";
  
  const title = isToday ? `${customTitle} - ${recipe.name}` : recipe.name;
  const description = recipe.description || "View this recipe on Menu.";
  const image = getRecipeImage(recipe) || "https://food-menu-app-xxww.onrender.com/icon-192.png";

  return {
    title,
    description,
    openGraph: { title, description, images: [{ url: image }], type: "article" },
    twitter: { card: "summary_large_image", title, description, images: [image] },
  };
}

async function RecipeDetail({ id, isToday }: { id: number; isToday: boolean; }) {
  const db = getDb();
  
  const settingsRows = await db.select().from(settings);
  const s = Object.fromEntries(settingsRows.map((r) => [r.key, r.value]));

  const [recipe] = await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!recipe) notFound();

  const customTitle = s.today_text || "Hicham's Main Dish of the day";

  const displayRecipe = isToday 
    ? { 
        ...recipe, 
        name: (
          <>
            <span 
              className="custom-color-text"
              style={{ 
                "--custom-light": s.today_color_light || "#e2543a",
                "--custom-dark": s.today_color_dark || "#ff6b4a"
              } as React.CSSProperties}
            >
              {customTitle}
            </span>
            <br />
            <span 
              className="custom-color-text"
              style={{ 
                "--custom-light": s.recipe_name_color_light || "#52525b",
                "--custom-dark": s.recipe_name_color_dark || "#a1a1aa",
                fontSize: "0.75em", 
                fontWeight: 400, 
                display: "block", 
                marginTop: 8 
              } as React.CSSProperties}
            >
              {recipe.name}
            </span>
          </>
        ) 
      } 
    : recipe;

  const history = await db.select({ date: mealPlan.date }).from(mealPlan).where(eq(mealPlan.recipeId, id)).orderBy(desc(mealPlan.date)).limit(1);
  const lastMadeDate = history.length > 0 ? history[0].date : null;

  const ings = await db.select().from(ingredients).where(eq(ingredients.recipeId, id)).orderBy(asc(ingredients.id));
  const steps = await db.select().from(instructions).where(eq(instructions.recipeId, id)).orderBy(asc(instructions.stepNumber));
  const admin = await isAdmin();

  return (
    <RecipeDetailClient
      recipe={displayRecipe as any}
      ingredients={ings}
      instructions={steps}
      isAdmin={admin}
      lastMadeDate={lastMadeDate}
    />
  );
}

function DetailSkeleton() {
  return (
    <>
      <div className="detail-hero" style={{ background: "#e8e2d8", opacity: 0.5 }} />
      <div style={{ height: 40, background: "#e8e2d8", margin: "24px 0", borderRadius: 8, width: "60%" }} />
    </>
  );
}

async function RecipeResolver({ params, searchParams }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }>; searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>; }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const search = await searchParams;
  const numericId = Number(id);
  if (Number.isNaN(numericId)) notFound();
  return <RecipeDetail id={numericId} isToday={"today" in search} />;
}

export default function RecipePage({ params, searchParams }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }>; searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>; }) {
  return (
    <main className="container">
      <Suspense fallback={<DetailSkeleton />}>
        <RecipeResolver params={params} searchParams={searchParams} />
      </Suspense>
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/recipes/page.tsx ---
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { getDb } from "@/db";
import { recipes } from "@/db/schema";
import { desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import HomeClient from "@/components/HomeClient";
import Spinner from "@/components/Spinner";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";

async function RecipeList() {
  await connection();
  const db = getDb();
  const admin = await isAdmin();
  const all = admin
    ? await db.select().from(recipes).orderBy(desc(recipes.createdAt))
    : await db.select().from(recipes).where(eq(recipes.isHidden, 0)).orderBy(desc(recipes.createdAt));
  return <HomeClient recipes={all} />;
}


export default function RecipesPage() {
  return (
    <main className="container">
      <Suspense fallback={<Spinner />}>
        <RecipeList />
      </Suspense>
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/recipients/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { useModal } from "@/components/ModalProvider";

type Recipient = { id: number; phoneNumber: string | null; name: string | null; excluded: number; };
type Group = { id: number; name: string; phones: string[]; };

export default function RecipientsPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const router = useRouter();
  const { show, confirm } = useModal();
  
  const [recipients, setRecipients] = useState<Recipient[]>([]);
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<Group[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  
  const [newGroupName, setNewGroupName] = useState("");
  const [selectedGroupPhones, setSelectedGroupPhones] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [showGroupForm, setShowGroupForm] = useState(false);

  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [editName, setEditName] = useState("");
  const [editPhone, setEditPhone] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => {
        if (!data.admin) router.push("/login");
        else loadData();
      });
  }, [router]);

  function loadData() {
    Promise.all([
      fetch("/api/recipients").then(r => r.json()),
      fetch("/api/recipients/groups").then(r => r.json())
    ]).then(([recs, grps]) => {
      setRecipients(Array.isArray(recs) ? recs.filter((r: Recipient) => r.phoneNumber) : []);
      setGroups(Array.isArray(grps) ? grps : []);
      setLoading(false);
    });
  }

  async function createGroup() {
    if (!newGroupName.trim() || selectedGroupPhones.length === 0) return;
    await fetch("/api/recipients/groups", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ name: newGroupName, phones: selectedGroupPhones }),
    });
    setNewGroupName("");
    setSelectedGroupPhones([]);
    setShowGroupForm(false);
    loadData();
  }

  async function deleteGroup(id: number) {
    const ok = await confirm({ 
      message: isAr ? ar.groups.deleteConfirm : "Delete this group?", 
      confirmText: isAr ? ar.modal.deleteBtn : "Delete", 
      tone: "danger" 
    });
    if (!ok) return;
    
    await fetch(`/api/recipients/groups?id=${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    loadData();
  }

  async function handleDeleteRecipient(id: number) {
    const ok = await confirm({ 
      message: isAr ? ar.groups.deleteConfirm : "Delete this recipient?", 
      confirmText: isAr ? ar.modal.deleteBtn : "Delete", 
      tone: "danger" 
    });
    if (!ok) return;
    
    await fetch(`/api/recipients?id=${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    loadData();
  }

  async function handleSaveRecipient(id: number) {
    if (!editPhone.trim()) {
      show({ 
        message: isAr ? ar.groups.phoneRequired : "Phone number cannot be empty.", 
        tone: "danger" 
      });
      return;
    }
    await fetch("/api/recipients", {
      method: "PUT",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ id, name: editName, phoneNumber: editPhone }),
    });
    setEditId(null);
    loadData();
  }

  function toggleGroupPhone(phone: string) {
    setSelectedGroupPhones(prev => prev.includes(phone) ? prev.filter(p => p !== phone) : [...prev, phone]);
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.loading : "Loading..."}</div>;

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{isAr ? ar.groups.recipientsTitle : "Recipients"}</h1>
      </div>
      
      {/* GROUPS SECTION */}
      <div className="form-card" style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <h2 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 18 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.title : "Groups"}</h2>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => setShowGroupForm(!showGroupForm)}>
            {isAr ? ar.groups.newGroup : "+ New Group"}
          </button>
        </div>

        {showGroupForm && (
          <div style={{ padding: 16, background: "var(--bg)", borderRadius: 8, marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <input type="text" placeholder={isAr ? ar.groups.groupName : "Group Name"} value={newGroupName} onChange={(e) => setNewGroupName(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8, marginBottom: 12, borderRadius: 6, border: "1px solid var(--line)" }} />
            <div style={{ maxHeight: 200, overflowY: "auto", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 6, padding: 8, marginBottom: 12 }}>
              {recipients.map(r => (
                <label key={r.id} style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, padding: 4 }}>
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={selectedGroupPhones.includes(r.phoneNumber!)} onChange={() => toggleGroupPhone(r.phoneNumber!)} />
                  {r.name || (isAr ? ar.groups.unnamed : "Unnamed")} ({r.phoneNumber})
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary" onClick={createGroup}>{isAr ? ar.groups.createBtn : "Create Group"}</button>
          </div>
        )}

        {groups.length > 0 ? (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 8 }}>
            {groups.map(g => (
              <div key={g.id} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, padding: "8px 12px", background: "var(--surface-2)", borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>👥 {g.name}</span>
                <span style={{ color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 12 }}>({g.phones.length})</span>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => deleteGroup(g.id)} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: "var(--accent)", cursor: "pointer" }}>✕</button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 13 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.noGroups : "No groups yet."}</p>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* RECIPIENTS TABLE WITH EDIT/DELETE AND EXCLUDED */}
      <div className="form-card" style={{ padding: 0, overflow: "hidden" }}>
        <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 14 }}>
          <thead style={{ background: "var(--bg)", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
            <tr>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.recipientName : "Name"}</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.recipientPhone : "Phone Number"}</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.excluded : "Excluded"}</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "end", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.actions : "Actions"}</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {recipients.map((r) => (
              <tr key={r.id} style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                {editId === r.id ? (
                  <>
                    <td style={{ padding: 8 }}>
                      <input type="text" value={editName} onChange={(e) => setEditName(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 6 }} placeholder={isAr ? ar.groups.recipientName : "Name"} />
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 8 }}>
                      <input type="text" value={editPhone} onChange={(e) => setEditPhone(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 6 }} placeholder={isAr ? ar.groups.recipientPhone : "Phone"} />
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 8, textAlign: "center" }}>—</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 8, textAlign: "end" }}>
                      <button onClick={() => handleSaveRecipient(r.id)} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ padding: "4px 10px", fontSize: 12, marginRight: 4 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.save : "Save"}</button>
                      <button onClick={() => setEditId(null)} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "4px 10px", fontSize: 12 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.cancel : "Cancel"}</button>
                    </td>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <td style={{ padding: 12 }}>{r.name || <span style={{ color: "var(--muted)", fontStyle: "italic" }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.unnamed : "Unnamed"}</span>}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 12 }}>{r.phoneNumber}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center" }}>
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={r.excluded === 1}
                        onChange={async (e) => {
                          await fetch("/api/recipients", {
                            method: "PUT",
                            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                            body: JSON.stringify({
                              id: r.id,
                              name: r.name || "",
                              phoneNumber: r.phoneNumber || "",
                              excluded: e.target.checked,
                            }),
                          });
                          loadData();
                        }}
                        style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "end" }}>
                      <button onClick={() => { setEditId(r.id); setEditName(r.name || ""); setEditPhone(r.phoneNumber || ""); }} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "4px 10px", fontSize: 12, marginRight: 4 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.edit : "Edit"}</button>
                      <button onClick={() => handleDeleteRecipient(r.id)} className="btn btn-danger" style={{ padding: "4px 10px", fontSize: 12 }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.delete : "Delete"}</button>
                    </td>
                  </>
                )}
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/recipients/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/replies/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";

type Voter = {
  phone: string;
  name: string | null;
  respondedAt: string;
  answer: "yes" | "no";
};

type PendingVoter = {
  phone: string;
  name: string | null;
};

type Reply = {
  pollId: number;
  question: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
  lastVoteAt: string | null;
  targetPhone: string;
  inviteeCount: number;
  counts: { yes: number; no: number; pending: number; total: number };
  voters: Voter[];
  pending: PendingVoter[];
};

export default function RepliesPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const router = useRouter();

  const [items, setItems] = useState<Reply[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (!data.admin) {
          router.push("/login");
        } else {
          setIsAdmin(true);
          loadReplies();
        }
      })
      .catch(() => router.push("/login"));
  }, [router]);

  function loadReplies() {
    setLoading(true);
    fetch("/api/replies")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }

  function fmtTime(iso: string | null): string {
    if (!iso) return "";
    try {
      return new Date(iso).toLocaleString();
    } catch {
      return iso;
    }
  }

  if (!isAdmin && loading) {
    return (
      <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40 }}>
        <p className="helper">{lang === "ar" ? "جاري التحقق..." : "Checking access..."}</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{lang === "ar" ? "الردود" : "Replies"}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">
        {lang === "ar"
          ? "كل الردود على الاستطلاعات — اضغط لعرض من صوت"
          : "All poll responses — click to see who voted"}
      </p>

      {loading ? (
        <p className="helper">{lang === "ar" ? "جاري التحميل..." : "Loading..."}</p>
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{lang === "ar" ? "لا ردود بعد" : "No replies yet"}</h2>
          <p>{lang === "ar" ? "أرسل استطلاعاً لبدء جمع الردود." : "Send a poll to start collecting replies."}</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16, marginTop: 24 }}>
          {items.map((r) => {
            const isOpen = openId === r.pollId;
            return (
              <div
                key={r.pollId}
                onClick={() => setOpenId(isOpen ? null : r.pollId)}
                style={{
                  background: "var(--surface)",
                  border: "1px solid var(--line)",
                  borderRadius: "var(--radius)",
                  padding: 16,
                  cursor: "pointer",
                  transition: "border-color 0.15s",
                }}
                onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "var(--line-strong)"; }}
                onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "var(--line)"; }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: 16 }}>
                  <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", marginBottom: 6 }}>
                      <span style={{
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        padding: "2px 8px",
                        borderRadius: 999,
                        background: "var(--accent-soft)",
                        color: "var(--accent)",
                        border: "1px solid var(--accent)",
                      }}>
                        📊 {lang === "ar" ? "استطلاع" : "Poll"}
                      </span>
                      {!r.isOpen && (
                        <span style={{
                          fontSize: 10,
                          fontWeight: 700,
                          padding: "2px 8px",
                          borderRadius: 999,
                          background: "var(--surface-2)",
                          color: "var(--muted)",
                          border: "1px solid var(--line)",
                        }}>
                          {lang === "ar" ? "مغلق" : "Closed"}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--ink)" }}>
                        {r.question}
                      </h3>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center", fontSize: 13 }}>
                      <span>
                        <strong style={{ color: "var(--olive)", fontSize: 15 }}>{r.counts.yes}</strong>{" "}
                        <span style={{ color: "var(--ink-soft)" }}>{lang === "ar" ? "نعم" : "Yes"}</span>
                      </span>
                      <span>
                        <strong style={{ color: "var(--accent)", fontSize: 15 }}>{r.counts.no}</strong>{" "}
                        <span style={{ color: "var(--ink-soft)" }}>{lang === "ar" ? "لا" : "No"}</span>
                      </span>
                      <span>
                        <strong style={{ color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 15 }}>{r.counts.pending}</strong>{" "}
                        <span style={{ color: "var(--ink-soft)" }}>{lang === "ar" ? "معلق" : "Pending"}</span>
                      </span>
                      <span style={{ marginInlineStart: "auto", fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                        {r.counts.total} {lang === "ar" ? "مدعو" : r.counts.total === 1 ? "invitee" : "invitees"}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {r.lastVoteAt && (
                      <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)", marginTop: 6 }}>
                        {lang === "ar" ? "آخر صوت" : "Last vote"}: {fmtTime(r.lastVoteAt)}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <Link
                    href={`/poll/${r.pollId}`}
                    onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    className="btn btn-ghost"
                    style={{ fontSize: 11, flexShrink: 0 }}
                  >
                    📊 {lang === "ar" ? "فتح" : "Open"}
                  </Link>
                </div>

                {isOpen && (
                  <div
                    onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    style={{
                      marginTop: 14,
                      paddingTop: 14,
                      borderTop: "1px solid var(--line)",
                      display: "flex",
                      flexDirection: "column",
                      gap: 14,
                    }}
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "0.06em", color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 8 }}>
                        {lang === "ar" ? "الذين صوتوا" : "Voters"} ({r.voters.length})
                      </div>
                      {r.voters.length === 0 ? (
                        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontStyle: "italic" }}>
                          {lang === "ar" ? "لا أحد صوت بعد" : "Nobody has voted yet"}
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 4 }}>
                          {r.voters.map((v) => (
                            <div
                              key={v.phone + v.respondedAt}
                              style={{
                                display: "flex",
                                alignItems: "center",
                                gap: 10,
                                padding: "6px 10px",
                                background: v.answer === "yes" ? "var(--olive-soft)" : "var(--accent-soft)",
                                border: "1px solid var(--line)",
                                borderRadius: 6,
                                fontSize: 12,
                              }}
                            >
                              <span style={{
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                color: v.answer === "yes" ? "var(--olive)" : "var(--accent)",
                                minWidth: 32,
                              }}>
                                {v.answer === "yes" ? (lang === "ar" ? "نعم" : "Yes") : (lang === "ar" ? "لا" : "No")}
                              </span>
                              <span style={{ flex: 1, color: "var(--ink)", fontWeight: 500 }}>
                                {v.name || (lang === "ar" ? "بدون اسم" : "Unnamed")}
                              </span>
                              <span style={{ color: "var(--muted)", fontVariantNumeric: "tabular-nums" }}>
                                {v.phone}
                              </span>
                              <span style={{ color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 11, minWidth: 130, textAlign: "end" }}>
                                {fmtTime(v.respondedAt)}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "0.06em", color: "var(--muted)", marginBottom: 8 }}>
                        {lang === "ar" ? "لم يصوتوا" : "Pending"} ({r.pending.length})
                      </div>
                      {r.pending.length === 0 ? (
                        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontStyle: "italic" }}>
                          {lang === "ar" ? "صوت الجميع" : "Everyone has voted"}
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 6 }}>
                          {r.pending.map((p) => (
                            <span
                              key={p.phone}
                              style={{
                                fontSize: 12,
                                padding: "4px 10px",
                                background: "var(--surface-2)",
                                border: "1px solid var(--line)",
                                borderRadius: 6,
                                color: "var(--ink-soft)",
                              }}
                            >
                              {p.name || (lang === "ar" ? "بدون اسم" : "Unnamed")}
                              <span style={{ color: "var(--muted)", marginInlineStart: 6 }}>{p.phone}</span>
                            </span>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div style={{
                      padding: 14,
                      background: "var(--bg)",
                      border: "1px dashed var(--line-strong)",
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      color: "var(--muted)",
                      textAlign: "center",
                    }}>
                      💬 {lang === "ar" ? "الدردشة قادمة قريباً" : "Chat coming soon"}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/sendpush/page.tsx ---
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import AdminSender from "@/components/AdminSender";

async function AuthGate({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) redirect("/login");
  return <>{children}</>;
}

function Loading() {
  return <div className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40 }}>Loading...</div>;
}

export default function AdminPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<Loading />}>
      <AuthGate>
        <AdminSender />
      </AuthGate>
    </Suspense>
  );
}
