--- FILE: src/app/settings/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import ImageUpload from "@/components/ImageUpload";
import VideoUpload from "@/components/VideoUpload";
import { useModal } from "@/components/ModalProvider";

type Settings = {
  today_text: string;
  today_color_light: string;
  today_color_dark: string;
  recipe_name_color_light: string;
  recipe_name_color_dark: string;
  inbox_title_color_light: string;
  inbox_title_color_dark: string;
  inbox_recipe_color_light: string;
  inbox_recipe_color_dark: string;
  badge_location_color_light: string;
  badge_location_color_dark: string;
  badge_time_color_light: string;
  badge_time_color_dark: string;
  cook_title_size: string;
  cook_title_color_light: string;
  cook_title_color_dark: string;
  cook_instruction_size: string;
  cook_instruction_color_light: string;
  cook_instruction_color_dark: string;
  logo_url: string;
  favicon_url: string;
  background_url: string;
  app_name: string;
  welcome_title: string;
  welcome_button: string;
  no_dish_image_url: string;
  no_dish_video_url: string;
  title_size: string;
  title_color_light: string;
  title_color_dark: string;
  label_size: string;
  label_color_light: string;
  label_color_dark: string;
};

const DEFAULT: Settings = {
  today_text: "Hicham's Main Dish of the day",
  today_color_light: "#e2543a",
  today_color_dark: "#ff6b4a",
  recipe_name_color_light: "#52525b",
  recipe_name_color_dark: "#a1a1aa",
  inbox_title_color_light: "#e2543a",
  inbox_title_color_dark: "#ff6b4a",
  inbox_recipe_color_light: "#52525b",
  inbox_recipe_color_dark: "#a1a1aa",
  badge_location_color_light: "#e2543a",
  badge_location_color_dark: "#ff6b4a",
  badge_time_color_light: "#17171a",
  badge_time_color_dark: "#ededed",
  cook_title_size: "28",
  cook_title_color_light: "#FFD700",
  cook_title_color_dark: "#FFD700",
  cook_instruction_size: "32",
  cook_instruction_color_light: "#ededed",
  cook_instruction_color_dark: "#ededed",
  logo_url: "",
  favicon_url: "",
  background_url: "",
  app_name: "Menu",
  welcome_title: "Hictra Recipes",
  welcome_button: "Enter",
  no_dish_image_url: "",
  no_dish_video_url: "",
  title_size: "40",
  title_color_light: "#17171a",
  title_color_dark: "#ededed",
  label_size: "11",
  label_color_light: "#71717a",
  label_color_dark: "#a1a1aa",
};

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const router = useRouter();
  const { show } = useModal();
  
  const [config, setConfig] = useState<Settings>(DEFAULT);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [devices, setDevices] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<"branding" | "typography" | "colors" | "noDish">("branding");

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/recipes").then(r => r.json()).then(d => setRecipes(Array.isArray(d) ? d : []));
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (!data.admin) router.push("/login");
        else load();
      });
  }, [router]);

  function load() {
    fetch("/api/visitors").then(r => r.json()).then(d => setDevices(d.devices || []));
    fetch("/api/settings")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...data }));
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    setSaving(true);
    await fetch("/api/settings", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(config),
    });
    setSaving(false);
    show({ 
      message: isAr ? ar.messages.settingsSaved : "Settings saved!", 
      tone: "info" 
    });
  }

  const ColorPair = ({ label, lightKey, darkKey }: { label: string; lightKey: keyof Settings; darkKey: keyof Settings }) => (
    <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
      <label>{label}</label>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12 }}>
        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--muted)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>Light</span>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
            <input type="color" value={config[lightKey]} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, [lightKey]: e.target.value })} style={{ width: 40, height: 36, padding: 0, border: "none", background: "none" }} />
            <input type="text" value={config[lightKey]} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, [lightKey]: e.target.value })} style={{ flex: 1 }} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--muted)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>Dark</span>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
            <input type="color" value={config[darkKey]} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, [darkKey]: e.target.value })} style={{ width: 40, height: 36, padding: 0, border: "none", background: "none" }} />
            <input type="text" value={config[darkKey]} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, [darkKey]: e.target.value })} style={{ flex: 1 }} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  if (loading) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>Loading...</div>;

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 650 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.title : "Settings"}</h1>
      </div>

      {/* TAB MENU */}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, overflowX: "auto", paddingBottom: 8, marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <button type="button" className={`btn ${activeTab === "branding" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`} onClick={() => setActiveTab("branding")}>
          {isAr ? ar.settingsPage.branding : "Branding"}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className={`btn ${activeTab === "typography" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`} onClick={() => setActiveTab("typography")}>
          {isAr ? ar.settingsPage.typography : "Typography"}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className={`btn ${activeTab === "colors" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`} onClick={() => setActiveTab("colors")}>
          {isAr ? ar.settingsPage.datesColors : "Dates & Colors"}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className={`btn ${activeTab === "noDish" ? "btn-primary" : "btn-ghost"}`} onClick={() => setActiveTab("noDish")}>
          {isAr ? ar.settingsPage.noDishTab : "No Dish Today"}
        </button>
        
      </div>

      <div className="form-card">
        
        {activeTab === "branding" && (
          <div>
            <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.logo : "Logo"}</label><ImageUpload onUploaded={(url) => setConfig({ ...config, logo_url: url })} initialUrl={config.logo_url || undefined} /></div>
            <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.favicon : "Favicon"}</label><ImageUpload onUploaded={(url) => setConfig({ ...config, favicon_url: url })} initialUrl={config.favicon_url || undefined} /></div>
            <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.background : "Background image"}</label><ImageUpload onUploaded={(url) => setConfig({ ...config, background_url: url })} initialUrl={config.background_url || undefined} /></div>
            <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.welcomeTitle : "Welcome title"}</label><input type="text" value={config.welcome_title} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, welcome_title: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10 }} /></div>
            <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.welcomeButton : "Enter button text"}</label><input type="text" value={config.welcome_button} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, welcome_button: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10 }} /></div>
            <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.appName : "App Name"}</label><input type="text" value={config.app_name || ""} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, app_name: e.target.value })} placeholder="e.g. Hictra Recipes" style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10 }} /><p className="helper" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.appNameHint : "Shows in browser tab and shared messages."}</p></div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === "typography" && (
          <div>
            <div className="form-section" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
              <h2>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.pageTitles : "Page Titles"}</h2>
              <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.titleSize : "Title font size (px)"}</label><input type="number" min="24" max="96" value={config.title_size} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, title_size: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10 }} /></div>
              <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.titleColor : "Title color"}</label><div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12 }}><div style={{ flex: 1 }}><span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--muted)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.light : "Light"}</span><input type="color" value={config.title_color_light} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, title_color_light: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", height: 36 }} /></div><div style={{ flex: 1 }}><span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--muted)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.dark : "Dark"}</span><input type="color" value={config.title_color_dark} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, title_color_dark: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", height: 36 }} /></div></div></div>
            </div>

            <hr style={{ borderColor: "var(--line)", margin: "24px 0" }} />

            <div className="form-section" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
              <h2>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.todayText : "Today Text"}</h2>
              <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.todayText : '"Today" title text'}</label><input type="text" value={config.today_text} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, today_text: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10 }} /></div>
            </div>

            <hr style={{ borderColor: "var(--line)", margin: "24px 0" }} />

            <div className="form-section" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
              <h2>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.labelsStyle : "Labels Style"}</h2>
              <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.labelSize : "Label font size (px)"}</label><input type="number" min="8" max="24" value={config.label_size} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, label_size: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10 }} /></div>
              <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.labelColor : "Label color"}</label><div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12 }}><div style={{ flex: 1 }}><span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--muted)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.light : "Light"}</span><input type="color" value={config.label_color_light} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, label_color_light: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", height: 36 }} /></div><div style={{ flex: 1 }}><span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--muted)", display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.dark : "Dark"}</span><input type="color" value={config.label_color_dark} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, label_color_dark: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", height: 36 }} /></div></div></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === "colors" && (
          <div>
            <ColorPair label={isAr ? ar.settingsPage.mainTitleColor : "Main Title Color (?today)"} lightKey="today_color_light" darkKey="today_color_dark" />
            <ColorPair label={isAr ? ar.settingsPage.recipeNameColor : "Recipe Name Color"} lightKey="recipe_name_color_light" darkKey="recipe_name_color_dark" />
            <hr style={{ borderColor: "var(--line)", margin: "16px 0" }} />
            <ColorPair label={isAr ? ar.settingsPage.inboxTitleColor : "Inbox: Main Title"} lightKey="inbox_title_color_light" darkKey="inbox_title_color_dark" />
            <ColorPair label={isAr ? ar.settingsPage.inboxRecipeColor : "Inbox: Recipe Name"} lightKey="inbox_recipe_color_light" darkKey="inbox_recipe_color_dark" />
            <hr style={{ borderColor: "var(--line)", margin: "16px 0" }} />
            <h3 style={{ fontSize: 14, marginBottom: 12, color: "var(--ink)" }}>{isAr ? "ألوان الشارات" : "Badge Colors"}</h3>
            <ColorPair label={isAr ? "لون الموقع" : "Location Badge Color"} lightKey="badge_location_color_light" darkKey="badge_location_color_dark" />
            <ColorPair label={isAr ? "لون الوقت" : "Time Badge Color"} lightKey="badge_time_color_light" darkKey="badge_time_color_dark" />
            
            <hr style={{ borderColor: "var(--line)", margin: "16px 0" }} />
            <h3 style={{ fontSize: 14, marginBottom: 12, color: "var(--ink)" }}>{isAr ? "وضع الطبخ (Cook Mode)" : "Cook Mode"}</h3>
            <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <label>{isAr ? "حجم العنوان (بكسل)" : "Title Size (px)"}</label>
              <input type="number" min="16" max="72" value={config.cook_title_size} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, cook_title_size: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10 }} />
            </div>
            <ColorPair label={isAr ? "لون عنوان الطبخ" : "Cook Title Color"} lightKey="cook_title_color_light" darkKey="cook_title_color_dark" />

            <hr style={{ borderColor: "var(--line)", margin: "16px 0" }} />
            <div className="field" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <label>{isAr ? "حجم نص الخطوات (بكسل)" : "Instruction Text Size (px)"}</label>
              <input type="number" min="16" max="72" value={config.cook_instruction_size || "32"} onChange={(e) => setConfig({ ...config, cook_instruction_size: e.target.value })} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10 }} />
            </div>
            <ColorPair label={isAr ? "لون نص الخطوات" : "Instruction Text Color"} lightKey="cook_instruction_color_light" darkKey="cook_instruction_color_dark" />


          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === "noDish" && (
          <div>
            <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.noDishImage : "No dish today image"}</label><ImageUpload onUploaded={(url) => setConfig({ ...config, no_dish_image_url: url })} initialUrl={config.no_dish_image_url || undefined} /></div>
            <div className="field"><label>{isAr ? ar.settingsPage.noDishVideo : "No dish today video"}</label><VideoUpload onUploaded={(url) => setConfig({ ...config, no_dish_video_url: url })} initialUrl={config.no_dish_video_url || undefined} /></div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div style={{ marginTop: 24, paddingTop: 24, borderTop: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 12 }}>
            {saving ? (isAr ? ar.settingsPage.saving : "Saving...") : (isAr ? ar.settingsPage.save : "Save Changes")}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

--- FILE: src/app/visitors/page.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { useModal } from "@/components/ModalProvider";

type Visitor = { id: string; phone: string | null; name: string | null; visits: number; lastSeen: string; lastPage: string | null };

export default function VisitorsPage() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const router = useRouter();
  const { show, confirm } = useModal();
  
  const [visitors, setVisitors] = useState<Visitor[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Edit state
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [editPhone, setEditPhone] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (!data.admin) router.push("/login");
        else loadVisitors();
      });
  }, [router]);

  function loadVisitors() {
    setLoading(true);
    fetch("/api/visitors", { cache: "no-store" })
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setVisitors(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }

  async function handleSave(id: string) {
    if (!editPhone.trim()) {
      show({ message: isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.phoneRequired : "Phone number is required", tone: "danger" });
      return;
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/visitors", {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id, phone: editPhone.trim() }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (!res.ok) {
        show({ message: isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.updateFailed : (data.error || "Failed to update"), tone: "danger" });
        return;
      }
      
      setEditId(null);
      loadVisitors();
    } catch {
      show({ message: isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.updateFailed : "Error updating visitor", tone: "danger" });
    }
  }

  async function deleteVisitor(id: string) {
    const ok = await confirm({ 
      message: isAr ? ar.messages.deleteLogConfirm : "Delete this log?",
      confirmText: isAr ? ar.modal.deleteBtn : "Delete",
      tone: "danger"
    });
    if (!ok) return;
    
    await fetch("/api/visitors?id=" + id, { method: "DELETE" });
    loadVisitors();
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="container" style={{ padding: 40 }}>{isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.loading : "Loading..."}</div>;

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 900 }}>
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.title : "Devices Log"}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">
        {isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.subtitle : "List of all devices that opened the app"}
      </p>

      <div className="form-card" style={{ padding: 0, overflow: "auto" }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", padding: "16px", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)", background: "var(--surface-2)" }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600 }}>
            {visitors.length} {isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.totalDevices : "Devices"}
          </span>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: 12 }} onClick={loadVisitors}>
            ↻ {isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.refresh : "Refresh"}
          </button>
        </div>

        <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 14, minWidth: 700 }}>
          <thead style={{ background: "var(--bg)", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
            <tr>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>#</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.name : "Name"}</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.phone : "Phone"}</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? "الزيارات" : "Visits"}</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? "آخر صفحة" : "Last Page"}</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "end", fontWeight: 600 }}>{isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.lastSeen : "Last Seen"}</th>
              <th style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600 }}></th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {visitors.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={7} style={{ padding: 24, textAlign: "center", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                  {isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.noDevices : "No devices registered yet."}
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              visitors.map((v, i) => (
                <tr key={v.id} style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                  <td style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontVariantNumeric: "tabular-nums" }}>{i + 1}</td>
                  
                  {editId === v.id ? (
                    <>
                      <td style={{ padding: 8, color: "var(--muted)", fontStyle: "italic" }}>{isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.linkPhone : "Link Phone"}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 8 }}>
                        <input 
                          type="text" 
                          placeholder={isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.phoneNumberPlaceholder : "Phone number"} 
                          value={editPhone} 
                          onChange={(e) => setEditPhone(e.target.value)}
                          style={{ width: "100%", padding: "6px 10px", borderRadius: 6, border: "1px solid var(--line)", background: "var(--input-bg)", color: "var(--ink)", fontSize: 13 }}
                          autoFocus
                        />
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--accent)" }}>{v.visits || 0}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "start", color: "var(--muted)" }}>—</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "end", color: "var(--muted)" }}>—</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 8, textAlign: "center" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => handleSave(v.id)} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ padding: "4px 10px", fontSize: 12, marginRight: 4 }}>
                          {isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.save : "Save"}
                        </button>
                        <button onClick={() => setEditId(null)} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "4px 10px", fontSize: 12 }}>
                          {isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.cancel : "Cancel"}
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <td style={{ padding: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: v.name ? "var(--ink)" : "var(--muted)" }}>
                        {v.name || (isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.unknown : "Unknown")}
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--ink-soft)", fontFamily: "monospace" }}>
                        {v.phone || "-"}
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--accent)" }}>
                        {v.visits || 0}
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--accent)", fontFamily: "monospace", fontSize: 12 }}>
                        {v.lastPage || "-"}
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "end", color: "var(--muted)" }}>
                        {new Date(v.lastSeen).toLocaleString()}
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: 12, textAlign: "center" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => { setEditId(v.id); setEditPhone(v.phone || ""); }} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "4px 10px", fontSize: 12, marginRight: 4 }}>
                          {isAr ? ar.visitorsPage.edit : "Edit"}
                        </button>
                        <button onClick={() => deleteVisitor(v.id)} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: "var(--accent)", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 16, padding: 4 }}>
                          🗑
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </>
                  )}
                </tr>
              ))
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/visitors/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/app/week/WeekClient.tsx ---
"use client";

import Spinner from "@/components/Spinner";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "@/components/LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";

type Slot = "breakfast" | "lunch" | "dinner" | "snack" | "dessert";

type RecipeMini = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  category: string | null;
  categoryAr: string | null;
};

type PlanEntry = {
  id: number;
  date: string;
  slot: Slot;
  position: number;
  recipe: RecipeMini;
};

const SLOTS: { key: Slot; label: string; labelAr: string; color: string }[] = [
  { key: "breakfast", label: "Breakfast", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.breakfast, color: "var(--olive)" },
  { key: "lunch", label: "Lunch", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.lunch, color: "#d97757" },
  { key: "dinner", label: "Dinner", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.dinner, color: "var(--accent)" },
  { key: "snack", label: "Snack", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.snack, color: "#7ba05b" },
  { key: "dessert", label: "Dessert", labelAr: ar.calendar.slots.dessert, color: "#c94e35" },
];

const DAY_NAMES_EN = ["Sunday", "Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday"];
const MONTH_NAMES_EN = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];

function fmt(y: number, m: number, d: number): string {
  return `${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(d).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}
function parseDate(s: string): Date {
  const [y, m, d] = s.split("-").map(Number);
  return new Date(y, m - 1, d);
}
function toStr(d: Date): string {
  return fmt(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate());
}
function mondayOf(date: Date): Date {
  const d = new Date(date);
  const offset = (d.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  d.setDate(d.getDate() - offset);
  d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return d;
}
function addDays(date: Date, n: number): Date {
  const d = new Date(date);
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + n);
  return d;
}
function monthParam(d: Date): string {
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

export default function WeekClient() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const search = useSearchParams();

  const fromParam = search.get("from");
  const weekStart = useMemo(() => {
    if (fromParam && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(fromParam)) {
      return mondayOf(parseDate(fromParam));
    }
    return mondayOf(new Date());
  }, [fromParam]);

  const weekEnd = useMemo(() => addDays(weekStart, 6), [weekStart]);
  const days = useMemo(
    () => Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => addDays(weekStart, i)),
    [weekStart]
  );

  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<PlanEntry[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [logoUrl, setLogoUrl] = useState<string>("");
  const [appName, setAppName] = useState("Menu");
  const [ratings, setRatings] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    const months = new Set([monthParam(weekStart), monthParam(weekEnd)]);
    setLoading(true);
    Promise.all(
      Array.from(months).map((m) =>
        fetch(`/api/meal-plan?month=${m}`)
          .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : { entries: [] }))
          .catch(() => ({ entries: [] }))
      )
    )
      .then((lists) => {
        const merged: PlanEntry[] = [];
        const seen = new Set<number>();
        for (const result of lists) {
          if (!result || !Array.isArray(result.entries)) continue;
          for (const e of result.entries as PlanEntry[]) {
            if (seen.has(e.id)) continue;
            seen.add(e.id);
            merged.push(e);
          }
        }
        setEntries(merged);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));

    fetch("/api/settings")
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((d) => { if (d.logo_url) setLogoUrl(d.logo_url); if (d.app_name) setAppName(d.app_name); })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, [weekStart, weekEnd]);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchRatings() {
      const newRatings: Record<string, number> = {};
      await Promise.all(
        days.map(async (day) => {
          const dateStr = toStr(day);
          try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/reviews/day/${dateStr}`, { cache: "no-store" });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.count > 0 && data.average > 0) {
              newRatings[dateStr] = data.average;
            }
          } catch (e) {}
        })
      );
      setRatings(newRatings);
    }
    fetchRatings();
  }, [days]);

  const byDate = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Map<Slot, PlanEntry[]>>();
    const startStr = toStr(weekStart);
    const endStr = toStr(weekEnd);
    for (const e of entries) {
      if (e.date < startStr || e.date > endStr) continue;
      if (!map.has(e.date)) map.set(e.date, new Map());
      const slotMap = map.get(e.date)!;
      if (!slotMap.has(e.slot)) slotMap.set(e.slot, []);
      slotMap.get(e.slot)!.push(e);
    }
    for (const slotMap of map.values()) {
      for (const list of slotMap.values()) list.sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position);
    }
    return map;
  }, [entries, weekStart, weekEnd]);

  function recipeName(e: PlanEntry): string {
    return isAr ? (e.recipe.nameAr || e.recipe.name) : e.recipe.name;
  }

  function rangeLabel(): string {
    const y1 = weekStart.getFullYear();
    const y2 = weekEnd.getFullYear();
    const m1 = weekStart.getMonth();
    const m2 = weekEnd.getMonth();
    const d1 = weekStart.getDate();
    const d2 = weekEnd.getDate();

    if (isAr) {
      const m1n = ar.calendar.months[m1];
      const m2n = ar.calendar.months[m2];
      if (y1 === y2 && m1 === m2) return `${d1} – ${d2} ${m1n} ${y1}`;
      if (y1 === y2) return `${d1} ${m1n} – ${d2} ${m2n} ${y1}`;
      return `${d1} ${m1n} ${y1} – ${d2} ${m2n} ${y2}`;
    }
    const m1n = MONTH_NAMES_EN[m1];
    const m2n = MONTH_NAMES_EN[m2];
    if (y1 === y2 && m1 === m2) return `${m1n} ${d1} – ${d2}, ${y1}`;
    if (y1 === y2) return `${m1n} ${d1} – ${m2n} ${d2}, ${y1}`;
    return `${m1n} ${d1}, ${y1} – ${m2n} ${d2}, ${y2}`;
  }

  function share() {
    const lines: string[] = [];
    lines.push(`🗓 *${isAr ? ar.weeklySummary.title : ar.weeklySummaryEn.title}*`);
    lines.push(rangeLabel());
    lines.push("");
    for (const day of days) {
      const dateStr = toStr(day);
      const slotMap = byDate.get(dateStr);
      if (!slotMap || slotMap.size === 0) continue;
      const dayName = isAr ? ar.calendar.days[day.getDay()] : DAY_NAMES_EN[day.getDay()];
      lines.push(`*${dayName}*`);
      for (const slot of SLOTS) {
        const list = slotMap.get(slot.key);
        if (!list) continue;
        for (const e of list) {
          lines.push(`  ${isAr ? slot.labelAr : slot.label}: ${recipeName(e)}`);
        }
      }
      lines.push("");
    }
    lines.push(`— ${isAr ? ar.weeklySummary.sharedFrom : ar.weeklySummaryEn.sharedFrom}`);
    window.open("https://wa.me/?text=" + encodeURIComponent(lines.join("\n")), "_blank");
  }

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 80, maxWidth: 760 }}>
      <div className="no-print" style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", marginBottom: 16 }}>
    <Link href="/calendar" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
  {isAr ? ar.calendar.backToCalendar : "← Back to Calendar"}
</Link>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          <Link href={`/week?from=${toStr(addDays(weekStart, -7))}`} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
            {isAr ? ar.weeklySummary.prevWeek : ar.weeklySummaryEn.prevWeek}
          </Link>
          <Link href={`/week?from=${toStr(addDays(weekStart, 7))}`} className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
            {isAr ? ar.weeklySummary.nextWeek : ar.weeklySummaryEn.nextWeek}
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="page-head" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
        <h1>{isAr ? ar.weeklySummary.title : ar.weeklySummaryEn.title}</h1>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">{rangeLabel()}</p>

      <div className="no-print" style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => window.print()}>
          🖨 {isAr ? ar.weeklySummary.print : ar.weeklySummaryEn.print}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary" onClick={share}>
          💬 {isAr ? ar.weeklySummary.share : ar.weeklySummaryEn.share}
        </button>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "center", padding: 40 }}>
  <Spinner />
</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="week-summary">
          {days.map((day) => {
            const dateStr = toStr(day);
            const slotMap = byDate.get(dateStr);
            const dayName = isAr ? ar.calendar.days[day.getDay()] : DAY_NAMES_EN[day.getDay()];
            const hasMeals = slotMap && slotMap.size > 0;

            return (
              <div key={dateStr} className="week-day">
                <Link href={`/calendar/${dateStr}`} className="week-day-name" style={{ textDecoration: "none", color: "var(--accent)" }}>
                  {dayName}
                </Link>
                <div className="week-day-meals" style={{ position: "relative" }}>
                  {ratings[dateStr] && (
                    <span style={{ position: "absolute", top: 0, insetInlineEnd: 0, fontSize: 11, background: "rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.15)", color: "#ffd700", padding: "2px 6px", borderRadius: 4, fontWeight: 600 }}>
                      ⭐ {ratings[dateStr].toFixed(1)}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {!hasMeals && <span className="week-day-empty">—</span>}
                  {hasMeals && SLOTS.map((slot) => {
                    const list = slotMap!.get(slot.key);
                    if (!list || list.length === 0) return null;
                    return (
                      <div key={slot.key} className="week-slot">
                        <span className="week-slot-dot" style={{ background: slot.color }} />
                        <span className="week-slot-label">{isAr ? slot.labelAr : slot.label}:</span>
                        <span className="week-slot-recipes">
                          {list.map((e, idx) => (
                            <span key={e.id}>
                              {idx > 0 && <span className="week-slot-sep"> · </span>}
                              <Link href={`/recipes/${e.recipe.id}`} className="week-recipe-link">
                                {recipeName(e)}
                              </Link>
                            </span>
                          ))}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="week-footer">
        {logoUrl && <img src={logoUrl} alt="" className="week-logo" />}
        {/* <span>{isAr ? appName : `Shared from ${appName}`}</span> */}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/week/WeekClient.tsx ---
