--- FILE: src/app/whatsapp/page.tsx ---
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { isAdmin } from "@/lib/auth";
import WhatsAppSender from "@/components/WhatsAppSender";

async function AuthGate({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  if (!(await isAdmin())) redirect("/login");
  return <>{children}</>;
}

function Loading() {
  return <div className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40 }}>Loading...</div>;
}

export default function WhatsAppPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<Loading />}>
      <AuthGate>
        <WhatsAppSender />
      </AuthGate>
    </Suspense>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/app/whatsapp/page.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/AdminSender.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { useModal } from "./ModalProvider";

type Recipe = { id: number; name: string; nameAr: string | null; imageUrl: string | null; };
type Recipient = { id: number; phoneNumber: string | null; name: string | null; };
type Preset = { id: number; name: string; title: string; body: string; pollQuestion: string | null; };

const LOCATION_PRESETS = ["Parent's house", "Home", "Beach house", "Work", "Other"];

export default function AdminSender() {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const { confirm } = useModal();
  
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<string>("");

  const [title, setTitle] = useState("Menu");
  const [body, setBody] = useState("New recipe added!");
  const [location, setLocation] = useState("");
  const [time, setTime] = useState("");
  const [noDish, setNoDish] = useState(false);
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");

  const [pollEnabled, setPollEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [pollQuestion, setPollQuestion] = useState("");

  const [recipients, setRecipients] = useState<Recipient[]>([]);
  const [selectedPhones, setSelectedPhones] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [phoneSearch, setPhoneSearch] = useState("");
  const [showRecipientDropdown, setShowRecipientDropdown] = useState(false);

  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState<Recipe[]>([]);
  const [recipeSearch, setRecipeSearch] = useState("");
  const [showRecipeDropdown, setShowRecipeDropdown] = useState(false);

  const [presets, setPresets] = useState<Preset[]>([]);
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<{ id: number, name: string, phones: string[] }[]>([]);
  const [showPresetManager, setShowPresetManager] = useState(false);
  const [editingPreset, setEditingPreset] = useState<Preset | null>(null);

  const titleRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
  const bodyRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/recipes", { cache: "no-store" }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => setRecipes(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []));
    fetch("/api/recipients").then(res => res.json()).then(data => setRecipients(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []));
    fetch("/api/recipients/groups").then(res => res.json()).then(data => setGroups(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []));
    fetch("/api/push/presets").then(res => res.json()).then(data => setPresets(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []));
  }, []);

  const filteredRecipients = useMemo(() => {
    if (!phoneSearch.trim()) return recipients.filter(r => r.phoneNumber);
    const lower = phoneSearch.toLowerCase();
    return recipients.filter(r => {
      if (!r.phoneNumber) return false;
      return r.phoneNumber.includes(lower) || (r.name && r.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower));
    });
  }, [recipients, phoneSearch]);

  const filteredRecipes = useMemo(() => {
    if (!recipeSearch.trim()) return recipes;
    const lower = recipeSearch.toLowerCase();
    return recipes.filter(r => r.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower) || (r.nameAr && r.nameAr.includes(lower)));
  }, [recipes, recipeSearch]);

  function togglePhone(phone: string | null) {
    if (!phone) return;
    setSelectedPhones(prev => prev.includes(phone) ? prev.filter(p => p !== phone) : [...prev, phone]);
  }

  function selectRecipe(id: number) {
    setUrl(`/recipes/${id}?today`);
    setRecipeSearch("");
    setShowRecipeDropdown(false);
  }

  function insertName(target: "title" | "body") {
    const ref = target === "title" ? titleRef : bodyRef;
    const setter = target === "title" ? setTitle : setBody;
    const val = target === "title" ? title : body;

    if (!ref.current) return;
    const start = ref.current.selectionStart;
    const end = ref.current.selectionEnd;
    const newValue = val.substring(0, start) + "{name}" + val.substring(end);

    setter(newValue);

    setTimeout(() => {
      ref.current?.focus();
      ref.current?.setSelectionRange(start + 6, start + 6);
    }, 0);
  }

  function insertTodayDate() {
    const today = new Date();
    const yyyy = today.getFullYear();
    const mm = String(today.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const dd = String(today.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    setUrl(`/calendar/${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`);
  }

  function insertYesterdayDate() {
    const yesterday = new Date();
    yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);
    const yyyy = yesterday.getFullYear();
    const mm = String(yesterday.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const dd = String(yesterday.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    setUrl(`/calendar/${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`);
  }

  async function savePreset(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const form = e.target as HTMLFormElement;
    const name = (form.elements.namedItem("presetName") as HTMLInputElement).value;
    const pTitle = (form.elements.namedItem("presetTitle") as HTMLInputElement).value;
    const pBody = (form.elements.namedItem("presetBody") as HTMLInputElement).value;
    const pPoll = (form.elements.namedItem("presetPoll") as HTMLInputElement).value;

    if (!name || !pTitle || !pBody) return;

    const method = editingPreset ? "PUT" : "POST";
    const payload = editingPreset ? { id: editingPreset.id, name, title: pTitle, body: pBody, pollQuestion: pPoll } : { name, title: pTitle, body: pBody, pollQuestion: pPoll };

    await fetch("/api/push/presets", {
      method,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });

    const updated = await fetch("/api/push/presets").then(r => r.json());
    setPresets(updated);
    setEditingPreset(null);
    form.reset();
  }

  async function deletePreset(id: number) {
    const ok = await confirm({ 
      message: isAr ? ar.pushPresets.deleteConfirm : "Delete this preset?",
      confirmText: isAr ? ar.modal.deleteBtn : "Delete",
      tone: "danger"
    });
    if (!ok) return;
    
    await fetch("/api/push/presets?id=" + id, { method: "DELETE" });
    setPresets(presets.filter(p => p.id !== id));
  }

  async function send() {
    if (pollEnabled && !pollQuestion.trim()) {
      setResult(JSON.stringify({ error: "Poll question is required" }, null, 2));
      return;
    }

    setBusy(true);
    setResult("");
    try {
      const target = selectedPhones.length > 0 ? selectedPhones.join(",") : "all";

      const finalUrl = noDish ? "/no-dish-today" : url.trim();
      const finalTitle = noDish && !title.trim() ? "No dish today" : title;
      const finalBody = noDish && !body.trim() ? "Sorry — no dish for today. Check back tomorrow!" : body;
      
      const payload: Record<string, unknown> = { title: finalTitle, body: finalBody, url: finalUrl, targetPhone: target };
      
      if (pollEnabled && pollQuestion.trim()) {
        payload.poll = { question: pollQuestion.trim() };
      }

      if (location.trim()) {
        payload.location = location.trim();
      }

      if (time.trim()) {
        payload.time = time.trim();
      }

      const res = await fetch("/api/push/send", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      setResult(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));

      if (res.ok && data.pollId) {
        setPollEnabled(false);
        setPollQuestion("");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setResult(String(err));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const previewRecipe = useMemo(() => {
    const match = url.match(/\/recipes\/(\d+)/);
    if (match) return recipes.find(r => r.id === parseInt(match[1]));
    return null;
  }, [url, recipes]);

  return (
    <main className="container" style={{ paddingTop: 40 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-end", marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <h1>{isAr ? ar.sender.title : "Send Push Notification"}</h1>
      </div>

      {showPresetManager && (
        <div className="form-card" style={{ marginBottom: 24, background: "var(--surface-2)" }}>
          <h2 style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
            {editingPreset ? (isAr ? ar.pushPresets.editPreset : "Edit Preset") : (isAr ? ar.pushPresets.newPreset : "New Preset")}
          </h2>
          <form onSubmit={savePreset} style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
            <input name="presetName" className="preset-input" placeholder={isAr ? ar.pushPresets.presetName : "Preset Name"} defaultValue={editingPreset?.name} required />
            <input name="presetTitle" className="preset-input" placeholder={isAr ? ar.pushPresets.presetTitle : "Title"} defaultValue={editingPreset?.title} required />
            <input name="presetBody" className="preset-input" placeholder={isAr ? ar.pushPresets.presetBody : "Body"} defaultValue={editingPreset?.body} required />
            <input name="presetPoll" className="preset-input" placeholder="Poll Question (Optional)" defaultValue={editingPreset?.pollQuestion || ""} />
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
              <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary">
                {editingPreset ? (isAr ? ar.pushPresets.updatePreset : "Update") : (isAr ? ar.pushPresets.addPreset : "Add Preset")}
              </button>
              {editingPreset && (
                <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => setEditingPreset(null)}>
                  {isAr ? ar.pushPresets.cancel : "Cancel"}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </form>

          <div style={{ marginTop: 24, borderTop: "1px solid var(--line)", paddingTop: 16 }}>
            <h3 style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>{isAr ? ar.pushPresets.savedPresets : "Saved Presets"}</h3>
            {presets.map(p => (
              <div key={p.id} style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", padding: 8, borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
                <span><strong>{p.name}</strong> — {p.title}</span>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                  <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "4px 8px" }} onClick={() => setEditingPreset(p)}>{isAr ? ar.pushPresets.edit : "Edit"}</button>
                  <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger" style={{ padding: "4px 8px" }} onClick={() => deletePreset(p.id)}>{isAr ? ar.pushPresets.delete : "Delete"}</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="form-card">
        <div className="field" style={{ position: "relative" }}>
          <label>{isAr ? ar.sender.recipients : "Recipients (leave empty for all)"}</label>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 6, padding: 8, border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, background: "var(--bg)", marginBottom: 8 }}>
            {selectedPhones.map(phone => {
              const rec = recipients.find(r => r.phoneNumber === phone);
              return (
                <span key={phone} style={{ display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, padding: "4px 8px", background: "var(--accent-soft)", color: "var(--accent)", borderRadius: 6, fontSize: 12 }}>
                  {rec?.name ? rec.name + " (" + phone + ")" : phone}
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => togglePhone(phone)} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: "inherit", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 14 }}>✕</button>
                </span>
              );
            })}
            {selectedPhones.length === 0 && <span style={{ color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 12, padding: 4 }}>{isAr ? ar.sender.everyone : "Everyone"}</span>}
          </div>
          {groups.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", marginBottom: 12 }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--muted)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center" }}>{isAr ? ar.groups.selectGroup : "Groups:"}</span>
            {groups.map(g => (
              <button key={g.id} type="button" className="chip" onClick={() => setSelectedPhones(Array.from(new Set([...selectedPhones, ...g.phones])))}>
                👥 {g.name} ({g.phones.length})
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
          <input type="text" placeholder={isAr ? ar.sender.searchByName : "Search by name or phone..."} value={phoneSearch} onChange={(e) => { setPhoneSearch(e.target.value); setShowRecipientDropdown(true); }} onFocus={() => setShowRecipientDropdown(true)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8 }} />
          {showRecipientDropdown && phoneSearch.trim() !== "" && (
            <>
              <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, zIndex: 10 }} onClick={() => setShowRecipientDropdown(false)} />
              <div style={{ position: "absolute", top: "100%", left: 0, right: 0, maxHeight: 200, overflowY: "auto", background: "var(--surface-solid)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, zIndex: 20, marginTop: 4, boxShadow: "var(--shadow-lg)" }}>
                {filteredRecipients.length === 0 ? <div style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 13 }}>{isAr ? ar.sender.noRecipients : "No recipients found"}</div> : filteredRecipients.map(r => (
                  <div key={r.id} onClick={() => { togglePhone(r.phoneNumber); setPhoneSearch(""); setShowRecipientDropdown(false); }} style={{ padding: "10px 12px", cursor: "pointer", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)", fontSize: 14, color: "var(--ink)" }} onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "var(--surface-2)"} onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"}>
                    <strong>{r.name || (isAr ? ar.sender.unnamed : "Unnamed")}</strong> <span style={{ color: "var(--muted)" }}>({r.phoneNumber})</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginBottom: 12 }}>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => setShowPresetManager(!showPresetManager)}>
            ⚙️ {isAr ? ar.pushPresets.title : "Manage Presets"}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="field">
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", marginBottom: 12 }}>
          {presets.map(p => (
            <button key={p.id} type="button" className="chip" onClick={() => { setTitle(p.title); setBody(p.body); if (p.pollQuestion) { setPollEnabled(true); setPollQuestion(p.pollQuestion); } else { setPollEnabled(false); setPollQuestion(""); } }}>
              {p.name}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 6 }}>
            <label>{isAr ? ar.sender.titleLabel : "Title"}</label>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "2px 8px", fontSize: 11 }} onClick={() => insertName("title")}>
              + {isAr ? ar.pushPresets.insertName : "{name}"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <textarea ref={titleRef} value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10, borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--line)", background: "var(--input-bg)", color: "var(--ink)", minHeight: 60, resize: "vertical" }} />
        </div>

        <div className="field">
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 6 }}>
            <label>{isAr ? ar.sender.bodyLabel : "Body"}</label>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "2px 8px", fontSize: 11 }} onClick={() => insertName("body")}>
              + {isAr ? ar.pushPresets.insertName : "{name}"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <textarea ref={bodyRef} value={body} onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10, borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--line)", background: "var(--input-bg)", color: "var(--ink)", minHeight: 80, resize: "vertical" }} />
        </div>

        <div className="field">
          <label>{isAr ? ar.sender.locationLabel : "Location (optional)"}</label>
          <input type="text" value={location} onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)} placeholder={isAr ? ar.sender.locationPlaceholder : "Parent's house, Home, Beach house"} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8, marginBottom: 8 }} />
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 6 }}>
            {LOCATION_PRESETS.map(p => (
              <button key={p} type="button" className="chip" onClick={() => setLocation(p)} style={{ fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer" }}>📍 {p}</button>
            ))}
          </div>

        <div className="field">
          <label>{isAr ? "الوقت" : "Time"}</label>
          <input type="time" value={time} onChange={(e) => setTime(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8 }} />
        </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ marginBottom: 16, padding: 14, background: "var(--bg)", border: pollEnabled ? "1px solid var(--accent)" : "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 10 }}>
          <label style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, cursor: "pointer", margin: 0 }}>
            <input type="checkbox" checked={pollEnabled} onChange={(e) => setPollEnabled(e.target.checked)} />
            <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600 }}>📊 {isAr ? ar.sender.addPoll : "Add a poll"}</span>
          </label>
          {pollEnabled && (
            <input type="text" placeholder={isAr ? ar.sender.pollQuestionPlaceholder : "Question..."} value={pollQuestion} onChange={(e) => setPollQuestion(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", marginTop: 12, padding: 8, borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--line)" }} />
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="field" style={{ position: "relative" }}>
          <label>{isAr ? ar.sender.searchRecipe : "Search Recipe (Optional)"}</label>
          <input type="text" placeholder={isAr ? ar.sender.searchRecipePlaceholder : "Type recipe name..."} value={recipeSearch} onChange={(e) => { setRecipeSearch(e.target.value); setShowRecipeDropdown(true); }} onFocus={() => setShowRecipeDropdown(true)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8, marginBottom: 8 }} />
          {showRecipeDropdown && recipeSearch.trim() !== "" && (
            <>
              <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, zIndex: 10 }} onClick={() => setShowRecipeDropdown(false)} />
              <div style={{ position: "absolute", top: "100%", left: 0, right: 0, maxHeight: 200, overflowY: "auto", background: "var(--surface-solid)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, zIndex: 20, marginTop: 4, boxShadow: "var(--shadow-lg)" }}>
                {filteredRecipes.length === 0 ? <div style={{ padding: 12, color: "var(--muted)", fontSize: 13 }}>{isAr ? ar.sender.noRecipes : "No recipes found"}</div> : filteredRecipes.map(r => (
                  <div key={r.id} onClick={() => selectRecipe(r.id)} style={{ padding: "10px 12px", cursor: "pointer", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)", fontSize: 14, color: "var(--ink)" }} onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "var(--surface-2)"} onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.background = "transparent"}>
                    {isAr && r.nameAr ? r.nameAr : r.name}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="field">
          <label style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, cursor: "pointer" }}>
            <input type="checkbox" checked={noDish} onChange={(e) => setNoDish(e.target.checked)} />
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>🍽 {isAr ? ar.sender.noDishLabel : "No dish today"}</span>
          </label>
        </div>

        <div className="field">
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 6 }}>
            <label>{isAr ? ar.sender.urlLabel : "URL (Optional)"}</label>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 4 }}>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "2px 8px", fontSize: 11 }} onClick={insertTodayDate}>
                📅 {isAr ? ar.todayBtn : "Today"}
              </button>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" style={{ padding: "2px 8px", fontSize: 11 }} onClick={insertYesterdayDate}>
                📅 {isAr ? ar.yesterdayBtn : "Yesterday"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <input type="text" value={url} onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="/recipes/3" style={{ width: "100%", padding: 8, marginBottom: 12 }} />
        </div>

        {url.trim() !== "" && !pollEnabled && (
          <div style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
            <label style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 8, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--muted)" }}>{isAr ? ar.sender.inboxPreview : "Inbox Preview"}</label>
            <div style={{ background: "var(--bg)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "var(--radius)", padding: 16 }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 8 }}>
                <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600 }}>{title}</h3>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)" }}>{isAr ? ar.sender.justNow : "Just now"}</span>
              </div>
              <p style={{ margin: "0 0 12px 0", fontSize: 13, color: "var(--ink-soft)" }}>{body}</p>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, padding: 10, background: "var(--surface)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8 }}>
                {previewRecipe?.imageUrl ? <img src={previewRecipe.imageUrl} alt="Preview" style={{ width: 60, height: 60, objectFit: "cover", borderRadius: 6 }} /> : <div style={{ width: 60, height: 60, borderRadius: 6, background: "var(--line)" }} />}
                <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 2 }}>
                  <strong style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--accent)" }}>{previewRecipe ? previewRecipe.name : (isAr ? ar.sender.viewLink : "View Link")}</strong>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)" }}>{url}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={send} disabled={busy} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 12 }}>
          {busy ? (isAr ? ar.sender.sending : "Sending...") : (isAr ? ar.sender.send : "Send")}
        </button>

        {result && <pre style={{ marginTop: 16, fontSize: 13, background: "var(--bg)", padding: 12, borderRadius: 8, overflow: "auto" }}>{result}</pre>}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/AdminSender.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/AutoTranslate.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { CUISINES, UNITS, PREPARATIONS, PickerItem } from "@/lib/pickers";

type Kind = "cuisine" | "unit" | "prep";

type Props = {
  kind: Kind;
  value: string;         // current value in the source field
  sourceIsAr?: boolean;  // if true, we're translating AR -> EN
  onTranslated: (result: string) => void;
  title?: string;
};

function findList(kind: Kind): PickerItem[] {
  if (kind === "cuisine") return CUISINES;
  if (kind === "unit") return UNITS;
  return PREPARATIONS;
}

export default function AutoTranslate({ kind, value, sourceIsAr, onTranslated, title }: Props) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  function translate() {
    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;

    setBusy(true);
    const list = findList(kind);

    // Match against EN value, AR value, or label (case-insensitive)
    const found = list.find((item) => {
      const targets = sourceIsAr
        ? [item.valueAr, item.value, item.label]
        : [item.value, item.label, item.valueAr];
      return targets.some((t) => t.toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase());
    });

    if (found) {
      const result = sourceIsAr ? found.value : found.valueAr;
      onTranslated(result);
      setDone(true);
      setTimeout(() => setDone(false), 1200);
    } else {
      // No exact match — try partial startsWith
      const partial = list.find((item) => {
        const target = sourceIsAr ? item.valueAr : item.value;
        return target.toLowerCase().startsWith(trimmed.toLowerCase());
      });
      if (partial) {
        const result = sourceIsAr ? partial.value : partial.valueAr;
        onTranslated(result);
        setDone(true);
        setTimeout(() => setDone(false), 1200);
      }
    }
    setBusy(false);
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className={`auto-translate-btn${done ? " done" : ""}`}
      onClick={translate}
      disabled={busy || !value.trim()}
      title={title || "Auto-translate"}
      aria-label="Auto-translate"
    >
      {done ? "✓" : "⇄"}
    </button>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/AutoTranslate.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/BackToTop.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function BackToTop() {
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const toggleVisibility = () => {
      if (window.scrollY > 200) {
        setIsVisible(true);
      } else {
        setIsVisible(false);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("scroll", toggleVisibility);
    // Run once on mount in case the page is already scrolled
    toggleVisibility();
    
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", toggleVisibility);
  }, []);

  return (
    <button
      className={`back-to-top ${isVisible ? "visible" : ""}`}
      onClick={() => window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" })}
      aria-label="Back to top"
      style={{
        position: "fixed",
        bottom: 24,
        right: 24,
        width: 48,
        height: 48,
        borderRadius: "50%",
        background: "var(--accent)",
        color: "white",
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",
        fontSize: 24,
        boxShadow: "0 4px 12px rgba(255, 107, 74, 0.4)",
        cursor: "pointer",
        zIndex: 9999,
        border: "none",
        opacity: isVisible ? 1 : 0,
        visibility: isVisible ? "visible" : "hidden",
        transition: "all 0.3s ease",
      }}
    >
      ↑
    </button>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/BackToTop.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/CookMode.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { pick } from "@/lib/i18n";
import KitchenTimer from "./KitchenTimer";

type Step = {
  id: number;
  stepNumber: number;
  text: string;
  textAr: string | null;
};

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  recipeName: string;
  steps: Step[];
};

type TimerState = {
  seconds: number;
  brief: string;
} | null;

export default function CookMode({ open, onClose, recipeName, steps }: Props) {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [wakeStatus, setWakeStatus] = useState<"idle" | "on" | "unsupported" | "denied">("idle");
  const wakeRef = useRef<any>(null);

  // Timer state
  const [activeTimer, setActiveTimer] = useState<TimerState>(null);

  // Inject Settings CSS directly
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    
    fetch("/api/settings")
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => {
        const size = data.cook_title_size || "28";
        const colorLight = data.cook_title_color_light || "#FFD700";
        const colorDark = data.cook_title_color_dark || "#FFD700";
        
        const isLight = document.documentElement.getAttribute("data-theme") === "light";
        const activeColor = isLight ? colorLight : colorDark;

        const styleId = "cook-mode-dynamic-styles";
        let styleEl = document.getElementById(styleId);
        if (!styleEl) {
          styleEl = document.createElement("style");
          styleEl.id = styleId;
          document.head.appendChild(styleEl);
        }

        const insSize = data.cook_instruction_size || "32";
        const insColorLight = data.cook_instruction_color_light || "#ededed";
        const insColorDark = data.cook_instruction_color_dark || "#ededed";
        const activeInsColor = isLight ? insColorLight : insColorDark;

        styleEl.innerHTML = `
          .dynamic-cook-title {
            color: ${activeColor} !important;
            font-size: ${size}px !important;
          }
          .dynamic-cook-instruction {
            color: ${activeInsColor} !important;
            font-size: clamp(16px, ${insSize}px, 48px) !important;
          }
        `;
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, [open]);

  // Hide Navbar and reset index when open
  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setIndex(0);
      document.body.classList.add("cook-mode-active");
    } else {
      document.body.classList.remove("cook-mode-active");
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.classList.remove("cook-mode-active");
    };
  }, [open]);

  // Wake Lock
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    async function acquire() {
      if (!("wakeLock" in navigator)) { setWakeStatus("unsupported"); return; }
      try {
        const sentinel = await (navigator as any).wakeLock.request("screen");
        if (cancelled) { sentinel.release().catch(() => {}); return; }
        wakeRef.current = sentinel;
        setWakeStatus("on");
        sentinel.addEventListener("release", () => {
          if (wakeRef.current === sentinel) {
            wakeRef.current = null;
            if (!cancelled) setWakeStatus("denied");
          }
        });
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) setWakeStatus("denied");
      }
    }
    acquire();
    function onVisible() {
      if (document.visibilityState === "visible" && open && !wakeRef.current) acquire();
    }
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisible);
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisible);
      if (wakeRef.current) { wakeRef.current.release().catch(() => {}); wakeRef.current = null; }
      setWakeStatus("idle");
    };
  }, [open]);

  // Keyboard navigation
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
      if (e.key === "ArrowRight" && index < steps.length - 1) setIndex((i) => i + 1);
      if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" && index > 0) setIndex((i) => i - 1);
    }
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open, index, steps.length, onClose]);

  if (!open || steps.length === 0) return null;

  const current = steps[index];
  const isFirst = index === 0;
  const isLast = index === steps.length - 1;
  const text = pick(lang, current.text, current.textAr);

  // Helper to extract a brief title from the step text (first 3-5 words)
  function getBrief(t: string) {
    const words = t.split(" ").slice(0, 4).join(" ");
    return words + "...";
  }

  function renderTextWithTimers(t: string) {
    const regex = /(\d+)\s*(minutes|minute|hours|hour|hrs|hr|mins|min|دقيقة|دقائق|ساعة|ساعات)\b/gi;
    const parts = [];
    let lastIndex = 0;
    let match;

    while ((match = regex.exec(t)) !== null) {
      if (match.index > lastIndex) parts.push(t.slice(lastIndex, match.index));

      const amount = parseInt(match[1], 10);
      const unit = match[2].toLowerCase();
      const isHour = unit.includes("hour") || unit.includes("hr") || unit.includes("ساعة");
      const seconds = isHour ? amount * 3600 : amount * 60;
      const brief = isAr ? `الخطوة ${index + 1}` : `Step ${index + 1}: ${getBrief(t)}`;

      parts.push(
        <button
          key={match.index}
          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setActiveTimer({ seconds, brief }); }}
          style={{ background: "rgba(255,107,74,0.2)", border: "1px solid #ff6b4a", color: "#ff6b4a", padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 6, margin: "0 4px", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: "0.8em", cursor: "pointer", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 4 }}
        >
          ⏱ {match[0]}
        </button>
      );
      lastIndex = regex.lastIndex;
    }

    if (lastIndex < t.length) parts.push(t.slice(lastIndex));
    return parts;
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="cook-overlay" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" style={{ display: "flex", position: "fixed", inset: 0, zIndex: 999, background: "#000", color: "#fff", flexDirection: "column", height: "100dvh", overflow: "hidden" }}>
        
        {/* Fixed Header */}
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", padding: "16px 20px", borderBottom: "1px solid #222", flexShrink: 0 }}>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} style={{ background: "#1a1a1a", border: "1px solid #333", color: "#fff", padding: "8px 14px", borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 14, cursor: "pointer" }}>
            ✕ {isAr ? "خروج" : "Exit"}
          </button>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#3fb950", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
            {wakeStatus === "on" && (isAr ? "📱 الشاشة تبقى مضاءة" : "📱 Screen stays on")}
            {(wakeStatus === "unsupported" || wakeStatus === "denied") && (isAr ? "📱 اجعل الشاشة مضاءة يدوياً" : "📱 Keep screen on manually")}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Fixed Title & Step Tracker */}
        <div style={{ flexShrink: 0, textAlign: "center", padding: "24px 20px 16px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
          <h1 className="dynamic-cook-title" style={{ 
            fontWeight: 700, 
            letterSpacing: "-0.02em", 
            margin: 0, 
            maxWidth: "90%", 
            whiteSpace: "nowrap", 
            overflow: "hidden", 
            textOverflow: "ellipsis" 
          }}>
            {recipeName}
          </h1>
          <div style={{ background: "rgba(255,107,74,0.15)", color: "#ff6b4a", border: "1px solid rgba(255,107,74,0.3)", padding: "6px 16px", borderRadius: 999, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "0.05em" }}>
            {isAr ? `الخطوة ${index + 1} من ${steps.length}` : `Step ${index + 1} of ${steps.length}`}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Scrollable Text */}
        <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", justifyContent: "center", alignItems: "center", padding: "20px 24px", overflowY: "auto" }}>
          <div className="dynamic-cook-instruction" style={{ lineHeight: 1.4, fontWeight: 500, maxWidth: 900, textAlign: "center", display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", justifyContent: "center", gap: "8px 4px" }}>
            {renderTextWithTimers(text)}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Fixed Bottom Nav */}
        <div style={{ flexShrink: 0, display: "flex", gap: 12, padding: 20, borderTop: "1px solid #222", paddingBottom: "calc(20px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0))" }}>
          <button type="button" disabled={isFirst} onClick={() => setIndex((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1))} style={{ flex: 1, padding: "20px 16px", fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid #333", background: "#1a1a1a", color: "#fff", cursor: isFirst ? "not-allowed" : "pointer", opacity: isFirst ? 0.3 : 1 }}>
            {isAr ? "→ السابق" : "← Prev"}
          </button>

          {isLast ? (
            <button type="button" onClick={onClose} style={{ flex: 1, padding: "20px 16px", fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid #3fb950", background: "#3fb950", color: "#fff", cursor: "pointer" }}>
              {isAr ? "✓ تم" : "✓ Done"}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setIndex((i) => Math.min(steps.length - 1, i + 1))} style={{ flex: 1, padding: "20px 16px", fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid #ff6b4a", background: "#ff6b4a", color: "#fff", cursor: "pointer" }}>
              {isAr ? "التالي ←" : "Next →"}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {activeTimer && (
        <KitchenTimer
          seconds={activeTimer.seconds}
          brief={activeTimer.brief}
          onClose={() => setActiveTimer(null)}
          onNextStep={!isLast ? () => { setActiveTimer(null); setIndex((i) => i + 1); } : undefined}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/CookMode.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/DayReviews.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { useModal } from "./ModalProvider";

type Review = {
  id: number;
  rating: number;
  comment: string | null;
  updatedAt: string;
  name: string | null;
};

type Data = {
  average: number;
  count: number;
  mine: { rating: number; comment: string | null } | null;
  reviews: Review[];
};

export default function DayReviews({ date, isAdmin }: { date: string; isAdmin?: boolean }) {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const { confirm } = useModal();

  const [data, setData] = useState<Data | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [rating, setRating] = useState(0);
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(0);
  const [comment, setComment] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [hasPhone, setHasPhone] = useState(false);
  const [phoneInput, setPhoneInput] = useState("");
  const [verifying, setVerifying] = useState(false);
  const [verifyError, setVerifyError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    setHasPhone(document.cookie.includes("viewer_phone="));
  }, []);

  function load() {
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/reviews/day/${date}`, { cache: "no-store" })
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((d) => {
        if (!d.error) {
          setData(d);
          if (d.mine) {
            setRating(d.mine.rating);
            setComment(d.mine.comment || "");
          } else {
            setRating(0);
            setComment("");
          }
        }
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [date]);

  async function verifyPhone() {
    const trimmed = phoneInput.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;
    setVerifying(true);
    setVerifyError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/auth/verify-phone", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ phoneNumber: trimmed }),
      });
      const d = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(d.error || "Failed to verify");
      setHasPhone(true);
      setPhoneInput("");
    } catch (err) {
      setVerifyError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Error");
    } finally {
      setVerifying(false);
    }
  }

  async function submit() {
    if (rating === 0) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/reviews/day/${date}`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ rating, comment }),
      });
      const d = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(d.error || "Failed");
      const updated = await fetch(`/api/reviews/day/${date}`, { cache: "no-store" }).then(r => r.json());
      setData(updated);
      setRating(0);
      setComment("");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Error");
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  async function deleteReview(id: number) {
    const ok = await confirm({ message: isAr ? ar.dayReviews.deleteConfirm : "Delete this review?", confirmText: isAr ? ar.modal.deleteBtn : "Delete", tone: "danger" });
    if (!ok) return;
    await fetch(`/api/reviews/day/${date}?reviewId=${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    load();
  }

  if (loading) return null;
  if (!data) return null;

  return (
    <section className="detail-section" style={{ marginTop: 48, paddingTop: 24, borderTop: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
      <h2 style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "baseline", gap: 12 }}>
        {isAr ? ar.dayReviews.title : "Day Review"}
        {data.count > 0 && (
          <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--muted)", fontWeight: 400, textTransform: "none", letterSpacing: 0 }}>
            ⭐ {data.average.toFixed(1)} ({data.count})
          </span>
        )}
      </h2>

      {hasPhone ? (
        <div style={{ background: "var(--surface)", padding: 20, borderRadius: "var(--radius)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", marginBottom: 24 }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 12 }}>
            {data.mine ? (isAr ? ar.dayReviews.edit : "Edit your review") : (isAr ? ar.dayReviews.question : "How were the meals today?")}
          </div>
          
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, fontSize: 28, cursor: "pointer", marginBottom: 16 }}>
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((star) => (
              <span
                key={star}
                onClick={() => setRating(star)}
                onMouseEnter={() => setHover(star)}
                onMouseLeave={() => setHover(0)}
                style={{
                  color: star <= (hover || rating) ? "#ffd700" : "var(--line-strong)",
                  transition: "transform 0.1s",
                  transform: star <= (hover || rating) ? "scale(1.1)" : "scale(1)",
                }}
              >
                ★
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>

          <textarea
            value={comment}
            onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={isAr ? ar.dayReviews.placeholder : "Add a note..."}
            style={{ width: "100%", minHeight: 80, padding: 12, borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid var(--line)", background: "var(--input-bg)", color: "var(--ink)", fontSize: 13, marginBottom: 12, resize: "vertical" }}
          />

          {error && <p style={{ color: "var(--accent)", fontSize: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>{error}</p>}

          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn btn-primary"
            onClick={submit}
            disabled={rating === 0 || submitting}
          >
            {submitting ? (isAr ? ar.dayReviews.saving : "Saving...") : data.mine ? (isAr ? ar.dayReviews.update : "Update Review") : (isAr ? ar.dayReviews.submit : "Submit Review")}
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ background: "var(--surface-2)", padding: 20, borderRadius: "var(--radius)", border: "1px solid var(--line)", marginBottom: 24 }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: "var(--ink)", marginBottom: 12 }}>
            🔒 {isAr ? ar.dayReviews.verify : "Verify your phone to leave a review."}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder={isAr ? ar.dayReviews.phonePlaceholder : "Your phone number"}
              value={phoneInput}
              onChange={(e) => setPhoneInput(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === "Enter") verifyPhone(); }}
              style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 180, padding: "8px 12px", border: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: 8, background: "var(--input-bg)", color: "var(--ink)", fontSize: 13 }}
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              className="btn btn-primary"
              onClick={verifyPhone}
              disabled={verifying || !phoneInput.trim()}
            >
              {verifying ? "…" : isAr ? ar.dayReviews.verifyBtn : "Verify"}
            </button>
          </div>
          {verifyError && (
            <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--accent)", marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 0 }}>{verifyError}</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {data.reviews.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 16 }}>
          {data.reviews.map((r, i) => (
            <div key={`${r.id}-${i}`} style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12 }}>
              <div style={{ width: 32, height: 32, borderRadius: "50%", background: "var(--surface-2)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--muted)", flexShrink: 0 }}>
                {(r.name || "?")[0].toUpperCase()}
              </div>
              <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, marginBottom: 2 }}>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600 }}>{r.name || (isAr ? ar.dayReviews.familyMember : "Family Member")}</span>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--muted)" }}>{new Date(r.updatedAt).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ color: "#ffd700", fontSize: 12, letterSpacing: 2, marginBottom: 4 }}>
                  {"★".repeat(r.rating)}{"☆".repeat(5 - r.rating)}
                </div>
                {r.comment && <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "var(--ink-soft)", lineHeight: 1.5, margin: 0 }}>{r.comment}</p>}
                
                {isAdmin && (
                  <button onClick={() => deleteReview(r.id)} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: "var(--accent)", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 12, marginTop: 6, padding: 0 }}>
                    🗑 {isAr ? ar.dayReviews.deleteBtn : "Delete"}
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/DayReviews.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/DynamicFavicon.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";

export default function DynamicFavicon() {
  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/settings")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        const url = data.favicon_url || data.logo_url;
        if (!url) return;

        // Remove Next.js default + any previous dynamic icons
        document.querySelectorAll("link[rel*='icon']").forEach((el) => {
          const href = el.getAttribute("href") || "";
          if (href.includes("/favicon.ico") || el.getAttribute("data-dynamic") === "true") {
            el.remove();
          }
        });

        const icon = document.createElement("link");
        icon.rel = "icon";
        icon.type = "image/png";
        icon.href = url;
        icon.setAttribute("data-dynamic", "true");
        document.head.appendChild(icon);

        const appleIcon = document.createElement("link");
        appleIcon.rel = "apple-touch-icon";
        appleIcon.href = url;
        appleIcon.setAttribute("data-dynamic", "true");
        document.head.appendChild(appleIcon);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  return null;
}
--- END FILE: src/components/DynamicFavicon.tsx ---

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";
import { ReactNode } from "react";

type Props = {
  title: string;
  titleAr: string;
  onNewGame: () => void;
  onShowRules: () => void;
  score?: number | string;
  scoreLabel?: string;
  scoreLabelAr?: string;
  extra?: number | string;
  extraLabel?: string;
  extraLabelAr?: string;
  best?: number | string;
  bestLabel?: string;
  bestLabelAr?: string;
};

export default function GameHeader({ 
  title, titleAr, onNewGame, onShowRules, 
  score, scoreLabel = "Score", scoreLabelAr = "النقاط",
  extra, extraLabel = "Lives", extraLabelAr = "أرواح",
  best, bestLabel = "Best", bestLabelAr = "الأفضل"
}: Props) {
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", padding: "16px", background: "var(--surface)", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--line)", borderRadius: "0 0 16px 16px", marginBottom: "16px", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: "12px" }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6 }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, alignItems: "center" }}>
          <Link href="/games" style={{ fontSize: "0.7rem", color: "var(--muted)", textDecoration: "none", fontWeight: 600 }}>
            ← {isAr ? "الألعاب" : "Games"}
          </Link>
          <Link href="/games/leaderboard" style={{ fontSize: "0.7rem", color: "var(--muted)", textDecoration: "none", fontWeight: 600 }}>
            🏆 {isAr ? "المتصدرين" : "Leaderboard"}
          </Link>
        </div>
        <h1 style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-head)", fontSize: "1.5rem", margin: 0, lineHeight: 1 }}>
          {isAr ? titleAr : title}
        </h1>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={onNewGame} style={{ fontSize: "0.7rem", padding: "4px 8px" }}>
            {isAr ? "↻ لعبة جديدة" : "↻ New Game"}
          </button>
          <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={onShowRules} style={{ fontSize: "0.7rem", padding: "4px 8px" }}>
            {isAr ? "? القواعد" : "? Rules"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {(score !== undefined || best !== undefined || extra !== undefined) && (
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 16, background: "var(--bg)", padding: "8px 16px", borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid var(--line)" }}>
          
          {score !== undefined && (
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center" }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: "0.6rem", textTransform: "uppercase", color: "var(--muted)", fontWeight: 700 }}>{isAr ? scoreLabelAr : scoreLabel}</span>
              <span style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--ink)" }}>{score}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {extra !== undefined && (
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center" }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: "0.6rem", textTransform: "uppercase", color: "var(--muted)", fontWeight: 700 }}>{isAr ? extraLabelAr : extraLabel}</span>
              <span style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--accent)" }}>{extra}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {best !== undefined && (
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center" }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: "0.6rem", textTransform: "uppercase", color: "var(--muted)", fontWeight: 700 }}>{isAr ? bestLabelAr : bestLabel}</span>
              <span style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", fontWeight: 700, color: "var(--olive)" }}>{best}</span>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
--- END FILE: src/components/GameHeader.tsx ---

--- FILE: src/components/HomeClient.tsx ---
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useMemo, useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useLang } from "./LanguageProvider";
import { t, pick } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { cuisineFlagUrl } from "@/lib/cuisine-flag";
import { CATEGORIES } from "@/lib/categories";
import { ar } from "@/lib/ar";
import { useModal } from "./ModalProvider";

import { getRecipeImage } from "@/lib/get-recipe-image";
type RecipeRow = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameAr: string | null;
  cuisine: string | null;
  cuisineAr: string | null;
  category: string | null;
  categoryAr: string | null;
  prepTime: number;
  cookTime: number;
  servings: number;
  imageUrl: string | null;
  videoUrl: string | null;
  dietaryTags: string | null;
  isHidden?: number | null;
};

const TAG_AR: Record<string, string> = {
  Vegetarian: "نباتي", Vegan: "نباتي صرف", Pescatarian: "بيسكاتاري",
  "Gluten-Free": "خالٍ من الغلوتين", "Dairy-Free": "خالٍ من الألبان",
  "Nut-Free": "خالٍ من المكسرات", Halal: "حلال", Kosher: "كوشير",
  "Low-Carb": "قليل الكربوهيدرات", Keto: "كيتو", Paleo: "باليو",
  Meat: "لحوم", Chicken: "دجاج", Seafood: "مأكولات بحرية",
  Fish: "سمك", Lamb: "لحم غنم", Beef: "لحم بقري",
  Legumes: "بقوليات", Spicy: "حار", Quick: "سريع",
};

type SortKey = "newest" | "name-asc" | "name-desc" | "time-asc" | "time-desc";

export default function HomeClient({ recipes }: { recipes: RecipeRow[] }) {
  const [logoUrl, setLogoUrl] = useState<string>("");
  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/settings").then((r) => r.json()).then((d) => { if (d.logo_url) setLogoUrl(d.logo_url); }).catch(() => {});
  }, []);
  
  const { lang } = useLang();
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const router = useRouter();
  const { show } = useModal();

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [cuisineFilter, setCuisineFilter] = useState<string>("");
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<string>("");
  const [tagFilter, setTagFilter] = useState<string>("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortKey>("newest");
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"grid" | "list">("grid");
  const [dailyDishOpen, setDailyDishOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isRolling, setIsRolling] = useState(false);
  const [dailyDish, setDailyDish] = useState<RecipeRow | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem("recipes_view");
    if (stored === "grid" || stored === "list") setViewMode(stored);
  }, []);

  const allCuisines = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    for (const r of recipes) {
      if (r.cuisine) set.add(r.cuisine);
      if (r.cuisineAr && !r.cuisine) set.add(r.cuisineAr);
    }
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [recipes]);

  const allTags = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    for (const r of recipes) {
      if (!r.dietaryTags) continue;
      try {
        const tags = JSON.parse(r.dietaryTags);
        if (Array.isArray(tags)) tags.forEach((tag) => set.add(tag));
      } catch {}
    }
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [recipes]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    let list = recipes.filter((r) => {
      const name = (pick(lang, r.name, r.nameAr) || "").toLowerCase();
      const cuisine = (pick(lang, r.cuisine, r.cuisineAr) || "").toLowerCase();
      if (q && !name.includes(q) && !cuisine.includes(q)) return false;
      if (cuisineFilter && (r.cuisine || r.cuisineAr) !== cuisineFilter) return false;
      if (categoryFilter && (r.category || r.categoryAr) !== categoryFilter) return false;
      if (tagFilter) {
        if (!r.dietaryTags) return false;
        try {
          const tags = JSON.parse(r.dietaryTags);
          if (!Array.isArray(tags) || !tags.includes(tagFilter)) return false;
        } catch { return false; }
      }
      return true;
    });

    list = [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      if (sort === "name-asc") return (pick(lang, a.name, a.nameAr) || "").localeCompare(pick(lang, b.name, b.nameAr) || "");
      if (sort === "name-desc") return (pick(lang, b.name, b.nameAr) || "").localeCompare(pick(lang, a.name, a.nameAr) || "");
      if (sort === "time-asc") return a.prepTime + a.cookTime - (b.prepTime + b.cookTime);
      if (sort === "time-desc") return b.prepTime + b.cookTime - (a.prepTime + a.cookTime);
      return 0;
    });
    return list;
  }, [recipes, query, cuisineFilter, categoryFilter, tagFilter, sort, lang]);

  const grouped = useMemo(() => {
    const groups: { key: string; icon: string; label: string; items: RecipeRow[] }[] = [];
    for (const c of CATEGORIES) {
      const items = filtered.filter((r) =>
        (r.category || "") === c.value || (r.categoryAr || "") === c.valueAr ||
        (r.category || "") === c.valueAr || (r.categoryAr || "") === c.value
      );
      if (items.length > 0) groups.push({ key: c.value, icon: c.icon, label: lang === "ar" ? c.valueAr : c.value, items });
    }
    const uncategorized = filtered.filter((r) => {
      const cat = (r.category || "").trim();
      const catAr = (r.categoryAr || "").trim();
      if (!cat && !catAr) return true;
      return !CATEGORIES.some((c) => c.value === cat || c.valueAr === cat || c.value === catAr || c.valueAr === catAr);
    });
    if (uncategorized.length > 0) groups.push({ key: "__other__", icon: "🍴", label: lang === "ar" ? "أخرى" : "Other", items: uncategorized });
    return groups;
  }, [filtered, lang]);

  const hasFilters = query || cuisineFilter || categoryFilter || tagFilter;
  const total = recipes.length;

  function openRandomMainDish() {
    setDailyDishOpen(true);
    setIsRolling(true);
    setDailyDish(null);
    setTimeout(() => {
      const mainDishes = filtered;
      if (mainDishes.length === 0) { show({ title: isAr ? ar.home.emptyTitle : "No main dishes found!", message: "", tone: "danger" }); setDailyDishOpen(false); return; }
      setDailyDish(mainDishes[Math.floor(Math.random() * mainDishes.length)]);
      setIsRolling(false);
    }, 1200);
  }

  function confirmDailyDish() {
    if (!dailyDish) return;
    router.push(`/recipes/${dailyDish.id}?today`);
  }

  function clearFilters() {
    setQuery("");
    setCuisineFilter("");
    setCategoryFilter("");
    setTagFilter("");
    setSort("newest");
  }

  return (
    <main className="container">
      <div className="page-head">
        <h1>{t(lang, "home_title")}</h1>
        <button type="button" onClick={openRandomMainDish} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "6px", marginBottom: "6px" }}>
          🎲 {isAr ? "فاجئني" : "Surprise Me"}
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="page-sub">
        {total === 0 ? "" : hasFilters
          ? (isAr ? `${filtered.length} من ${total} وصفة` : `${filtered.length} of ${total} recipes`)
          : total === 1 ? t(lang, "recipe_count_one") : t(lang, "recipe_count").replace("{n}", String(total))}
      </p>

      {total > 0 && (
        <div className="filter-bar" style={{ display: "block" }}>
          
          <div className="filter-top-row">
            <div className="search-wrap">
              <span className="search-icon">🔍</span>
              <input type="text" className="search-input" placeholder={isAr ? ar.home.searchPlaceholder : "Search recipes..."} value={query} onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)} />
              {query && <button type="button" className="search-clear" onClick={() => setQuery("")} aria-label="Clear search">✕</button>}
            </div>
            
            <button type="button" className="filter-toggle-small" onClick={() => document.querySelector(".filter-dropdowns")?.classList.toggle("open")}>☰</button>
            
            <div className="view-toggle">
              <button type="button" className={`view-toggle-btn${viewMode === "grid" ? " active" : ""}`} onClick={() => { setViewMode("grid"); localStorage.setItem("recipes_view", "grid"); }}>▦</button>
              <button type="button" className={`view-toggle-btn${viewMode === "list" ? " active" : ""}`} onClick={() => { setViewMode("list"); localStorage.setItem("recipes_view", "list"); }}>☰</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="filter-dropdowns">
            <select className="filter-select" value={categoryFilter} onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">{isAr ? ar.home.allCategories : "All categories"}</option>
              {CATEGORIES.map((c) => <option key={c.value} value={c.value}>{c.icon} {isAr ? c.valueAr : c.value}</option>)}
            </select>
            
            <select className="filter-select" value={cuisineFilter} onChange={(e) => setCuisineFilter(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">{isAr ? ar.home.allCuisines : "All cuisines"}</option>
              {allCuisines.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </select>
            
            <select className="filter-select" value={tagFilter} onChange={(e) => setTagFilter(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">{isAr ? ar.home.allTags : "All tags"}</option>
              {allTags.map((tag) => <option key={tag} value={tag}>{isAr ? TAG_AR[tag] ?? tag : tag}</option>)}
            </select>
            
            <select className="filter-select" value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as SortKey)}>
              <option value="newest">{isAr ? ar.home.newest : "Newest"}</option>
              <option value="name-asc">{isAr ? ar.home.nameAsc : "Name A-Z"}</option>
              <option value="name-desc">{isAr ? ar.home.nameDesc : "Name Z-A"}</option>
              <option value="time-asc">{isAr ? ar.home.timeAsc : "Fastest first"}</option>
              <option value="time-desc">{isAr ? ar.home.timeDesc : "Longest first"}</option>
            </select>

            {hasFilters && <button type="button" className="filter-clear" onClick={clearFilters}>{isAr ? ar.home.clear : "Clear"}</button>}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {total === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{t(lang, "home_empty_title")}</h2>
          <p>{t(lang, "home_empty_text")}</p>
          <Link href="/recipes/new" className="btn btn-primary">{t(lang, "home_empty_cta")}</Link>
        </div>
      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <h2>{isAr ? ar.home.noResults : "No results"}</h2>
          <p>{isAr ? ar.home.noResultsHint : "Try different keywords or clear your filters."}</p>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={clearFilters}>{isAr ? "مسح الفلاتر" : "Clear filters"}</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {viewMode === "grid" && (
            <div className="category-sections">
              {grouped.map((group) => (
                <section key={group.key} className="category-section">
                  <div className="category-section-head">
                    <h2 className="category-section-title">
                      <span className="category-section-icon">{group.icon}</span>
                      <span>{group.label}</span>
                    </h2>
                    <span className="category-section-count">{group.items.length}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="category-section-line" />
                  <div className="recipe-grid">
                    {group.items.map((r) => {
                      let tags: string[] = [];
                      try {
                        if (r.dietaryTags) {
                          const parsed = JSON.parse(r.dietaryTags);
                          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) tags = parsed;
                        }
                      } catch (e) { tags = []; }
                      const displayName = pick(lang, r.name, r.nameAr);
                      const displayCuisine = pick(lang, r.cuisine, r.cuisineAr);
                      const flagUrl = cuisineFlagUrl(r.cuisine) || cuisineFlagUrl(r.cuisineAr);
                      const hasVideo = !!(r.videoUrl && r.videoUrl.trim());
                      return (
                        <Link key={r.id} href={`/recipes/${r.id}`} className="recipe-card">
                          <div className="recipe-card-img-wrap">
                        {r.isHidden === 1 && (
                          <span style={{ position: "absolute", top: 8, left: 8, background: "var(--accent)", color: "white", padding: "2px 8px", borderRadius: 4, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, zIndex: 10 }}>
                            {isAr ? ar.home.hidden : "Hidden"}
                          </span>
                        )}
                            {getRecipeImage(r) ? (
                              <div className="img-with-watermark">
                                <img src={getRecipeImage(r)!} alt={displayName} className="recipe-card-img" loading="lazy" decoding="async" />
                                {logoUrl && <img src={logoUrl} alt="" className="watermark" />}
                              </div>
                            ) : <div className="recipe-card-img" />}
                            {hasVideo && <span className="recipe-card-video-emoji" title={isAr ? "يحتوي على فيديو" : "Has video"}>🎬</span>}
                          </div>
                          <div className="recipe-card-body">
                            <div className="recipe-card-head">
                              <div className="recipe-card-text">
                                <h3>{displayName}</h3>
                                {displayCuisine && <span className="recipe-card-cuisine">{displayCuisine}</span>}
                              </div>
                              {flagUrl && <img src={flagUrl} alt={displayCuisine} className="recipe-card-flag" loading="lazy" />}
                            </div>
                            <div className="recipe-card-meta">
                              <span>⏱ {r.prepTime + r.cookTime} {isAr ? "د" : "min"}</span>
                              <span>🍽 {r.servings}</span>
                            </div>
                            {tags.length > 0 && (
                              <div className="recipe-card-tags">
                                {tags.slice(0, 3).map((tag) => <span key={tag} className="chip">{isAr ? TAG_AR[tag] ?? tag : tag}</span>)}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </Link>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </section>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {viewMode === "list" && (
            <div className="recipe-list">
              {filtered.map((r) => {
                let tags: string[] = [];
                      try {
                        if (r.dietaryTags) {
                          const parsed = JSON.parse(r.dietaryTags);
                          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) tags = parsed;
                        }
                      } catch (e) { tags = []; }
                const displayName = pick(lang, r.name, r.nameAr);
                const displayCuisine = pick(lang, r.cuisine, r.cuisineAr);
                const hasVideo = !!(r.videoUrl && r.videoUrl.trim());
                return (
                  <Link key={r.id} href={`/recipes/${r.id}`} className="recipe-list-item">
                    <div className="recipe-list-img">
                      {r.isHidden === 1 && (
                        <span style={{ position: "absolute", top: 4, left: 4, background: "var(--accent)", color: "white", padding: "2px 6px", borderRadius: 4, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: 700, zIndex: 10 }}>
                          {isAr ? ar.home.hidden : "Hidden"}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {getRecipeImage(r) ? <img src={getRecipeImage(r)!} alt={displayName} loading="lazy" decoding="async" /> : <div className="recipe-list-img-placeholder" />}
                      {hasVideo && <span className="recipe-list-video">🎬</span>}
                      {r.isHidden === 1 && (
                        <span style={{ position: "absolute", top: 4, left: 4, background: "var(--accent)", color: "white", padding: "2px 6px", borderRadius: 4, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: 700 }}>
                          {isAr ? "مخفي" : "Hidden"}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="recipe-list-body">
                      <h3>{displayName}</h3>
                      <div className="recipe-list-meta">
                        {displayCuisine && <span>{displayCuisine}</span>}
                        <span>⏱ {r.prepTime + r.cookTime} {isAr ? "د" : "min"}</span>
                        <span>🍽 {r.servings}</span>
                      </div>
                      {tags.length > 0 && (
                        <div className="recipe-list-tags">
                          {tags.slice(0, 3).map((tag) => <span key={tag} className="chip">{isAr ? TAG_AR[tag] ?? tag : tag}</span>)}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {/* DISH OF THE DAY MODAL */}
      {dailyDishOpen && (
        <div onClick={() => { if (!isRolling) setDailyDishOpen(false); }} style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, zIndex: 999, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)", backdropFilter: "blur(6px)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 20 }}>
          <div onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} style={{ background: "var(--surface-solid)", borderRadius: 16, overflow: "hidden", maxWidth: 420, width: "100%", border: "1px solid var(--line-strong)", boxShadow: "var(--shadow-lg)", minHeight: 300, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", justifyContent: "center" }}>
            {isRolling ? (
              <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: "center", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", gap: 16 }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 64, animation: "spin 1s linear infinite" }}>🎲</div>
                <h2 style={{ fontSize: "1.25rem", margin: 0 }}>{isAr ? "جاري اختيار الطبق..." : "Rolling the dice..."}</h2>
                <style>{`@keyframes spin { 100% { transform: rotate(360deg); } }`}</style>
              </div>
            ) : dailyDish ? (
              <>
                {dailyDish.imageUrl ? (
                  <img src={dailyDish.imageUrl} alt={pick(lang, dailyDish.name, dailyDish.nameAr)} style={{ width: "100%", height: 220, objectFit: "cover" }} />
                ) : <div style={{ width: "100%", height: 220, background: "var(--line)" }} />}
                <div style={{ padding: 24, textAlign: "center" }}>
                  <h2 style={{ fontSize: "1.5rem", margin: "0 0 8px", fontFamily: "var(--font-head)" }}>{pick(lang, dailyDish.name, dailyDish.nameAr)}</h2>
                  <p style={{ color: "var(--muted)", margin: "0 0 24px", fontSize: "0.875rem" }}>{isAr ? "هل توافق على هذا الطبق لليوم؟" : "Do you agree to make this the dish of the day?"}</p>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
                    <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={openRandomMainDish} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 12 }}>🎲 {isAr ? "رمي النرد" : "Re-roll"}</button>
                    <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => setDailyDishOpen(false)} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 12 }}>{isAr ? "إلغاء" : "Cancel"}</button>
                    <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={confirmDailyDish} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 12 }}>{isAr ? "موافق" : "Agree"}</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
